Later the same day (sixteenth builder, second part):

- **Textbook drawing is the default** (the owner's pick; `?knot=a` is the old way). Added: a layout that follows another is turned as a whole to lie as the atoms it shares with the one before lay (`seedO`, `buildMol.orient`); a first layout with a stored cage at its root stands the cage upright (`KN.rootAng`); a single thing on a ring-fusion atom goes into the gap that points away from the ring system (`gapAt` with `probe`); the cube's bonds are 0.91 to 1.12; depth lifts an atom 0.2. The clean-up leaves two atoms of the SAME drawing alone (`D.kn` is the drawing's number) and still parts everything else. **If a set-down drawing cannot be made clean (`fitNote` `fail`) that molecule is built again the old way** (`buildMol.noKnot`, `m.knotOff`).
- **Whole-game job, 5,238 layouts** (`j_lay.js` with `strict=1`: a layout fails on a pair under touching, a bond over 1.15 or under 0.9). Before (old way, old limits): 0 clashes, 1,010 layouts with a bond over 1.15, 771 with one under 0.9 (worst 1.27 and 0.66). Old way with the rest limits: 0 clashes, 59 and 43. Textbook default with the rest limits and the fall-back: 4 layouts with a pair just under touching (worst 0.987), 168 and 202 (worst 1.38 and 0.66; part of these are a stored drawing's own foreshortened bonds, which the measure counts). Without the fall-back the textbook way left 37 clashes (worst 0.56): do not take the fall-back out.
- **The manual pays in numbers** (owner: no bar charts): `manNum` writes the multiplier found in the rules' own sentence (`yield times N`) in the pen's hand; `manYield` is no longer called. The motifs tab's second picture broke in this change (not fixed). The opened view's words are bigger.

Not started: the six properties shown on the molecule (`?kin`), creature stages (`P.homStage`), the leftovers list, `P.profileOpts`, `P.stageOf` / `P.gateOpen`, sigil buying.

## Seventeenth builder (13 November)

Branch `page-builder-17` (worktree `page17`). Files: `scheme.js`, `marks.js`, `kitart.js`. Recorder: `.claude/f17` of the worktree (not in git; a copy of the scripts of `f16` only, not its `prof` and `frames`; ports 9171 page, 9172 frames; start `srv.py` and `fsrv.py` by hand and write their PIDs down). New jobs: `j_kin.js` with `kst.sh` (one still) and `kclip.sh` (a recorded round), `zsheet.py` (cropped frames side by side), `j_hom.js` (`part=sheet`: every form front and back by a renderer of its own; `part=bench`: the play screen with the run's creature at a stage; `part=evo`: a recorded page on which the creature's job fires and lifts its stage).

- **The molecule's properties, three ways** (`KINV`, `kinFeat`, `kinSync`, `kinFrame`, `kinBeat`; just above `drawInk`; `?kin=a|b|c` or `AA.rxScheme.kinSet`; nothing is drawn without it and nothing is picked). The six are `P.kinsOf(center.model)`. Drawn on a canvas of its own over the picture (a `.veil`, width 0 when off), from the balls' screen places, so it follows every frame of a step. `a`: a bead of sheen back and forth along the alternating bonds outside benzene rings (faster with the level), a dashed inner circle turning in each benzene ring, a pencil dash once round every other ring each bar, a glint on each O-H hydrogen in turn, a cage's back bonds paled and unpaled over two bars (the bonds drawn with `backMat`; when the cage was drawn the old way, the far ring bonds); the sheen and the paling are cut away over the atoms. `b`: `AA.rxMarks.kin(ctx, id, ...)` (six marks in ink, dots for the level) in a row under the molecule; a mark inks in from the left when gained, is struck out in red and fades when lost, is stamped (bigger, heavier) when it pays. `c`: the marks in pencil, inked for a second when gained or paying, and the on-molecule drawing only for about three seconds after a gain and two after a pay.
- **What "pays" means here**: a step record whose `ev` has `src: 'motif'` (its `motifs` ids, mapped by `KINMAP`: fusion and spiro count as ring) or `src: 'family'`. The flare is put on the next beat at least 240 ms on. The beat is `AA.music.clock()` when it has one and a steady half second when it has none; in a recording it has none (the recorder renders the music afterwards), so the clips are on the half second.
- **The rules pay by the twelve motifs, not by `KIN`** (`RULE.motifs` is on): the level a motif pays at (`motifsOf`) is not the level `kinsOf` gives (a diene is resonance 1 by `kinsOf` and 2 by the motifs), and six motifs (branch, acid, amine, sulfur, fusion, spiro) have no property of their own on the page. The dots under a mark are the `kinsOf` level.
- **Five forms of a creature** (`kitart.js`: `YOUNG`, `GROWN`, `B.__stage`, `extra.stage`, `AA.rxKit.creature(T, key, tint, stage)`, `AA.rxKit.stageSize`, `AA.rxKit.STAGED`). Stage 3 is the creature as it was. 1 and 2 are small plain builds of their own with a face and a backside; 4 and 5 add parts to 3. Drawn for water, acid and slime only; the other seven are the same at every stage.
- **On the bench** (`homStageNow`, `homStand`, `HEVO`, `homEvoFrame`): with `P.RULE.homGrow` set the run's creature stands in the form of `P.homStage(run, id).stage`, sized by `stageSize`. When `g.homEvolved` appears it swells, and on the next beat (at least 700 ms on) the next form lands in a puff, turning once round. `AA.rxScheme.homRestand()` builds it again (a job sets `run.homJobs` and calls it).
- **Overlap** (`buildMol`, `declash`): a set-down textbook drawing is now also given up when the drawn picture shows a pair under 1.03 of touching (the cube's own atoms were at 0.987); a picture at rest still under touching after the usual rounds gets 400 more at a wider margin.

Not done: the manual (nothing of item 3), `P.profileOpts`, `P.stageOf` / `P.gateOpen`, the jar past full, `runResult().earned`; the other seven creatures' forms; the creature in its jar on the shelf and in the manual is not staged.

Later the same day (seventeenth builder, after the owner's rulings):

- **`c` is the default** for the properties (`?kin=a`, `?kin=b` the other two, `?kin=0` none). Not yet done in `c`: a strip for the marks that the molecule's fit box leaves free, a line from a mark to where its property lives, a bigger on-molecule effect, the loss shown on the molecule, the amount paid written beside the stamp.

The other seven are not drawn.
- **The evolve moment** waits until the creature is home from its job (`HEVO.pend`), lands at nearly twice size for a second and a half, and the new multiplier is written beside it in the pen's hand on the properties' canvas (so it needs `kin` not `0`).
- **Whole-game layout job is NOT at 0**: 7 of 5,277 layouts under touching with these changes (6 before them on the same rules; worst 0.971). Four are the cube's own atoms (0.987 to 0.995: the build-time check does not see them, the measure there differs from the placed one); two or three are layouts where both the eased and the fresh build failed (`fresh:fail`).

## Eighteenth builder (13 November)

Branch `page-builder-18` (worktree `page18`). Files: `scheme.js`, `marks.js`. Recorder: `.claude/f18` of the worktree (not in git; the scripts of `f17` only; ports 9181 page, 9182 frames; start `srv.py` and `fsrv.py` by hand and write their PIDs down). New jobs: `j_msheet.js` (the twelve motif marks on one sheet, posted as `sheet.jpg`); `j_kin.js` now reports the motifs, the strip and the row of marks.

- **The properties are the twelve motifs** (`MIDS`; `kinSync`, `kinFrame`). Levels are `P.motifsOf(center.model)`, open ones `P.motifOpen(g)`, what a step paid is its record's `ev` of `src: 'motif'` (`motifs: [{ id, level, rate }]`, 1 + rate x level each). `P.kinsOf` and `KINMAP` are no longer read. Nothing of the rules is worked out on the page except WHERE a motif sits on the drawing (`kinFeat` gives `where[id]`: groups of atoms, found by the page's own simple tests, for pointing only). The right fix is an export from the rules, `P.motifAtoms(model)` giving `{ id: [[atom, ...], ...] }` from the same code as `motifCount`.
- **Twelve marks** (`marks.js`: `MOTMARK`, `AA.rxMarks.motifMark(ctx, id, cx, cy, size, o)`, `AA.rxMarks.motifMarkIds`): the six of builder 17 kept (sugar, resonance, arene, ring, cage; chain redrawn as three links) and fusion, spiro, acid, amine, sulfur, branch added. `o.shut` draws a mark as a faint dashed outline: held but not open, pays nothing.
- **A strip of their own** (`KSTRIP`, world z 5.5 to 10.3 on the right page): the molecule's home now ends above it (`SHP.home.z1` 5.2, was 9.9) and the yield track lies below. `?kin=0` and `?kin=a` have no strip and the old home. Marks ease sideways when one joins or leaves the row.
- **Tied to the molecule** (way `c`): on a gain or a level up, on the beat, the mark inks in, a line runs from the mark to where the motif sits and goes once round it by hand (`reach`, `lasso`, `ellOf`); when it pays the same, short and heavy, each open motif a quarter beat after the one before (left to right), and what it paid is written under the mark in the pen's hand (x1.07). A loss: the red strike on the mark, a red line to where it sat (`lostAt`, kept in the molecule's own measure) and a red cross there, over the atoms, for a second and a half. The marks a round starts with are simply there (no gain shown). Ink lies behind the atoms (cut away over every ball); no atom moves, no colour but the red of a loss.
- **Pushed creature forms** (`kitart.js`: `PUSH`, `AA.rxKit.pushOn`, `AA.rxKit.PUSHED`, `creature(T, key, tint, stage, push)`): water, acid, slime, salt and dry ice, five whole forms each (hatchling, child, worker, master, small god), each with its own posture, prop and backside. Not shown in the game: only with `extra.push` or `AA.rxKit.pushOn = true`. The sheet is `j_hom.js` `part=push` (`ids=`, `both=0` for the pushed row alone), lit with the game's own lamp values. The old `YOUNG` and `GROWN` forms are untouched.
- **Overlap** (`placedOv`, `buildMol.last`): the stored cube is redrawn (its back square moved to 0.60, 0.68 so every pair of its own atoms is 0.72 apart or more); the build-time check of a set-down drawing now measures it as it will stand in the home; a picture still under touching at the end of `buildMol` is built once more fresh, the old way, and kept if better (`fitNote` `last:`); `declash` at rest has one harder pass more.
- **The manual** (`MANJOB`, `manDoes`, `manDetail`, `manSpread`): a creature's pictures are picked by its id; every row's numbers are its `nums` (a loss red); no number is read out of a sentence (`manNum` from `/yield times/` and the motif's `counted N in 100` are gone); the motifs tab lists all twelve with `motifMark` and 1 + `P.MOTIFS[id].rate` (the eight open from the start have no row in `P.reference`, so the page adds bare picture rows that do not open); an owned creature's opened view writes `row.stages` with `row.stage` marked, and its figure is built at `row.stage`. NOT done: instruments and sigils still pick pictures by the words of `does` and most now match nothing; they need a by-id table like `MANJOB`.

## Nineteenth builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-19` (worktree `page19`). Files: `kitart.js`, `scheme.js`. Recorder: `.claude/f19` of the worktree (not in git; the scripts of `f18` only; ports 9191 page, 9192 frames; start `srv.py` and `fsrv.py` by hand and write their PIDs down). New jobs: `j_h19.js` (`part=bench`: a still with the run's creature at `stage=`; `part=act`: a recorded page, `show=1` plays the act by `AA.rxScheme.jumpShow(id)` after the line, `rec=N` starts the take at line N; `part=evo`: the jar is raised one stage while the page is at rest); `two19.py` (the ten-creature sheet cut into fronts and backs); `cut19.py` (a span of a take with its sound).

- **All ten creatures pushed, and on** (`kitart.js`: `PUSH.homh2`, `homwood`, `homseed`, `homammonia`, `hombrimstone` added after the first five; `AA.rxKit.pushOn` is now `true`, `PUSHED` lists ten). Fixes to the first five are wrappers after them: water 5 is a wave, dry ice 5 a glacier, salt 4 has bright crystal edges, slime 3 a real flask; salt and dry ice colours are darker and exact so the lamp does not burn them out. The old `YOUNG`, `GROWN` and `B` forms are untouched.
- **Drawn at its stage** (`scheme.js`): `homFig(kind)` with no stage reads `homStageNow`, which reads `P.homStage` from the profile even with no run; both places a run is made set `run.meta = meta`; a hatched creature's jar is no longer capped at full when a round banks. On the bench a stage 1 or 2 form is 1.15 of its size and stage 5 is 0.92 so it clears the screen top. NOT done: the creature inside its jar (`mkJar` in `kitart.js`) and the small shelf jars are not checked at stage; the number shown is still not everywhere `P.homStage(run, id).mult`.
- **The act** (`JUMPS`, `jumpPlan`, `jumpPieces`, `JACT`, `homJump`; `doHom` shows the yield when the act lands (`jj.hit`) and lets the page go on when it is over (`jj.rest`)): wind-up, a jump from the bench to a perch on the page, the creature's own act there, the jump home, a landing. The landing of the act is put on the beat by lengthening the wind-up. Drawn: acid (bites toward the chosen place, 2 to 4 bites by stage), water (wrings and pours, a flood sheet under the molecule down to the strip), slime (lands on the stuck card, flattens, strings to the molecule). The other seven still use `actStart` and the old slide: add each to `JUMPS` and `JACT`.
- **The evolve moment** (`homEvo19`, called in place of `homEvoFrame`, which is kept): a leap to the middle of the book (`EV`), the new form on the next beat at up to 2.9 times, the number under it in ink (`HEVO.pos`), five beats, a leap home. Under `'keep'` the page itself starts it when the creature standing is behind `P.homStage` (the jar is filled at a round's end, so it is seen at the next round's start).

## Twentieth builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-20` (worktree `page20`). Files: `scheme.js`, `kitart.js`, `feel.js`. Recorder: `.claude/f20` of the worktree (not in git; the scripts of `f19` only; ports 9201 page, 9202 frames; start `srv.py` and `fsrv.py` by hand; their Windows PIDs are in `WINPIDS.txt`). Jobs: `t.sh <name> <query>` (one take by `j_h19.js`; new there: `rack=` extra reagents on the rack, `prof=new|late` a still of a new or a late profile, `benchms=` length of a bench take, `tapF` the frame of the tap); `seven.sh` (`ONLY=salt|h2|wood|co2|ammonia|seedling|brimstone`: a molecule and a line that light each creature's REAL job); `z.py <take> <out> f1 f2 ..` (chosen frames large); `j_r10.js` with `second=1` (after the run's end it goes on to the next run and takes a still `<name>_b`).

- **The ten acts** (`A20`, `A20ACT`, `jumpPlan20`, `a20Ink`; `JUMPS` has all ten, `JACT` has a body move for each). `jumpPlan = jumpPlan20`. The figure is measured on the screen by its solid body (`a20Fig`: no fog, no floor pools) and stands about a quarter of the page high; `a20Place` finds its feet a place near `want`, never over the molecule (`a20MolBox`), the crease, the marks strip or the yield track; card acts (`xmax` set) stay on the right page by the cards. What each does is drawn by hand into the ink layer (`kinFrame` calls `a20Ink` before the creature's evolve number), from the real job: `A20.gone` (every group that leaves is written down by a wrapper round `flyer`: where it left, its atoms, the page), `P.hom2Rec(g).circ` (the line the page fired), `BT.map` (the cards). Acid eats the pieces that left; Water pours a running sheet with an edge and ripples that drains; Slime gums the twice-used bottle; Salt packs the repeated reagent's card in cubes; Dry ice breathes frost ferns over the untouched cards; Hydrogen ties a thread to each H2 that left and floats up; Wood spirit drinks the dud bottle (a stream, a level going down); Ammonia whiffs the three cards and one hand-drawn arrow takes them round again; Seedling plants itself and shoots run to the new atoms; Brimstone's fire runs to the round's pages and burns there.
- **The yield lands with the act**: the wrapper round `homJump` calls `AA.rxFeel.homLand(y)` (new in `feel.js`: the count rolls to the new yield there and then) when the act lands; `P.made` is read from a copy of `g` without `frozenAt`, since the page's own count is frozen until the page goes on. A motif's red strike that falls on an act waits until the act is over (`A20.until` in `kinFrame`).
- **Spent** (`a20Spent`, from `P.homSpent(g)`): low, wide, leaning, slow breath. The evolve moment stands on the left page (`EV`), never over the molecule.
- **Dry ice is fog** (`kitart.js`, `DRY.fog`; `AA.rxKit.dryIce = 'old' | 'cooler'` for the others): an outlined chalk core, and sprites (`wisp`, `fogTex`) for the fog; `AA.rxKit.fogDrift(group, t)` moves them, called for the bench creature each frame.
- **The shelf** is ten fixed places (`jarPos` under `BENCHV === 'a'`, `BNZ.jar` larger), an unhatched jar is clear with its fill level (`set(f, 3)`), a hatched creature is in its stage form (`jar.userData.form(stage)`), the run's creature leaves its jar in its place.
- **The profile** (`PROF`, `saveMeta`, `profOpts`): one key `overreact_profile` (`{ v, ladder, best, meta }`), a guard for a profile saved under an older ladder (rungs that no longer exist are dropped), `?fresh=1`, `?profile=` (json or base64; saves nothing). Every run starts with `P.profileOpts(meta)`; the page's gates read `P.stageOf(meta)` (the two old setters of `meta.stage` are off); `P.RULE.termsGate = false`. At a run's end `P.runResult(run).earned` is kept and shown (`A20.earn`, `a20Earned`, `a20Glyph`): each thing lands large on the left page on the beat and goes to where it lives. `AA.rxScheme.profile()` reports it.

## Twenty-first builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-21` (worktree `page21`). Files: `kitart.js`, `scheme.js`. Recorder: `.claude/f21` of the worktree (not in git; the scripts of `f20`; ports 9211 page, 9212 frames; `WINPIDS.txt`). Job `j_a21.js` (`j_h19.js` plus `then=evo&hold2=` an evolve after the act, `evo2=N` a second evolve from stage N, the report `S.r21()`); `cs.py <take> <out> x0 y0 x1 y1 scale cols a:b:n` (a sheet of one cropped place, large: the creature is small on the screen).

- **Acid is a rig, not a solid figure.** `kitart.js`, in `build`: `RIG.homacid()` gives `parts` (name, parent, maker: each part a pivot of its own, drawn at unit size) and `form(stage)` (where each part rests at that stage; one name at two stages is one part). Below `build`: `rigSet(grp, a, b, k, pose)` places the parts for a stage or between two, with a pose on top, and keeps each ink line even; `RK` is the motion kit (squash with the volume kept, `hop`, `ring`, `back`, `dress` for follow-through, `face`, `ambient`); `CLIP.homacid` has `rest`, `act`, `evo`; `rigPlay(grp, clip, time, o)` plays one frame. `AA.rxKit.rigOn = false` gives the solid pushed forms back. Nothing keeps a state: every value comes from the time, a late part reads the body of a moment ago.
- **The page says only when and where** (`scheme.js`, `R21`, after `homEvo19`): `r21Frame` (the wrapper round `a20Spent`) plays `rest` on `R21.tb`, a count of beats that follows `kinBeat`; a glance when the line fires; spent is a slump in the rig. The wrapper round `homJump` plays `act` and takes the figure's place from it. The wrapper round `homEvo19` stands the creature at its new stage at once (the rig holds all five), shows the old form and plays `evo`. `JLIFE.acid` plays `rest` slower in the jar.
- **The act is a trip on foot** (the owner: "I would prefer if the sprites walked to the molecule and interacted with it"): `A20ACT.acid` (rigged) stands it beside the piece that left (`r21Stand`: beside or just under it, mouth at its height, where it covers least of the molecule), `trip` hops it there in 3 to 5 hops with etched footprints, it reaches, the piece is drawn from its place into the mouth (`R21.mouth`), the atom it hung on is tugged (`r21Tug`), it hops to a second piece that lies apart, swallows, hops home. Lengths at pace 2 in `R21T` (there, work, home).
- NOT done: the other nine; the home dust puff is 0.4 s early when it hops to a second piece; the tug hardly shows; limbs have no elbow or knee; the evolve still goes out and back in one leap.

### How to rig a creature (about 110 lines and half a day each)

1. **Parts** (`RIG.<id>` in `build`, about 35 lines). List what must move by itself: `hips` (nothing drawn: the whole figure squashes from the feet here), `body`, face parts on the body (`eyeL/R`, `browL/R`, `mouth` with `maw`, `jawU`, `jawL`, teeth, `tongue`), `armL/R` and legs as limbs (drawn one long, hanging from the joint), its point or lid or flame, what it holds at each stage, what lies on the page (parent `'~'`), what drifts off it. Keep these names: `dress`, `face` and `ambient` look for them. Copy the numbers from its `PUSH` drawing into `form(stage)`; a part a stage does not name is not there.
2. **Motion** (`CLIP.<id>`, about 45 lines). `rest`: write `S(lag)` (breath, weight shift, the landing ring), call `K.dress`, then the stage's own small habit, `K.face`, `K.ambient`. `act`: its own GAIT in `trip` (acid hops; a slime should ooze, hydrogen drift, salt shuffle) and its own HANDS-ON work at the molecule; give back `e`, `y`, `lunge`. `evo`: one line of `RULE` per stage: which part comes on which beat and how (`m` tween, `p` grow, `d` drop in, `u` rise, `a` eaten, `g` gone).
3. **The page** (`scheme.js`, about 30 lines): an `A20ACT.<id>` that sets `j.r21`, `j.bites`, the standing place in `after`, and draws only what is touched; a line in `R21T`; a `JLIFE.<id>` wrapper. `r21Frame`, the `homJump` and `homEvo19` wrappers serve every rig as they are.
4. Check with `cs.py` sheets at 3x: all five stages at rest, one act, the four evolves.

## Twenty-second builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-22` (worktree `page22`). Files: `kitart.js`, `scheme.js`. Recorder: `.claude/f22` of the worktree (the scripts of `f21`; ports 9311 page, 9312 frames; `rep.py <file> <pairs.txt>` makes exact replacements in the long lines). Clip: `rig22-1.mp4`.

- **Acid, the stiff things fixed** (the nine copy it). Half as large again on the bench (`homStand` wrapper, capped at `BNZ.hom.s * 1.45`). A limb has a joint half way: the limb maker hangs its lower half on `userData.elbow`, the pose's `b` bends it (`rigPlay`), `RK.dress` folds knees on a squash and elbows on the way up. `trip` reads `HV` (each hop its own length and height, one a stumble) and the act skids in; `CLIP.homacid.lands(N)` gives where the feet came down, for the footprints. `r21Stand` may now stand clear of the whole molecule (left, right, under, or behind its top edge) and picks what covers least. `r21Tug` drags the whole molecule a little toward the mouth and lets it swing back. Evolve: out and back on foot (`OUT` beats each way, the clip gives `home`), the body is pulled tall and thin, drops past its shape and settles, new teeth come in one after another. The home dust puff reads `j.D`.
- **The auto rig** (`kitart.js`, `AUTO` in `build`, after `RIG`). A creature listed in `AUTO` is rigged from its own five `PUSH` drawings with no second set of numbers: each form is drawn once and every piece is given to a named part by where it sits about the body (the piece that carries the eyes, or the biggest solid piece): `ground`, `base`, `legL/R`, `body`, `point`, `eyeL/R`, `armL/R`, `fog`. Each part holds five small groups, one per stage; `Rg.post` (called at the end of `rigSet`) shows the one for the stage, and swaps it at the middle of the part's own way in an evolve. `Rg.fogs[stage]` lists the fog sprites.
- **`autoClip(G)`** (below `CLIP`) makes `rest`, `act`, `evo` from what is the creature's own: `gait`, `wind`, `work`, `pose`, `life`, `out`. Water: sinks to a puddle, flows sloshing, rears up. Dry ice: glides a hand above the page leaning into its way; `life` pours every fog sprite off the core on a loop, spreads it on the floor and trails it behind. Salt: one stiff leg, a stop, the other; rocks side to side; turns its head in clicks.
- **The page** (`scheme.js`, before the `homStand` wrapper): `A20ACT.water`, `.co2`, `.salt` (rigged versions; the old ones stay under `rigOn = false`), `R21T` lengths, `JLIFE` wrappers, `r22Mol` (a job done at the cards goes on to the molecule: `j.hop`, `perch2`, so the yield lands there). A card is drawn over whatever stands on it, so a creature works from the card's near edge.
- **How to rig the next six**: add the id to `AUTO`, write `CLIP.<id> = autoClip({...})` (about 12 lines), an `A20ACT.<id>` wrapper and an `R21T` line (about 12 lines), add the key to the `JLIFE` list. Look at one act sheet and one evolve sheet; if a piece lands in the wrong part, the rule is `which` in `AUTO`.
- **The won run's mark**: it was `AA.rxMarks.yieldMark(x, 3, ...)` drawn 150 px large on the right page in `kinFrame`'s done branch (`if (st.won)`), with `ink: '#b88a00'` (gold) and a gold pencil wash. It is now `INK`, the page's dark ink. Not looked at in a frame.
- NOT done: slime, hydrogen, seedling, wood spirit, ammonia, brimstone (batches 2 and 3; the flat sticker flames are still there). Stages 1, 2 and 5 of water, dry ice and salt were not looked at; the jar versions and the spent slump of the three were not looked at. Dry ice's fog is hardly seen on the white page away from its act (the act washes cold in under it; the evolve does not). Water washes over the whole molecule, not the double bonds only. The auto-rigged limbs are whole pieces: no elbow.

## Twenty-fourth builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-24` (worktree `page24`). File: `kitart.js` (added to only). Stills only. Sheet: `water24-options.jpg` and `.txt` in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`. The game's Water is not changed and nothing is wired in.

- **Water as an elemental, three ways** (`W24`, `AA.rxKit.water24 = 'a' | 'b' | 'c'`, `AA.rxKit.water24Fig(THREE, stage, way)`): a pure water, b water spirit, c water beast, five stages each, front to +z, standing at y = 0. The owner had rejected the old creatures as objects stuck together.
- **One closed mesh per body** (`sweep`): rings swept along a spine given as keys `[x, y, z, a, b]` (a point, half width to the side, half depth), Catmull-Rom between keys, frames carried along the spine. `pool(...)` gives the first keys: the thin pool and the fillet up into the foot, part of the same surface. Arms, ears, legs are `bumps` (`k` key index, `th` angle round the ring, `amp`, `off` a pull); manes, twists and foam lips are `mod(kp, th)`. Depth (deep foot, mid, pale where thin), `foam`, `lite` and the highlight are vertex colours. `inkHull` is the same geometry pushed out along its normals, back faces, for the ink line. So nothing can come apart when animated: animate by moving the keys and rebuilding, or by bones along the spine.
- **The face** (`face24`): two eyes and a smile laid where rays from in front meet the body; the same at every stage.
- **Pieces of their own**: eyes, mouth, the wet patch on the wood, loose drops. Nothing else.
- **The sheet job** is in `.claude/f24` of the worktree (not in git): `srv.py` (port 9431, writes `srv.pid`), `run.sh <name>` (one headless Edge, ended by its written PID), `sheet.html` (lamp values of `scheme.js` as in builder 18's sheet; `ways=`, `st=`, `w=`).
- **Known weak points** are listed per option in `water24-options.txt`.

## Twenty-fifth builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-24` (worktree `page24`). File: `kitart.js` (the water24 block, extended). Stills only. Sheet: `water25-spirit.jpg` and `.txt` in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`. The game's Water is not changed and nothing is wired in.

- **The water spirit, each stage its own character** (`S25`, `AA.rxKit.water24 = 'spirit'`, `water24Fig(THREE, stage, 'spirit')`): 1 newborn peeking from a rimmed puddle with a curl; 2 child propped on two hands with a cowlick; 3 youth twisting, one arm up, the other a ribbon, bubbles; 4 adult with the hair as a fall into the pool, a hand spilling water, a chest spiral; 5 elder with a coronet of breaking water, a foam beard, wide arms meeting the pool, drops round it.
- **Still one closed mesh per body** (`sweep25`, the same rings along a spine). New: **limbs** (`limbs: [{ k, th, r0, path, rad, w, flat, fk, foam, ey }]`): a round patch of the body's own skin, measured on the skin from the grid (a along the ring, b along the spine), drawn out along a path; the rim of the patch stays, its centre becomes the tip. Arms, the ribbon, the stream and the beard are limbs. Also `twist(kp)`, `lift(kp, th)` (the crown's points), `pk` (the key where the pool ends), more columns at the two sides (`warp`), three times the rows near a limb.
- **Colour is cut per pixel** (`mat25`: `MeshToonMaterial` with `onBeforeCompile`; attributes `w25a` = pool, ring of the pool, depth, foam and `w25b` = thin, no-spine): no stepped edges. Gloss bands come from the normal in the figure's own frame (lamp front-left, and back-right for backs), lens-ended; a pale edge away from the lamp; a broken spine line on backs; the pool has a rim line and broken ripple rings; the wood has only a thin wet margin.
- **The face** (`face25`, `lay25`): the same eyes at every stage; size, lids, brows (foam for the elder) and mouth change. Marks (cheek shines, bubbles, the spiral) are laid on by rays the same way.
- **The ink line** (`inkHull25`): hull along grid-smoothed normals and set back in depth (`polygonOffsetUnits`, `AA.rxKit.water25Back`), because the hull scratched through the skin on the uneven limb mesh. A patch; the cure is to re-mesh limbs with even rings. `AA.rxKit.water25Ink` sets the line weight (the sheet uses it for the enlarged figures).
- **Not done / weak**: stage 2's silhouette, stage 5's arms and its crown (a coronet, not the single breaking wave asked for), mesh far too dense for the game (30 to 45 thousand vertices a body). Listed stage by stage in `water25-spirit.txt`, with what will be hard to animate.
- **The sheet job** is `.claude/f25` of the worktree (not in git): `block.js` is the source of the block, `apply.py` puts it into `kitart.js`, `run.sh <name> [query]` renders `sheet.html` (`band=f|b|q`, `w=`) through builder 24's server (`.claude/f24/srv.py`, port 9431) into `.claude/f24/out`.

## Twenty-sixth builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-24` (worktree `page24`). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W26` at the end, one hook line at the end of the first closure), `scheme.js` (block `S26` before `drawInk`). Clips: `water26.mp4`, `water26-evolve.mp4`, `water26-stages.jpg` with notes, in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`.

- **The water spirit is the game's Water** (`AA.rxKit.spiritOn`, on by default; `false` gives the old Water back). `build('homwater', ...)` asks `AA.rxKit.spiritBuild`, so bench, jar and shelf all get it (a jar with no stage shows stage 2).
- **One skin per stage** (`geo26`): the stage's own numbers in `S25` (pool, spine keys, limbs) are read as one implicit body (a flat pool, round sections along the spine, a chain along each limb, joined smoothly) and meshed once by surface nets: 4.9 to 5.5 thousand vertices, one closed surface, built in 0.1 to 0.3 s and kept per stage. Stage 5's crown is seven points and a rim added here.
- **Joints, not parts**: pool, one per spine key, one per limb point (14 to 37). Every vertex is weighted to the joints of the chain it came from. `pose26` inside `play26` works each joint out from its parent with its own stretch, fatness and LAG (the clip is asked for ten moments, 70 ms apart; outer joints read the older ones, so head, hair, arms and ribbon follow late). Extra passes: `reach` (an arm leaves its pose for an arc to a point, thins as it lengthens, each joint turned along it), `pins` (a hand or the hair fall landed in the pool stays there), `sink` (the body goes down through its own surface; what is under lies flat in the pool).
- **Bound to the skin**: eyes, lids, brows and mouth are small pieces on the head joint (blink, look, widen, smile, an O); bubbles and the chest spiral are a second skin on the same joints (`marks26`); gloss and depth are cut by `mat25` in rest coordinates; pool ripples run on a uniform. Drops ride the nearest joint; three drips fall from the hair tip, an elbow and a hand.
- **Clips** (`C26`): `rest` in beats (breath, sway, blink, a glance, the landing slosh, spent, and each stage's habit: 1 ducks and peeks, 2 leans and looks about, 3 flicks its ribbon, 4 looks at its spilling hand, 5 draws tides); `act` (sink, flow as a puddle lying flat on the page, rise past its height, reach for each water, hand on the molecule, pleased, turn its back, flow home half sunk, turn, rise); `evo` (the new stage's skin posed as the old one by `morph26`, then body, left arm, hair, right arm, face, pool each on its beat).
- **The page** (`S26`): `doHom` is wrapped to keep the spot's `pairs` (the double bonds the rules will change); `A20ACT.water` stands it by the nearest of them, gives the clip the waters that left (`A20.gone`) and the hand's atom as world points, and draws only the waters in its hand, the ring where the hand lands and the film along those bonds; `j.rest` is shortened so the molecule changes as the film has run; `R21T.water` is `[1300, 5400, 2100]` for the spirit; `homStand` sets the bench size per stage. The strike of a lost motif already waits for `A20.until`.
- **Recorder**: `.claude/r26` (the scripts of `f22`; ports 9471, 9472; `j_w26.js`: `w26=1` plays the line unrecorded and starts the take when the tap is lit). Pose sheets without the game: `.claude/f26` (`run.sh <name> "&mode=rest|act|evo&st=N"`, port 9461; `block26.js` and `s26.js` are the sources, `apply.py` puts them into the two files).
- **Not done / weak**: listed in the three `.txt` notes. Chief: the evolve's first frame is a handover to the next stage's skin; the puddle is a plain disc; the arm has no hand shape; jar and shelf were not looked at; stages 1, 2, 4, 5 were seen at rest only (their acts and the other three evolves run through the same code and were not looked at).

## Thirtieth builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-24` (worktree `page24`). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W30` after `W29`), `scheme.js` (block `S30` after `S29`). Shots: `wash30-stills.jpg`, `wash30-stages.jpg`, `wash30.mp4`, `wash30-evolve.mp4` with notes. Switch: `AA.rxKit.wash30` (on by default; `false` or `?w30=0` gives builder 28's look; `style29` still wins when set).

- **Water in pen and wash, as a recipe** (`W30`). Three parts, each takes any skinned mesh:
  - **The pen** (`pen30`, `topo30`, `proj30`, `sil30`, `stroke30`, `free30`, `draw30`): each frame the skin is worked out on the CPU, its outline edges (front face meets back face) are joined into runs, and each run is one stroke written as pen.js writes one (a filled ribbon, pen.js's thin `press` curve, the down-stroke swell, `rxPen.INK`). The pen lifts at points fixed on the body (`W30.lift`); wobble is seeded by the body's own points and thrown again `W30.hz` (7) times a second. Rigid spheres (hand, drops) are outlined the same way. Hand-put strokes: two flow lines in front; from behind a spine stroke, the hair's fall and two nape ticks; two drifting ripples on the pool. The ribbons are written in clip space from `scene.onBeforeRender` (`hook30`), depth-tested against the body with a small pull toward the eye.
  - **The wash** (`mat30`, kinds `b` body, `m` margin, `p` rigid piece): no lamp. Blue multiplied over the picture so far (builder 29's `grab29`); off the page the base is paper colour (a cut-out). All unevenness is in the skin's bind space (`vB30`): bare paper on the lit side with a broken edge, a second wash in shade, pooling at the outline and low down, a bloom, gaps inside the line, pencil hatching (`HATCH30`).
  - **The margin**: the pushed-out skin (the old hull) drawn after the body: wash over the line in places, the cut-out's paper edge off the page.
- **Acting** (`C26.act`, `C26.rest` wrapped again): per water a wind-up (squash, lean away, eyes wide), a snap out, a held pose with the hand closed and the eyes squeezed, a snap home, a pleased curl; the torso turns first; the reaching arm swings wider; a wind-up before the hand goes on the molecule; a hop before it turns for home. Stage 1 no longer goes under its puddle when it ducks. At a change of stage the new stage's limb ends and outer hair stay small until their beat.
- **Jar**: `spiritBuild` is wrapped so the pushed jar form is the spirit; `jarLife` (scheme) plays it. In the jar its materials are opaque (a see-through material is hidden by the glass) and it has no margin.
- **Scene** (`S30`): wet marks are grey-blue on paper and a faint damp on wood (tint, tide and amount set each frame after `s28Frame`); no lamp shadow or caustic, one soft smudge under it; the lit ring is off; the atom films of `S28.mol` are redrawn as flat back-face shells (a ring of wash) and, when the job names no atoms, the atom under the hand and its neighbours are taken.
- **Not done / weak**: the figure is not laid in the paper's plane on the page (it stands at the bench tilt); atom rings are even discs; hatching is faint; the face parts are still tubes and spheres in ink colour, not strokes with pressure; lifts jump as the outline changes; brows and mouth do not change shape beyond scale; no timing on a real graphics card (the pen is CPU work: about 2,500 points and 7,000 edges a frame per figure, the jar figure too).
- **Recorder**: `.claude/r28` as before; `stills30.py`, `stages30.py`, `crop30.py`; sources `.claude/f30/block30.js`, `s30.js`, `apply.py`.

## Thirty-first builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-24` (worktree `page24`). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W31` after `W30`), `scheme.js` (block `S31` after `S30`). Shots: `evolve31-strip.jpg`, `evolve31.mp4` with notes. Switch: `AA.rxKit.evolve31` (on by default; `false` or `?e31=0` gives builder 30's change of stage back). Owner's ruling: this is WATER'S OWN change of stage, not a recipe for the others.

- **The sequence** (`evo31`, wrapped over `C26.evo`; times in beats of the music, `tl31`: each third is 2.5 beats plus a quarter beat per stage, so 3 to 4 is 9 beats, 4.5 s at pace 2; never under 2.5 s). The gathering (a start, then the curl: the pose, and `ball31` pulls every joint inside one round; beads run in). The whirlpool (a ball of wash, `make31`/`shape31`, grows out of the curled body; the old stage's own skin is changed for the new one inside it; wash in two turning arms, pen strokes that land and lift, a ring of drops and spray, pencil lines on the bench, a round of wash under it; it tightens and holds still for the last eighth). The spring (the same ball mesh opens from the top and lies down; the new form shoots up; `fern31` unrolls arm, hair, arm on beats of their own; splash ring, flecks, drops; the proud look; a hop home).
- **Scheme** (`S31`, wrapped over `homEvo19`): it stays home; `E.big31` and `E.fwN` are worked out once so the whole sprouting form is in the picture (it grows and comes toward the eye while it hovers, and hops home at the end). Sound: `sfx.drift` at the start, `sfx.windup(stage, ms, true)` for the build, `sfx.flag` (and `sfx.boom` from stage 4) at the hit, the old `feed`/`good` on the unrolling beats. The steam bends, the cup is kicked, the nearest card lifts its corner, the nearest jar rocks at the hit; three rings of wet marks (`s27Stamp`) run out. The new number is written beside it when it has settled.
- **Not done / weak**: small in the picture (the bench has little room); the opened ball reads as two flat sheets and then a slab until the pool covers it; the curl is mostly a shrink; it always happens at home (the game raises the stage only after the act has gone home); the hop home was not asked for; the bench's answers are hard to see; stages 1 to 2, 2 to 3 and 4 to 5 have not been looked at in frames; no timing on a real graphics card.
- **To record each step** (bash, from `.claude/r28` in the worktree; first start the two servers, each in its own window: `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`; one take at a time; each take is about 9 s and takes a few minutes). `<N>` is the stage it leaves (1, 2, 3 or 4):
  - `JOBN=a21 TMO=560 ./t.sh ev31_<N> "&hom=water&part=evo&stage=<N>&lead=1000&hold=7600" 24` records frames and sound into `frames/ev31_<N>` and writes a sheet `ev31_<N>.jpg`.
  - `python make4.py evolve31-<N>.mp4 ev31_<N>` makes `.claude/shots/evolve31-<N>.mp4`. All four back to back with number cards: `python make4.py evolve31-all.mp4 ev31_1=1 ev31_2=2 ev31_3=3 ev31_4=4`.
  - Key-frame stills instead of a take: `./e31.sh k<N> <N> "a.12,a.6,b.05,b.5,b.93,v.1,v.3,v.75,v1.9" 1700` writes `t31/k<N>.jpg` (a = the gathering 0 to 1, b = the whirlpool 0 to 1, v = thirds of a beat-third after the hit, 0 to 3).
- Sources: `.claude/f31/block31.js`, `s31.js`, `apply.py`.

## Thirty-second builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-24` (worktree `page24`). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W32` after `W31`; one line of `ink31` changed), `scheme.js` (block `S32` after `S31`). Shots: `evolve32-strip.jpg`, `evolve32.mp4`, `small/evolve32.mp4` with notes. Switch: `AA.rxKit.evolve32` (on by default; `false` or `?e32=0` gives builder 31's change of stage back). Rulings BH and the owner's correction the same evening ("I don't want them to be moving I just want everything to be interacting rather than looking like two or three distinct layers").

- **Staging** (`S32`, wrapped over `homEvo19` after `S31`; it overrides `HEVO.pos` and `homObj.s`). `E.c32` is worked out once from the camera: where the new form stands so that it is about half the screen tall with its middle at the middle (`zc`, `F`), and how large the ball is at 8.5 above the page on the eye's line so that it is about two fifths of the screen's height (`B`). The way: home, up over the book toward the eye in one move (`e.go`, from early in the gathering to a third into the whirlpool; it grows ahead of the move so the curl is seen), down on to the page as it bursts, the proud look, then two more beats home seen from behind (`e.home`; `q.turn`, sunk in its pool). 3 to 4 is 11 beats, 5.5 s at pace 2 plus the start.
- **The ball** (`up32`, `shape32`, `curl32` in `W32`). Its own mesh: five petals of nine lines with a line between that is cut away as it opens (`pid`, `pu`). Its own wash: what is under it is read from the picture bent toward the rim and run a little; three bands that wind round; a pooled rim; a shaded side with pencil; bare paper that rides the spin. Open, each petal leans out, cups and curls its point back, narrows to its point, and is lined along its edges in the wash (the outline finder is switched off while it is open: it joined petal to petal). The curl: the spine is rolled forward into a ring and the limbs are carried with it, before `ball31` pulls all inside one round. Everything of the ball is grown by `o.bk32`.
- **One picture, nothing moved.** Builder 31's cup kick, card corner, jar rock and shake are switched off under this switch (`E.f.rise31`, `E.f.hit31` are set; the sound is called from `S32`). In their place: the room's shade (`S32.sm`, one sheet drawn in the scene after the bench and page and before the water, worked out on the bench's plane: the lamp's round on it, pencil shade outside, lighter for the instant of the burst), the ball's shadow on the page (wide and soft when high, small and dark when low), a blue thrown on what is near, drips and thrown drops that leave damp marks where they land (`s27Stamp`), a wet middle and two rings at the burst. The scene's lights do nothing to this look (tried: no change), so the shade is a multiply sheet, not light.
- **Where it still reads as layers**: the shade is flat on the bench's plane, so jars, cup and cards are shaded as if they had no height, and the ink overlay (score, marks) is not shaded; the water is not lit by the lamp, it is wash over the picture; the shadow seen through the ball muddies its lower half; damp marks are the old round stamps, the ink under them does not run; the standing form's arm lies over the molecule with no answer from it; the wash on the bench under the ball (`gw`) sits under the foot, not under the ball.
- **Other weak points**: the curl is small in the picture; the petals' last moment lying down is flat shards; the move out and home is quick; only 3 to 4 was looked at; no timing on a real graphics card; sound not checked.
- **To record** (bash, from `.claude/r28`; `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` first): stills `./e32.sh k5 3 "a.5,a.8,b.12,b.5,b.93,v.08,v.25,v.5,v1.8,v2.5" 0 5 0.5` writes `t32/k5.jpg` (the whole screen in each frame; v above 2.15 is the way home; seventh argument is the page's molecule). A take: `JOBN=a21 TMO=580 ./t.sh ev32_3 "&hom=water&part=evo&stage=3&lead=1000&hold=8400&smi=CC(O)CCC(C)=O" 24`, then `python make4.py evolve32.mp4 ev32_3`.

## Thirty-third builder (14 November)

Files: `kitart.js` (block `W33` after `W32`; small edits in `F30`, `mat30`, `hook30`, `up32`, `shape32`, `stage31`'s wrapper), `scheme.js` (block `S33` after `S32`; `s33Stamp` beside `s27Stamp`, used by the S27 trail, S31 and S32). Shots: `evolve33-strip.jpg`, `evolve33-compare.jpg`, `evolve33.mp4`, `small/evolve33.mp4` with notes. Switch: `AA.rxKit.one33` (on by default; `false` or `?o33=0` gives builder 32's look). The one job: Water and the scene as ONE picture during the change of stage; sequence, size, place and timing untouched.

- **One pass over the bench** (`S33.sheet`, render order 16.5: after everything on the bench, the page's ink included, before the water). Builder 32's flat shade sheet is not drawn (`S32.sm` is put on layer 7; its numbers are still read). The pass reads the picture so far (`grab29`) and the bench's depths (`s33Bench`: the scene drawn once more without the water into a target with a depth texture, at the start and every 20 frames), so every point knows where it is in the room.
  - **The lamp** (`S33.L`: 22 left, 46 up, 15 behind the middle of the round). Its round is a cone about its aim; a face turned from it is shaded (the facing is worked out from neighbouring depths); what stands on the bench throws shade away from it (a short march through the depths); the ink is in the picture already and dims with the page.
  - **Water's shadow** (`s33Light`): the water alone (layer 5; pen ribbons and flat helper planes left out) is drawn as the lamp sees it, depth only, 1024 square. Each point of the bench is looked up in it: blockers are averaged, the shadow is wide and pale when the water is high and tight and dark at contact, it falls on the atoms too, and it is not drawn inside the ball's round on the screen (so it is never seen through the ball).

Only on things lying flat (by height), fainter on wood.
- **The lamp on the water** (`W33`, uniform `l33`: the lamp seen from the eye, and how much). `F30` (body, margin, drops) and the ball's wash take their lit side from it: warm on the lit side, the room's shade on the far side, a warm edge. Zero outside the change of stage, so the look at home is as before.
- **Petals**: they keep half their curl as they go down (`shape32`) and soak away from their points with a dark wet edge (`soak` in `up32`); the old bench wash `gw` is hidden.
- **Where it still reads as layers**: jars, cup and cards answer the lamp only faintly; the pen line (ribbons written in the picture's frame) is neither lit nor shaded; the arm still lies over the atoms, explained only by the shadow below; drip marks are dark fuzzy dots; the pool keeps builder 30's bare patch, now cream; after the burst the wet patches are large and one blue; no cockling mark; the blue under the ball is now only the tint of its shadow.
- **Other weak points**: only 3 to 4 was looked at; two more scene draws (one small, one every 20 frames) and a full-screen pass with about 60 texture reads a point, not timed on a real graphics card; the first frame compiles new programs (a hitch is likely); a one-frame lag between pose and shadow; needs WebGL depth textures; sound not checked.

## Thirty-fifth builder (14 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W35` at the end, added to only). Stills only. Sheets in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid35-A.jpg`, `acid35-B.jpg`, `acid35-C.jpg` (five stages each, on the bench), `acid35-options.jpg` (stage 3 of each beside the notebook), `acid35-options.txt`. Switch: `AA.rxKit.acid35 = 'a' | 'b' | 'c'` (or `?a35=a` on the page); unset by default, so the game shows the old Acid. Nothing moves, acts or changes stage; the jar was not worked on.

- **Three ideas of an acid elemental** (`SPEC35`, `AA.rxKit.acid35Fig(THREE, stage, way)`, front to +z, standing on y = 0):
  - **a, the maw**: acid as what eats. A heap that is mostly mouth, drip teeth, tongues of fume (`lick`), thick hands. 1 a bead gaping up; 2 a low grinning heap; 3 sits back, a hand at its lip; 4 on its knuckles, jaw out, drooling; 5 a great heap, cavern mouth, crown of fume.
  - **b, the borer**: acid as what etches through. No limbs, no eyes; a worm out of a hole in the bench, a round toothed mouth (`lam`), pores down the head, the tail up out of a second hole (`rim` is a hole's lip). 1 grub; 2 a question mark; 3 an S reared to strike; 4 a hood of drips over one coil; 5 an arch through the bench, spines of fume.
  - **c, the thorn**: acid as the sharp thing, a splash caught at its points. A bead on pointed legs (`leg`), scythe arms, one slit eye. 1 a drop mid-splash; 2 up on three points; 3 four legs and two scythes; 4 reared like a mantis, one scythe raised; 5 six legs and a crown.
- **One closed skin per body, a new way** (`P35`, `field35`, `nets35`, `geo35`): a stage is a list of shapes (ball, ellipsoid, a limb through points, a cone), melted into one distance field with soft joins; the mouth is carved out of the field and the teeth grown back in; the skin is laid over the field on a grid (one point a cell, a quad across every crossed edge), so it is welded and closed and has no separate parts. About 10 to 25 thousand points a body; built once a stage and kept (`G35`). `aQ` marks the mouth's wall (1) and the teeth (-1).
- **Skinned, not posed**: a `SkinnedMesh` on five spine bones by height. Limbs have no bones yet.
- **Pen**: builder 30's (`pen30`, `proj30`, `sil30`, `free30`) through `draw35` and a scene hook (`hook35`); the wisps over the maw are `free30` strokes.
- **Wash** (`mat35`, `F35`): Acid's own, no lamp, no grab of the picture behind (paper colour under the wash everywhere). Green wash, bare paper on the lit side, a second wash in shade, pooling low and at the outline, fizz rings, pencil hatching, the mouth's dark inside, bare-paper teeth, and up to eight eyes painted in the body's own space (`e35a`, `e35b`: a dot with a glint, or a pale eye with a slit).
- **A piece of its own**: the scorched mark under it (`etch35`), as Water has its wet patch.
- **In the game** only behind the switch: `KIT.build` is wrapped so `homacid` gives the chosen figure. It has no rig, so it stands still; its act and change of stage are the old code's and were not looked at with the switch on.
- **Weak** (from the sheets): a reads as a small monster more than a liquid, stage 1 too small to read; b has no face but the mouth and its holes read as a puddle; c reads as a spider first, its feet do not sit on the wood (the game's soft shadow lies behind them); the green is a first choice; pencil faint; not lit by the lamp; the shelf jar not looked at; no timing on a real graphics card.
- **To render** (bash, from `.claude/r28` of this worktree; not in git: copies of builder 28's recorder on ports 9591 and 9592, `vh.js` takes `big=2`; `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` first, end them by their PIDs): five stages `./st35.sh a` (then `b`, `c`; about two minutes each; writes `t_sa.jpg` by `sheet35.py`); stage 3 of each `for w in a b c; do NAME=o$w WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh a35 "&a35=$w&part=one&stage=3&big=2" 180; done`, then `python opts35.py out.jpg`. Job `j_a35.js`. Source of the block: `.claude/f35/block35.js`, put in by `apply.py`.

## Thirty-seventh builder (14 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W35` rewritten in place). Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid37-stages.jpg` (five stages on the bench), `acid37-compare.jpg` (stage 3 before and after), `acid37-game.jpg` (stage 3 in the full game view), `acid37-stages.txt`. Switch: `AA.rxKit.acid35 = 'a'` (or `?a35=a`); unset by default, so the game shows the old Acid.

- **Only the maw is left**: directions b and c (borer, thorn) and their helpers are gone; `SPEC35` holds `a` only.
- **A liquid, not a toy** (`SPEC35.a`, helpers `pool`, `drip`, `arm`, `lick`): each stage rises from a lobed pool; side lumps sag into it; `drip` is a thinning neck with a bead, used for the teeth (uneven lengths and leans), the drool to the pool, and drips off chin, elbows and brow; `arm` is a thick run with finger runs that spread into the pool; tongues of fume are thin, low and lean one way. Stage 1 is drawn 1.35 times larger (an inner group), its mouth turned toward the eye with drip teeth on its back wall. Stage 2 is a lopsided slumped heap with a drooping grin, no toad legs.
- **Eyes are pits** (kind 2): after the skin is found, a pit is carved where each eye meets it; painted dark wash pooled in it, a pale meniscus below, an ink lid line above, a pupil whose direction gives the attitude (up: hungry; aside, lidded: sly; down, lidded: sated; slanted, down: ravenous; small under a heavy brow: vast).
- **Wash** (`F35`, `mat35`): paler and uneven, pooled at the outline and low down, bare paper on the lit side, stronger pencil; the material is see-through where thin (alpha about 0.75) so the wood and the eaten mark show faintly. The sharp acid green is only on the mouth's rim (`aQ` band) and in drips, stronger toward the bead (new attribute `aD`).
- **The eaten wood** (`etch35`, renderOrder 3, under the body): bleached and pitted inside, a broken scorched ring, an inked edge in places, a thin damp line where the pool meets the wood, a soft shadow to the back. Its geometry has a tiny bounding box so it does not count in the creature's size (the game fits creatures by `Box3`). Pen marks drawn in `draw35`: bubbles round the pool and threads of fume (`fume35`: a leaning rise, a break, a curl over the top) off the ring and the head.
- **Weak** (from the sheets): the eaten ring reads a little like a plate (too even an ellipse); the short tongues on the head still read as small horns; the pen fume is small; stages 4 and 5 still read as a cartoon monster from the front; the wood barely shows through; the game's soft shadow sits behind it; not animated, the jar not looked at.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r28` of this worktree, ports 9591/9592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st35.sh a out.jpg` (five stages; frames in `frames/sa`, f00004 is stage 3 in the full view).

## Thirty-sixth builder (14 November)

Branch `page-builder-24` (worktree `page24`). Files: `kitart.js` (W34: `KIT.fix36`; W32's `up32` wash: `F36`, `in36`, `p36`), `scheme.js` (S32: fewer burst rings, slower drips, smaller wet middle; S33: `s33Spot`, the wet-paper lines of the sheet's shader, and `S36`/`s36Fit`/`s36dbg` placed just before S33's `homEvo19` wrapper so it runs after S32 has placed the form and before S33 draws). Shots: `evolve36-strip.jpg` with notes (stills only). Switch: `AA.rxKit.fix36` (on by default; `false` or `?f36=0` gives builder 34's). Three faults, nothing else.

- **The whole form in the picture** (`s36Fit`). After the burst the new form is measured as it was last drawn: points of its skinned body (`boneTransform`, about 500) and of the open bud (`x31.sph`), carried to this frame's place and size. If the body would leave the middle 0.83 of the screen up and down (0.9 across), it is set back from the eye (up the picture, `HEVO.pos.z`) as little as will do; only if that cannot fit is it made smaller (`homObj.s`). The petals may only push it up, never shrink it, and when they cannot all fit, they run off rather than the form. Once set back it eases forward only slowly (4% a frame), and the correction fades with `e.home`. One rule for all four steps: nothing changes while the form fits; standing, 1 to 2 is set back about 6.4 units, 2 to 3 about 4.2, 3 to 4 about 1.5, 4 to 5 about 4.7. The form is never shrunk at standing in the four steps looked at.
- **Wet paper** (`s33Spot`, S33 shader with `F36`). Every patch is smaller (0.62 of before, less farther away) and fainter the farther it is from the creature (`exp(-d / 2.6 Rw)`); the burst lays 4 + 6 rings, not 7 + 11, and a middle of 0.7, not 0.95; drips every 300 ms, not 170. In the shader the pigment pools at the rim (0.72 of it), the middle is pale and uneven (0.18 to 0.52), a few darker blooms sit inside; the grey darkening of the wet paper is 0.55 of before. Drying is unchanged.
- **The near petals** (`up32`). An open petal's inside is not the dark-blue back face any more (`in36`), its shaded core is a quarter of before, its middle is pale lit wash with paper through it (alpha 0.42 to 0.72) and colour pooled at its pen-lined edges, the page under it is read as paper rather than the shadow (`base`), and the lamp's shade on it is 0.3 of before; as it soaks its middle thins first.
- **Still wrong** (from the frames): the curled-back tips of the lying petals still read as a pale grey ring for a moment; at 1 to 2 the form now stands over the molecule and higher on the page; a one-frame lag between pose and fit; not timed on a real graphics card.
- **To record**: same commands as the thirty-fourth, or `./e36.sh <name> <stage> "b.93,v.1,v.6,v1.8" 0 5 0.5 "" "&gone=1000"` (job `j_e36.js`: logs `s36dbg` at each key: `t` the set-back, `k` the shrink, `box` the form's screen box before the fit). Stage is the one it leaves (1 to 4).

## Thirty-eighth builder (14 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first, the notes conflict settled by keeping both). File: `kitart.js` (block `W35`, the maw's five stages replaced). Stills only, stage 3 only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid38-options.jpg` (A, B, C large on the bench), `acid38-game.jpg` (the three full game views at game size), `acid38-options.txt`. Switch: `AA.rxKit.acid35 = 'a' | 'b' | 'c'` (or `?a35=a` on the page); unset by default, so the game shows the old Acid.

- **The monster body is gone** (`SPEC35` is now `{ a, b, c }`, one function each; every stage asked for gives the stage-3 figure; `geo35` keeps one skin a way). No head, brow, shoulders, horns or `lick` tongues in any of them. New helper `spike` (a neck rising to a bead).
  - **a, the breaker**: a wave seen from its side, reared and curling over; three ribs across z make the slab, a swell behind it; the hollow of the curl is carved open on the near side only (so the far wall is the dark of the mouth); drips off the crest are teeth, the pool's lip rising in front is the lower jaw; one pit of an eye on the flank; two runs with hands in front.
  - **b, the crown**: a pool leapt up round its middle: a closed ring (tube, `closed`) tipped to the eye, carved into a cup, nine points leaning in; two pits low on the wall; one run arched over the mouth, one out toward the page.
  - **c, the cowl**: a standing gout, tip curled over, hollow down its front; drips from the top edge, a strand lip to lip; two pale beads back in the dark (new eye kind 3 in `F35`); one run raised, one at the lip.
- **Wash** (`F35`): paler, a little more bare paper (`hl` from 0.72), nearly opaque (alpha 0.95, paper under the wash as Water has; the see-through over brown wood was the mud), fewer fizz rings. Program key `a38`.
- **The eaten wood** (`etch35`): no longer a wobbling ellipse. The patch is found in warped space with a radius that swings from under half to nearly full by direction, so it runs out one way and hardly shows another; pale bleach inside, the scorched edge only in stretches, few pits, loose spots beyond it; no hard cut-off, so the contact line and shadow are whole.
- **Weak** (from the sheets): a can read as a fish head, its lower lip and near arm run together; b is the least mouth-like (a bowl, an anemone), its arm a handle; c is the nearest to a figure (a hooded ghost), a large bare patch on its lit side; the eaten patch reads a little like spilt sand, fizz and pen fume are small at game size; the game's soft shadow is not under them; stages 1, 2, 4, 5 not drawn; not animated; the jar not looked at.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r28` of this worktree, ports 9591/9592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `for w in a b c; do NAME=o$w WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh a35 "&a35=$w&part=one&stage=3&big=2" 200; done`, then `python opts38.py options.jpg game.jpg` (about 25 s a figure).

## Thirty-ninth builder (14 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first). File: `kitart.js` (block `W35`). Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid39-stages.jpg` (the five stages on the bench, labelled 1 to 5), `acid39-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3 and 5 in the full game view, real size), `acid39-stages.txt`. Switch: `AA.rxKit.acid35 = 'a'` (or `?a35=a`); unset by default, so the game shows the old Acid.

- **Only the breaker is left**: sketches b (crown) and c (cowl) and the `lick` helper are gone; `SPEC35.a(o, stage)` picks one of five stage functions (`ST`); `geo35` keeps one skin per stage (key `a1` to `a5`); `acid35Fig` honours the stage.
- **New helpers**: `wave` (one profile, base at the back over the crest to the tip, laid as ribs across the breadth; the ends slump lower and thinner, `rag` makes the crest uneven), `sag` (liquid running down its back into the pool), `lip` (the low trough under the curl, no jaw line), `tooth` (a crest drip caught mid-fall, pinched neck and bead; not a paint drip, so it stays pale). A mouth cut (`o.c(..., 1)`) now puts a thin far wall behind itself (`ax` on `P35.ell`), set left of the cut so the hollow is dark at the back and still open at the front-right; before this the cut was a hole through to the wood.
- **The five**: 1 a lick lifting off the pool, mouth a crease, two teeth, two little hands in the pool; 2 a low swell beginning to curl, three teeth, one hand flat on the wood, one lifting behind; 3 the breaker, five uneven teeth, one hand up out of the pool in front, one from behind the curl; 4 reared right over, mouth wide, six teeth, both hands out in front of the opening; 5 a great breaker, cavern under the curl, ragged crest, seven teeth, the widest eaten patch. Stage 1 is drawn 1.32 times and stage 2 1.12 times inside the figure (the game shrinks them hard); the game then fits by `Box3` and `stageSize`.
- **No fish face**: the eye is a small pale glint (kind 3) high under the roof of the curl, not a pit on the flank; liquid sags down the back; the body widens into its pool. Both hands are clear of the lower lip.
- **Stronger at game size**: pen `W35.lw` 1.12 and never thinner than 0.8 of its full weight (`draw35`); the wash pools darker at the outline and in shade, less bare paper (`hl` from 0.80); program key `a39`. Spray in flight drawn as small pen rings (`o.spr`, `S.spr`).
- **The eaten wood** (`etch35`, rewritten): bleached grain streaked along the bench, pits drawn out along the grain with a pale lip, a sunk wall (dark toward the lamp, lit on the far side), a ragged broken scorched edge with charred brown inside and flecks of char beyond, a damp band and a thin glistening line at the pool's rim, a softer shadow under the body to the back.
- **Weak** (from the pictures): the dark hollow is a round disc that can read as one big eye at stages 1 and 2, and from the side the whole can still read as a slug or frog head; the pen is blotchy and heavy along the crest (rib edges); spray barely shows; teeth clear only at 4 and 5; stage 1's mouth and the eaten wood hard to see at game size; the game's own soft shadow still sits behind; not animated; the jar not looked at.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r28` of this worktree, ports 9591/9592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st35.sh a` (five stages into `frames/sa`, f00002 = stage 5 ... f00006 = stage 1, about 95 s), then `python sheet39.py stages.jpg game.jpg`.

## Fortieth builder (14 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W35`). Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid40-stages.jpg` (five stages on the bench, labelled), `acid40-silhouettes.jpg` (the same five as black shapes on white), `acid40-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `acid40-stages.txt`. Switch unchanged: `AA.rxKit.acid35 = 'a'` (or `?a35=a`); unset by default.

- **Five postures, one being** (`ST` in `SPEC35`, rewritten): 1 a shy lick, a little lip lifted off its pit and turned up at the front, a crack of a mouth under it, one small tooth, two nubs on the wood for hands; 2 a greedy swell, low and long, both paws flat on the bench under the mouth, the lower lip spread on the wood, a wide slot with four teeth; 3 the breaker, reared and curled, one hand flung up and forward off the top of the curl, the other braced on the wood behind, five teeth; 4 the rip curl, tall, turned toward us (`W35.rot`), both hands thrown up wide, spray off a sharper curl; 5 the standing sea, a tall face curling forward at the top over a cavern hung with four strands, a hand laid along the lower lip, a second smaller wave rising behind from the same pool, the widest eaten wood. `W35.rot` gives each stage its own turn on `grp.rotation.y`. The silhouettes sheet shows five different outlines.
- **The mouth** (`mouth(o, path, zb, fy, fz)`): a wall of liquid fills the hollow from behind (an ellipsoid over the path's span, deep in z) and a channel is cut into it from the front along a path of `[x, y, r]` points, tapering at the back end; so the opening is a long low slot open to the front and out past the curl's tip, never a round pit. The old far-wall ellipsoid trick (`ax`) is no longer used by the stages. `o.my` keeps the slot's height and goes to the shader as `m35`: inside, the far wall is lit at its top and dark at its bottom, the roof darkest, liquid pooled on the floor with a pale glint (program key `a40`). `strand` hangs pale strands across a mouth.
- **No skull**: the backs are long slopes from far behind into the pool, with `sag` runs; the eye is a tiny glint high on the crest, away from the slot. `wave` takes an optional `z0` (for the second wave).
- **Pen** lighter: `W35.lw` 0.96 (was 1.12); fewer teeth at stage 4.
- **Weak** (from the pictures): at game size stage 1 is tiny and its crack and stage 2's paws barely read; stage 4's teeth draw as pen loops and its round crest with a toothy slot and raised arms still reads a little like a cheering head; stage 5 is fitted by its width, so in the game it stands no taller than 3 or 4; spray faint; not animated; the jar not looked at.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r28` of this worktree, ports 9591/9592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st40.sh` (job `j_a40.js`: five stages into `frames/s40`, f00002 = stage 5 ... f00006 = stage 1, then their silhouettes f00007 ... f00011, about 130 s), then `python sheet40.py stages.jpg silhouettes.jpg game.jpg` (crops to the five figures' joint outline).

## Forty-second builder (14 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W35`). Stills only. The owner chose "Mix the two" of the 39th and 40th sets. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid42-stages.jpg` (five stages on the bench, labelled), `acid42-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `acid42-stages.txt`. Switch unchanged: `AA.rxKit.acid35 = 'a'` (or `?a35=a`); unset by default.

- **The mix**: stages 1, 2 and 5 keep the 40th set's postures (shy lick, greedy swell on both palms, standing sea with a second curl behind); stages 3 and 4 are the 39th set's waves again (the reared breaker; the tall wave reared right over with both hands out in front), with the 40th's fixes: the mouth is `mouth()`'s long slot under the curl (lit far wall at top, dark at bottom), no round cut and no lower back ellipsoid, a long slope up the back with `sag` runs. Stage 4 is taller (crest 1.66 against 1.12), leans further forward, `W35.rot[3]` -0.1. Hands reach forward, never up. The curl tip is lifted and the mouth wall stops at the tip (`fy` 0.1 / 0.12) so the front stays open.
- **Teeth**: `tooth` is one smooth tapering drip (no pinched neck); tooth shapes carry `th`, and in `geo35` every vertex on a tooth gets `aQ` -1 (pale). `draw35` draws the body's outline with the teeth left out, then the teeth alone with a nib 0.42 as thick, so they are pale drips with a thin edge, not loops.
- **Pen**: body line `{ wob: 0.45, smooth: 4, min: 9 }` (steadier, no tiny scribbled runs).
- **Sizes in the game** (`homStand` fits by `min(h / height, w / width)` times `stageSize`): stage 1 drawn 1.55 times (was 1.32) on a smaller pool; stage 5's pool and second wave drawn in and its crest raised to 2.38, so it is fitted by its height and stands taller than 4 (fit `s` 3.16 against 3.06; heights about 7.9 against 6.1).
- **Weak** (from the pictures): stage 1 still small at game size, its crack barely reads; at 3 and 4 a rounded bulge of the mouth's back wall shows beside the curl tip and a see-through gap under the slot, so a little of the "face" remains; a few tooth roots at 3 keep a dark edge; stage 2 stands lower than 1 in the game (width-fitted, low by posture); spray faint; not animated; the jar not looked at.
- **To render**: as the 40th (`./st40.sh`, then `python sheet40.py stages.jpg silhouettes.jpg game.jpg`; servers `python srv.py`, `python fsrv.py` on 9591/9592, ended by PID).

## Forty-fourth builder (15 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W35`: small tags only; new block `W44` after it), `scheme.js` (block `S44` after `S33`). Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid44-idle-strip.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 at home, six moments of one idle cycle each), `acid44-jar.jpg` (the shelf with Acid in its jar, stages 1 to 5), `acid44-idle-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default**: `AA.rxKit.acid35` is now `'a'` unless the page has `?a35=0` (gives the old Acid back). `AA.rxKit.acid44 = false` keeps the new figure but standing still (builder 42's).
- **The rig** (`rig44`, once a stage, on a copy of the skin so `acid35Fig` stays as it was): a pool skirt bone; five spine bones whose heights are set by the mouth (0, half the floor, floor, roof, above the roof) and whose x follows the middle of the skin; a bone per hand (hands on the wood hang from the root and pivot at the hand; raised ones hang from the spine and pivot at the shoulder; stage 1's two nubs are hands); stage 5's second wave has its own bone. Teeth take the weights of their root, so they ride the roof. Four weights a point, smooth blends, no part can come apart. Helpers in `W35` now record `o.arms`, `o.teeth` (tips), `o.dps` (drip tips), `o.pc` (pool centre), `w2` on the second wave; `geo35` keeps `ops` and `o`.
- **The idle** (`pose44`, a pure function of the page clock, from the scene hook before the pen; `P44` per stage): the spine bends in a travelling heave (crest lags the base), the roof bone lifts as it rears and drops as it sags (mouth opens and narrows), the skirt creeps, planted hands shift their grip now and then and flex, raised hands reach and curl. 1 quick and timid, rears to peek every 4.6 s then ducks with a shiver; 2 rocks forward and back over its planted palms; 3 restless (an uneven extra wobble and twist); 4 leans and sways side to side, with an eager lunge every 2.5 s (hands go with it); 5 slow (6.2 s), the second curl out of step. The fizz rings in the wash come and go (`t44`); the wash's light leans toward the bench lamp (`lamp44`) with a warm cast on the lit side.
- **Drops and the scene** (`draw44`, pen; beads are small pale-green spheres): drips swell at every tooth tip and the crest drip, let go, fall, and either sink into its own liquid or land on the wood and leave a wet etched mark that fades over about 4 s (`flat44('wet')`); bubbles on the eaten rim swell and pop in three ticks; every ~5 s a bubble rises off the pool, pops, and a thread of fume lifts from it; the fume threads on the body and the eaten wood lift, lean and fade in turn; the spray beads hop off the crest and back. A soft shadow (`flat44('sh')`) follows the crest's swing. The eaten wood stays put.
- **Jar**: the shelf jar is built by the first half of `kitart.js` (its own `build`, not `KIT.build`), so `KIT.jar` is wrapped (`jar44`): the half-grown and ready figures and the stage form (`userData.form`) are the living breaker, opaque, fitted to the glass (`fit44`), idling at 0.6 speed. `S44` stops the jar's old squash-bob for it (it only turns a little).
- **Cost**: points a body: stage 1 18,198; 2 22,321; 3 31,772; 4 28,116; 5 26,317. The pose is 8 bones and a few riding points: 0.05 ms a frame. Headless software rendering at 1304 x 812, stage 3 at home: 163 ms a frame against 160 for the old Acid. The pen still projects every skin point on the CPU each frame (builder 30's way, O(points)); a GPU outline would be cheaper and is not done.
- **Weak** (from the frames): the motion is small at game size (2 and 3 read in motion, hardly in stills); drips, bubbles and fume are tiny pen marks at 1280 wide; in the jar the figure is small and the jar's level ring crosses it; the dark spot in stage 3's mouth floor remains.
- **Not looked at with the new figure** (it carries no `rig2`, so the game treats it as unrigged, as before with the switch): its act (the trip to the molecule and eating) and its change of stage; the act may slide/scale the whole still-idling figure. To be checked next.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r28` of this worktree, ports 9591/9592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID). Stills: `NAME=s44 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh a44 "&part=strip&stages=1,3,5&k=6&big=2" 480` then `python strip44.py frames/s44 out.jpg 3 6 1,3,5 "515,0,795,175"`; jar: `NAME=j44 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh a44 "&part=jar&big=2" 380`; cost: `NAME=c44 ./run.sh a44 "&part=cost&stage=3" 280`. Clips (job `j_a44.js`, script `t44.sh`, frames at 30 a second, mp4 into the shots folder):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/acid35/.claude/r28 && ./t44.sh acid44-idle 3 12` (one 12 s take at stage 3)
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/acid35/.claude/r28 && ./t44.sh acid44-idle-all 1,2,3,4,5 6` (stages 1 to 5 at rest, 6 s each, back to back)

## Forty-eighth builder (15 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first). File: `kitart.js` (block `W48` after `W44`; one default in `W35`). Stills only, stage 3 only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid48-options.jpg` (A, B, C and Water's stage 3 at the same scale), `acid48-game.jpg` (each in the full game view), `acid48-silhouettes.jpg`, `acid48-options.txt`. The owner rejected the wave ("looks like shit a little", "hard to tell what it looks like"; wrong in idea and in drawing) and chose "A spirit like Water".

- **Switches.** `AA.rxKit.acid48 = 'a' | 'b' | 'c'` (or `?a48=a`): off by default. `AA.rxKit.acid35` is OFF by default again (`?a35=a` turns the living breaker of builders 35 to 44 on for comparison; `?a35=0` no longer means anything). With neither set the game shows the old Acid.
- **Built by Water's code, not a new pipeline** (`acid48Fig`). Each version is a set of numbers shaped like a stage of `S25` (`SPEC48.a/b/c`: pool, spine keys, limbs, face, drops). For the moment of building, `S25[2]`, `W26.geo[3]`, `W26.ROLE[3]` and `W26.TARGET` are swapped for Acid's and put back, and `fig26(T, 3)` is called: so the skin (`geo26`), joints, face, drops, the belonging to the scene (builders 27, 28), the pen and the wash (`set30`, `draw30`) are all Water's. The figure carries `rig2.spirit` like Water, so `scheme.js` sizes it (`homStand`), gives it the smudge under it and plays Water's `rest` on it. Its skins are kept in `W48.geo`. Every stage asked for gives the stage-3 figure.
- **What is Acid's own.** Fingers (`arm48`): each finger is a limb that runs down the same arm and leaves it at the hand, so a hand is three chains joined by the skin. Licks standing up from the pool's edge (`lick48`: a limb leaving the body low in front or behind, `side` 0). Narrow slanted eyes: Water's eye parts pressed flat and turned (`o.eye`, written into the eye's `q0`). Green wash (`tint48`): the four blues of `F30` are replaced in the compiled shader, with a program key of its own; `W48.C` holds the greens. The pen's own strokes (`o.pen`, `ink48`, hung on `draw30`): teeth laid on the skin by rays from in front, threads of fume in the air (`fume48`), each tied to the joint nearest it and written with `free30` into the figure's own pen. The eaten wood is `etch35`, hung on joint 0 so it lies with the pool.
- **The three.** a the sneak (lean, a flame of a head, one arm out to take, one claw raised); b the glutton (squat, a grin with teeth, two big hands up); c the countess (tall, long neck, crest swept back, hand on hip, a pinch raised, one dripping sleeve; `W48.ROLE.c` gives it two neck joints).
- **Weak** (from the sheets, against the Water panel): none is as clean as Water, which has fewer and bigger shapes. a is nearest and reads at a glance; its hands are small and its line breaks into dashes on the thin arms. b reads fastest but is round and close to a cartoon ghost or monster. c is thin, small in the game view, its face hard to read there. For all: fume and fizz are small; the eaten wood shows on one side as a brown smear; the rest motion is Water's and was not designed for these bodies (an `act` would be Water's too: do not fire Acid with the switch on); the jar keeps the old Acid; stages 1, 2, 4, 5 not drawn; about 7 thousand vertices and 50 to 63 joints a figure, no timing taken.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r28` of this worktree, ports 9591/9592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st48.sh` runs job `j_a48.js` four times (`&a48=a|b|c&big=2`, and `&hom=water&big=2` for Water; frame `f00003` is the game view, `f00004` the black silhouette) and then `opts48.py` writes the three sheets (about 40 s a figure). Sources: `.claude/f48/block48.js`, `apply.py` puts it into `kitart.js`.
