## Fiftieth builder (15 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first). File: `kitart.js`, block `W48` only. Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid50-stages.jpg` (the five Acid stages, Water's five below at the same scale), `acid50-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `acid50-silhouettes.jpg`, `acid50-stages.txt`.

- **What exists.** The sneak (A of builder 48, the owner's pick) in five stages, `SPEC48[0..4]`, each built the way builder 48 built A (numbers in Water's stage-3 slot through `fig26`, Water's skin, joints, face, pen and wash). 1 the spark: a small flame standing up out of its pit, leaning, two little claws up by its face. 2 the imp: waist-deep, hunched forward, claws knotted at its chest, a toothy grin (pen zigzag `teeth48`), a horn of flame. 3 the sneak: as picked, one long arm reaching for the page, one claw raised, a horn of flame. 4 the fiend: hooked over, head hung toward the page, long arms spread wide with long dripping fingers, a flame forked three ways. 5 the devourer: arms raised with big claws, a crown of flame tongues, a wide grin with teeth, sleeves of acid pouring down to a wide pit like a cloak.
- **Not Water's design** (owner: "Don't copy the water completely"): no droplet peeking over a puddle, no ribbon, no pouring hair, no splash crown or beard; flame heads with forked tongues (`tongue48`) instead of the teardrop with a curl; a ring of standing licks round every pool (`licks48`) so it is never a smooth round puddle.
- **Fixed from A**: hands bigger and every finger long and pointed (`fin48`: three points, a needle tip, a bend); arms thicker so the pen line holds; the pit (`etch48`, replaces `etch35` here) is an even ragged ring all round with a dark floor, pale fizz rings, a bleached lip and a broken pen line at its edge; it is laid flat on the bench each frame (`lay48`: the figure's own floor is tilted by the bench view, so a pit in it sank under the wood); fume threads longer and heavier, pen fizz rings and curls at the pool.
- **Switch.** `AA.rxKit.acid48 = 'a'` or `?a48=a` (off by default; B and C are gone). The bench figure takes its stage from the build (`extra.stage`); the figure carries its stage (`S.st`), so `homStand` sizes it like Water's stage and Water's rest for that stage plays on it. The jar keeps the old Acid; `acid35` stays off.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r28` of this worktree, ports 9591/9592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st50.sh [stages] [rows]` runs job `j_a50.js` (`&a48=a&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/sN`, `&hom=water&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/wN`; `f00003` the game view, `f00004` the silhouette), then `python sheet50.py` writes the three sheets. Block source `.claude/f50/block50.js`, `python .claude/f50/apply.py` puts it into `kitart.js`.
- **Weak** (from the sheets): stage 1 is small and its face tiny at game size; stage 2's hands are a knot rather than clearly rubbing claws; stage 5's head is small for the body so its grin hardly reads in the game view, and it looks a little like a candelabra; pen fizz rings are too small at game size; the licks are the same at every stage; Water still has fewer, bigger, cleaner shapes; the rest motion is Water's; 5 to 7.6 thousand vertices a figure, no timing taken.
## Forty-ninth builder (15 November)

Branch `dryice-builder-49` (worktree `dryice49`, from `steam`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W49` after `W48`). Stills only, stage 3 only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `dryice49-options.jpg` (A, B, C and Water's stage 3 at the same scale), `dryice49-game.jpg` (each in the full game view), `dryice49-silhouettes.jpg`, `dryice49-options.txt`. Ruling BL: Dry ice is "a spirit like Water too". The owner's note during the work: "Don't copy the water completely": Water's code and hand, not Water's design (no teardrop head with a curl, no smile, no raised arm, no round pool).

- **Switch.** `AA.rxKit.dry49 = 'a' | 'b' | 'c'` (or `?d49=a`): off by default; without it the game shows the old Dry ice.
- **Built by Water's code** (`dry49Fig`), the way builder 48 built Acid: each version is a set of numbers shaped like a stage of `S25` (`SPEC49.a/b/c`); `S25[2]`, `W26.geo[3]` and `W26.TARGET` are swapped for the moment of building and put back, and `fig26(T, 3)` is called. Skin, joints, face, pen and wash are Water's; the figure carries `rig2.spirit`, so `scheme.js` sizes it, gives it its smudge and plays Water's `rest`. Skins are kept in `W49.geo`. Every stage asked for gives the stage-3 figure.
- **What is Dry ice's own.** The spill (`roll49`, `spill49`): ten or eleven low pillows of fog set round the foot on an oval, each lying ALONG the rim, each a limb joined to the foot by a thread that runs under the flat pool and is not seen; so the figure stands in a scalloped bank of fog, not a pool. (First try: rolls running outward from the foot. They read as crossed legs. Do not go back to that.) Heavy lids (`face.lid`). The wash (`tint49`): the four blues of `F30` changed for white and grey-violet in the compiled shader, the paper under it a colder white, and the lit side left bare much further round (`smoothstep(0.52, 0.64, l9`), so the fog is mostly paper; `W49.C` holds the colours. Pen strokes (`o.pen`; written by builder 48's `ink48`, `S.t48` is set so it leaves the wash alone): a wisp that falls (`fall49`), the block as a cube drawn on the skin (`cube49`), the hood's rim, stars of frost on the wood (`star49`, tied to joint 0). Grains of frost are the drops, lying flat.
- **The three.** a the dozer (a heavy mound, big head lolling, lids nearly shut, an "o" breathing a curl, one arm hanging, one lying on a roll); b the hooded one (tall, a soft hood with a floppy tip and a drawn rim, one arm held level with a sleeve of fog falling from it, the block on its chest; uses `arm48`); c the shy one (small, round, big head, wide eyes, both hands holding the block, elbows out).
- **Weak** (from the sheets, against the Water panel): none reaches Water, which has a clear gesture and an elegant line; these are heavier and plainer. c reads fastest, then a, then b (thin; its face is a few pixels in the game view; nearest to Water in build). All three look more like snow or marshmallow than fog: the pen line is hard all round; softness will have to come from motion. Frost on the wood is small. The rest motion is Water's and was not designed for these bodies (the pillows are limbs: an `act` would be Water's too, do not fire Dry ice with the switch on). The jar keeps the old Dry ice. Stages 1, 2, 4, 5 not drawn. About 7 thousand vertices and 65 to 75 joints a figure, no timing taken. The log's `s30.err` (`hull27 ... 'value'`) is there for Acid's figures too and is not from this block.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r49` of this worktree, untracked; ports 9791/9792; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st49.sh` (or `./st49.sh a` for one; `NOSHEET=1` skips the sheets) runs job `j_d49.js` (`&d49=a|b|c&big=2`, and `&hom=water&big=2` for Water; frame `f00003` is the game view, `f00004` the black silhouette), then `opts49.py` writes the three sheets (about 40 s a figure). Sources: `.claude/f49/block49.js`; `apply.py` puts it into `kitart.js`.

## Fifty-third builder (15 November)

Branch `dryice-builder-49` (worktree `dryice49`; `steam` merged in first). File: `kitart.js`, block `W49` only. Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `dryice53-stages.jpg` (the five Dry ice stages, with Water's five below at the same scale), `dryice53-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `dryice53-silhouettes.jpg`, `dryice53-stages.txt`.

- **What exists.** The shy one (C of builder 49, the owner's pick) in five stages, `SPEC49[0..4]`, each built like Acid's (numbers in Water's stage-3 slot through `fig26`, with `W49.ROLE` and `W49.TARGET` set for each stage; the figure carries `S.st`, so `homStand` sizes it like Water's stage). 1 a breath: wide eyes over ONE fat roll, the block a glint under the chin. 2 the huddle: fat arms hugging the block, face turned away, three rolls. 3 the shy one, as picked. 4 the keeper: heavy lids, the block in its chest with a cold glow, forearms forward with fog sleeves, a skirt of rolls. 5 the old frost: a hood with a drawn rim and a long drooping tip, bowed, eyes shut, the block deep in its chest, a bank spilling far along the bench, rime and ferns.
- **Dry ice's own parts (new).** The head ends in a soft peak that droops over to one side (no teardrop curl, no flame fork), with a pen fold line over the brow (stages 2 to 4). `box49`: a real little block of ice (Lambert boxes with the colour in the emissive, so `set30` does not take them; three cold blues) drawn round by the pen; `glow` puts a soft blue disc over it from stage 4. `rime49`: soft white patches with frost needles (canvas alpha map) lying flat on joint 0; `frost49`/`fern49`/`star49`: ink ferns and stars over them. `wisp49`: wisps that fall from the arms.
- **Fog, not snow.** `cut49` and a wrapped `sil30`: skin vertices low in the bank (`o.bank` = [y0, y1]) and in patches along the outer and back rolls are kept out of the pen's silhouette, so the line breaks and stops. `tint49` adds dry brush to the wash: low and outer, the colour gives way to what is behind (`bg`) in horizontal streaks of noise. It also takes the lamp's warmth off (never beige), makes the shade grey-violet and the eye glints cold blue. In the `draw30` wrapper, `G.pl` and the chest role are hidden from the pen while it draws, so Water's pool ripples and front flow lines are not drawn.
- **Switch.** `AA.rxKit.dry49 = 'c'` or `?d49=c` (off by default; A and B are gone). The bench figure takes its stage from the build (`extra.stage`). The jar keeps the old Dry ice.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r49` of this worktree, untracked; ports 9791/9792; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st53.sh [stages] [rows]` (rows `d` and `w`) runs job `j_d49.js` with `&d49=c&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/dN` and `&hom=water&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/wN` (`f00003` is the game view, `f00004` the silhouette); then `python sheet53.py` writes the three sheets. The block source is `.claude/f53/block53.js`; `python .claude/f53/apply.py` puts it into `kitart.js`.
- **Weak** (from the sheets): the head still reads mostly as a ball with a floppy nightcap tip. It still looks like snow or marshmallow when still; it is fog only at the broken lower edge of the bank. Silhouettes 3 and 4 are close, and so are 1 and 2 on screen (each stage is scaled to a fixed height). At 4 and 5 the sleeves and rolls can read as crossed legs, and the hood rim at 5 a little like a balaclava. Frost is small at game size. The block outline is crisp. The rest motion is Water's. 5 to 9.4 thousand vertices a figure, no timing taken.

## Fifty-second builder (15 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W52` after `W48`, before `W49`; tags and two small changes in `W48`), `scheme.js` (block `S52` after `S44`). Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid52-idle-strip.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5: the game view, then six moments of one idle cycle cut from the 1920-wide view, enlarged 2x), `acid52-jar.jpg` (the shelf, and the jar close at stages 1, 3, 5), `acid52-idle-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.acid48` is `'a'` unless the page has `?a48=0` (gives the old Acid). `AA.rxKit.idle52 = false` (or `?i52=0`) keeps the sneak but plays Water's rest on it, as builder 50 left it.
- **Its own idle, not Water's.** `C26.rest` is wrapped: for the sneak, `rest52` fills the clip's numbers itself (Water's rest is not run). Then `play26` is wrapped so `pose52` bends the joints after the whole play chain: the flame tip (`bj` from the head joint up) and every fork (`tongue`, the crown) lengthen, bend and snap back (`bend52`); the licks round the pool stretch up and sink out of step; each stage's arm business (`rot52` about the shoulder, `push52` along the arm); every finger curls, drums and uncurls in the picture plane (`curl52`). All of it moves joints of the one skin, so nothing detaches. Time is the page clock (`clk52`), slowed to 0.6 and made smaller in the jar.
  - 1 darting: sways fast with quick jerks side to side, ducks down into its pit and pops up with a small overshoot, claws wriggle and twitch by its face.
  - 2 hunched, bobbing, rubs its claws together (the hands slide past each other in opposite circles), now and then stops and peeks over one shoulder, then the other.
  - 3 leans toward the page with the long arm stretching out, fingers flexing, then draws back; the raised claw drums finger after finger.
  - 4 a slow swaying hook, head tilting, long arms swinging out of step, fingers curling; drips from both hands.
  - 5 slow and grand: arms rise and fall and the sleeves go with them (fading toward the pool, where they stay), the crown flares, dashes of pen run down the sleeves (the pour).
  - All: candle-flame sway with bursts of shiver; a sly glance toward the molecule (eyes slide, head half turns, brows down) every 4 to 8 s; the grin widens now and then; blinks.
- **Drops and the scene.** A drip (Water's drip pieces, bigger) gathers on a chosen fingertip (stage 5: on each sleeve), stretches, falls straight down in the world to the wood under the hand, and leaves a small dark etched mark that fades over 4 s (three flat marks on the pool joint, so they lie on the bench like the pit) and a pen fizz ring. A bubble swells on the pool and pops in four ticks (two at stages 3 to 5). Builder 48's pen strokes now move (`pen52`): fume threads grow up off the head, curl, rise and thin away; fizz rings swell and pop; curls rise; the teeth widen with the grin. Air drops riding a finger follow it. The pit stays put (`lay48` unchanged).
- **Jar.** `KIT.jar` is wrapped (`jar52`, builder 44's method): half-grown, ready and the stage form are the sneak (opaque, fitted to the glass). `S52` plays the stage form (`big2`) at rest like Water's form; the jar's old figures stay for the speck. Fix found here: in the jar the margin material W30 puts on (`none`) has no line width, so `play27` threw every frame and nothing after it ran (this is the old `hull27 ... 'value'` error); `W52` gives that material a dummy width before playing, for the sneak only.
- **W48 changes.** Limb numbers carry tags (`arm`, `fi`, `lick`, `tongue`, `crown`, `sleeve`; strokes `air`, `fz`, `teeth`); `ink48` skips a stroke with `off`; the default of `acid48`.
- **Cost** (headless software rendering, 1304 x 812, stage 3 at home): `pose52` 0.07 ms a frame. Frames 147 ms with the new idle (209 in a second, noisier pass), 162 with Water's rest on the sneak, 159 for the old Acid: the same within this renderer's noise. The pen work per frame is builder 30's (CPU outline of the skin) plus a few short strokes.
- **Act and change of stage with the new figure** (called directly with `rigPlay`, not fired in a run; nothing throws): the act is Water's `act` clip (sink into a flat puddle, flow, rise, Water's reach with a hand, turn its back, flow home), played on the sneak's skin; the arms are finger chains and the forks count as arms, so expect the reach to grab with a finger chain and the forks to swing with it. During it the idle, drips and marks stop (Water's drips play) and the idle comes back after. The change of stage is Water's `evo`: it morphs from WATER's skin of the old stage (`geo26(T, from)` builds Water's numbers, not Acid's), so the first part of the change shows the sneak's joints on Water's old shape. Both need their own clips next (BD: the trip and the eating).
- **Weak** (from the frames): at true game size the big moves read (duck, lean and reach, arm swings, head tilt, flame tip, the falling drip and its mark) but the eye slide, grin and finger drumming are a few pixels; fume and fizz are thin; stage 2's rubbing is small; stage 5's pour dashes hardly show; in the jar the figure is small, its pen line breaks into dashes and the jar's level ring crosses it. The stage 1 duck sinks the face out of sight for about half a second. Stages 2 and 4 in the jar were not looked at.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r28` of this worktree, ports 9591/9592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID). Job `j_a52.js`, sheet `sheet52.py`. Strip: `NAME=s52 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh a52 "&part=strip&stages=1,3,5&k=6&big=2" 560` then `python sheet52.py frames/s52 out.jpg 1,3,5 6 "860,50,1080,215;780,0,1180,250;700,0,1260,260"`; jar: `NAME=j52 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh a52 "&part=jar&stages=1,3,5&big=2" 360`; cost: `NAME=k52 ./run.sh a52 "&part=cost&stage=3" 360`; act/evo probe: `NAME=c52 ./run.sh a52 "&part=clips&stage=3" 360`. Clips (script `t52.sh`, 30 frames a second, 2560 capture scaled to 1920, mp4 into the shots folder):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/acid35/.claude/r28 && ./t52.sh acid52-idle 3 12` (12 s at rest, stage 3)
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/acid35/.claude/r28 && ./t52.sh acid52-idle-all 1,2,3,4,5 6` (stages 1 to 5 at rest, 6 s each)
  Block source `.claude/f52/block52.js`; `python .claude/f52/apply.py` (from the worktree root) puts it into `kitart.js`.

## Fifty-sixth builder (15 November)

Branch `dryice-builder-49` (worktree `dryice49`; `steam` merged in first). File: `kitart.js` only (block `W56` directly after `W49`, and small changes inside `W49`). No `scheme.js` change. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `dryice56-idle-strip.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5: the game view, then six moments over 4 s cut from the 1920-wide view round the home spot, enlarged 2x), `dryice56-jar.jpg` (the shelf, and the jar close at stages 1, 3, 5), `dryice56-idle-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.dry49` is `'c'` unless the page has `?d49=0` (gives the old Dry ice). `AA.rxKit.idle56 = false` (or `?i56=0`) keeps the shy one but plays Water's rest on it. Bench and jar both work for all five stages, nothing throws.
- **Found and fixed:** since builder 52 merged, builder 52's Acid idle keyed on `S.a48`, which the Dry ice figures also carry (their pen strokes), so with the switch on Dry ice was playing ACID's idle and drips. `W56` hides `S.a48` from the play chain for every Dry ice figure, so `W52` never starts on it.
- **Its own idle, not Water's** (`W56`, builder 52's method): `C26.rest` is wrapped (`rest56` fills the clip's numbers), then `play26` is wrapped so `pose56` moves the joints after the whole play chain. Time is the page clock (`clk56`), slowed to 0.6 in the jar.
  - The fog: each roll of the bank (the limbs of `roll49`) wells up small under the figure, swells, creeps out along its own line, flattens a little, shrinks (short as well as thin, so never a sausage) and comes again, out of step with the others; the large rolls of stages 1 and 2 only swell and lean out. Soft mist pieces (flat cloudy sprites on the pool joint, drawn over the bank's foot) creep outward over the wood and fade. In the wash (`tint49`, uniform `u49` = the idle's clock): the dry-brush streaks at the bank's foot creep outward, the body's edges thin and fill in patches that sink, faint grey streaks sink through it. The pen line's breaks (`cut56`, replaces `cut49`'s mask each frame) creep outward along the bank and a few gaps open and close on the body as patches sink (the face kept nearly whole). The body breathes and its girth swells joint by joint out of step. The drooping tip sways and droops on each breath out. Wisps (pen) peel off the tip, the hands, the sleeves and the block and sink to the bank, curling out; the breath out falls as a wisp; a shiver now and then sends a pen puff rolling off each side; the static wisps of `W49` slide down and thin; the frost ferns grow and fade, the rime with them. The block keeps its place in the hands, with a slow cold glint (a small pen star; the glow pulses at 4 and 5). Water's drips are hidden.
  - Manner: slow blinks; glances that slide away and hold; every cycle it notices the viewer (eyes front, wide, brows up), ducks, then peeks sideways. 1 ducks under its roll and comes up somewhere else along it. 2 rocks as it hugs the block, squeezing it closer, face turned away, turning back when it notices you. 3 holds the block out a little, notices you, pulls it back and hunches behind it, then peeks. 4 calm, heavy lids, a slow nod on each breath out, sleeves pouring wisps. 5 asleep: a slow deep breath, the head bowing on each breath out, the bank spilling.
- **W49 changes:** strokes tagged (`t: 'wisp' | 'fern' | 'box'`, fern patch `g`, `ft`), block meshes `userData.box49`, rime `userData.r56`, the shader takes `u49` (and `K56E`, `K56S`: `?e56=`, `?s56=` on the page to try other amounts; also `?g56=` gaps, `?m56=` mist, `?w56=` wisp weight), the default of `dry49`.
- **Jar.** `KIT.jar` is wrapped (`jar56`, builder 52's way): half-grown, ready and the stage form are the shy one fitted to the glass (the stage form lifted clear of the jar's front lip). The ready one is played by the existing `JLIFE.co2` (it plays any rigged figure); the half-grown and the stage form play their rest from their own `onBeforeRender` (once a frame), so no `scheme.js` block is needed. The hull27 width fix of builder 52 is applied for Dry ice too.
- **Cost** (headless software rendering, 1304 x 812, stage 3 at home, 30 frames each): `pose56` 0.73 ms a frame. Frames 163 ms with the new idle (203 in a second, noisier pass), 159 with Water's rest on the shy one, 139 for the old Dry ice. The cut mask (about 8 thousand vertices of noise a frame) is most of `pose56`.
- **Act and change of stage with the new figure** (called directly with `rigPlay`, not fired in a run; nothing throws): the act is Water's `act` clip played on the shy one's skin (it sinks flat, flows, rises, reaches with an arm, turns its back, flows home); the rolls are limbs and go with Water's moves, the box and frost stay on their joints. During it the idle, mist, wisps and moving breaks stop (the strokes are put back, `W49`'s still cut comes back) and the idle returns after. The change of stage is Water's `evo`: it morphs from WATER's skin of the old stage (`geo26(T, from)` builds Water's numbers), so the first part shows the shy one's joints on Water's old shape. Both need their own clips (BC: Dry ice's evolution comes after Acid's is seen).
- **Weak** (from the frames): in any one still frame it still reads more as snow or marshmallow than fog; what makes it fog is motion (rolls changing, mist creeping, breaks crawling) that stills only half show. Wisps and the shiver puff are thin pen lines, one or two pixels at game size. The grey streaks in the wash read as blotches. Stage 5's bank can lie flat like cloth. Stage 1 moves least. In the jar, stage 5 is small and low. Stages 2 and 4 were not looked at in the strip, nor in the jar.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r49` of this worktree, untracked; ports 9791/9792; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID). Job `j_d56.js`, sheet `sheet56.py` (builder 52's), `grid56.py` (crops as a grid). Strip: `NAME=s56 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh d56 "&part=strip&stages=1,3,5&k=6&span=4000&big=2" 580` then `python sheet56.py frames/s56 out.jpg 1,3,5 6 "840,40,1140,200;780,10,1200,240;720,0,1280,260"`; jar: `NAME=j56 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh d56 "&part=jar&stages=1,3,5&big=2" 480`; cost: `NAME=k56 ./run.sh d56 "&part=cost&stage=3" 480`; act/evo probe: `NAME=c56 ./run.sh d56 "&part=clips&stage=3" 380`. Clips (script `t56.sh`, copied from `t52.sh`; 30 frames a second, 2560 capture scaled to 1920, mp4 into the shots folder):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/dryice49/.claude/r49 && ./t56.sh dryice56-idle 3 12` (12 s at rest, stage 3)
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/dryice49/.claude/r49 && ./t56.sh dryice56-idle-all 1,2,3,4,5 6` (stages 1 to 5 at rest, 6 s each)
  The servers must be running (start them as above; `my56.pids` holds the PIDs of the last ones). Block source `.claude/f56/block56.js`; `python .claude/f56/apply.py` (from the worktree root) puts it into `kitart.js`; `.claude/r49/p56_w49.py` holds the `W49` changes (already applied).

## Fifty-fifth builder (15 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W55` after `W52`, before `W49`), `scheme.js` (block `S55` after `S52`). Nothing in `W48`, `W52`, Water's blocks or `S31` to `S36` was changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid55-evolve-strip.jpg` (four rows, 1 to 2 ... 4 to 5; six moments each in the full game view: sinking, boil at its widest, held, claws out, tallest, home and settled), `acid55-evolve-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.evo55` (`?v55=0` or `false` gives the old change of stage, which is Water's played on the sneak). Only the sneak (`acid48`) uses it; Water's own change of stage is untouched (the wrapper passes every other figure and clip straight on).
- **What exists: "Eats itself, erupts".** `play26` is wrapped for Acid's `evo` clip: Water's `evo` is not run. The timeline is in beats of the music (`PAR55`, `ph55`): go, sink, boil (its last part held), erupt, look, home; 1 to 2 is 9 beats, 2 to 3 10.75, 3 to 4 11.75, 4 to 5 13 (4.5 to 6.5 s at pace 2). The hit falls on a whole beat.
  - **Go** (`S55`): from home over the open book in one arc to the place worked out once (`s55Place`): the new form about half the screen tall, its middle a little above the screen's middle, the pit's widest about a third of the screen's width; if that would open over the molecule, the place moves left of it (it does on the test page). It grows on the way. `s55Fit` makes it smaller only if any of it above the page would leave the middle 0.88 / 0.94 of the screen.
  - **Sink** (`sink55`): the OLD stage's figure (built once by `acid48Fig`, beside the new one, like builder 31's old figure) grins, its hands are carried over its head (`hand55`) and its claws curl; then every joint is carried straight down in the world (`dive55`: the world's down in the joints' own space), twisting about the pool's axis and pinched where it passes the rim. The page hides what is under it (checked first: the bench and the book write depth), so it goes down its own hole, flame tip last. No shrink, no swap that shows: the old body is removed and the new one put in place at the start of the boil, both wholly under the page.
  - **Boil** (`pit55`/`stage55`, one flat sheet on the page under the room's lamp pass): a hole with a ragged edge that gnaws round, a floor seen nearer the eye the deeper it is, the far inner wall lit and streaked, a thin lit near lip and a broken pen line; bubbles swell and burst on the floor; the paper outside chars brown at the rim and bleaches beyond; the fume's shade lies on the page away from the lamp; spat drops fly in pen and leave etched pocks that fade in 1.5 s. Pen licks leap at the rim, big pen bubbles swell and burst (`ink55`). The fume: 18 puffs of pale green-grey wash, lit on the lamp's side, rising along the figures' own up, leaning a little toward the lamp, with short pen curls and two wavering edge lines. Held instant: the boil, licks and spitting stop; only the fume moves.
  - **The room gives way**: builder 33's one-lamp pass is driven from `S55` (`s55Lamp`, using `s33Make`, `s33Bench`, `s33Light` without changing them): the lamp's round on the place, the room in shade, the form's real shadow from the lamp, the lamp on its wash (`l33`); lighter for an instant at the hit. Water's bench helpers that are wrong for Acid are held off during it (`S27`: no wet trail, no lamp-through-water light; its soft blob shadows give way while it is at the middle).
  - **Erupt** (`rise55`): the new stage's figure is under the page until the hit. The claws shoot up out of the pit through the fume (the fume is torn aside at once), hold, slap down on the rim, the body hauls itself out (overshoot for the imp, the fiend), the claws let go, head thrown back, then it looks down at the page with its stage's face (`face55`). Each step its own way: 1 to 2 a small pit, a quick pop with a big overshoot; 2 to 3 one arm first, the head comes up to the eyes and peers left and right, then the other arm, a slow climb out; 3 to 4 comes out bent over and unfolds like a hook straightening; 4 to 5 the widest pit, the tallest column, both claws wide on the rim, a slow rise. Pen dashes run down its body (acid running off).
  - **Home**: the pit closes to the new stage's own and fades, the scorch fades (the page is clean after: checked in the last frame of each row), it turns its back (`z.turn`), goes home in a low arc with its flame tip bobbing, and its new number is written beside it (`HEVO.num`, as Water's).
  - **Sound** (existing calls only, on the beats): `drift` (leaves home), `squish` (goes down), `draw` riser and `pen` crackle over the boil, `flag` (and `boom` from 3 to 4) plus `swish` at the hit, `stamp` at the slap, `feed` fizz as it comes out, looks and turns, `swish` going home, `good` and `stamp` as it settles.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r55` of this worktree, not in git: copies of builder 28's recorder on ports 9891/9892; start the servers with `powershell -File startsrv.ps1 -Dir <r55 path>`, which writes their PIDs to `srv55.pids`; end them by those PIDs). Stills: `./e55.sh <name> <stage it leaves> "<beats,...>" "<ms after home>"` (job `j_e55.js`, sheet `t55/<name>.jpg`); the strip: `./all55.sh <prefix>` then copy `t55/<prefix>-strip.jpg`.
- **Clips for the helper** (one at a time; each take a few minutes):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/acid35/.claude/r55 && for s in 1 2 3 4; do JOBN=a21 TMO=600 ./t.sh ev55_$s "&hom=acid&part=evo&stage=$s&lead=1000&hold=9000" 24; done`
  - each step alone: `python make4.py acid55-evolve-<s>.mp4 ev55_<s>`
  - all four back to back with number cards and sound: `python make4.py acid55-evolve-all.mp4 ev55_1="1 to 2" ev55_2="2 to 3" ev55_3="3 to 4" ev55_4="4 to 5"`
- **Weak**: see `acid55-evolve-strip.txt`. Also: the pit is one flat painted sheet (its depth is drawn, not modelled), so seen low it is a disc; the fume puffs are flat cards facing the eye; the arms stretch thin from an under-page shoulder while the claws are up; the evolution was looked at in stills only, sound not heard, no timing on a real graphics card (the lamp pass adds two scene draws as Water's does).

## Fifty-eighth builder (15 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W58` after `W55`, before `W49`; small hooks in `W52`: `rest52`, `pose52` and `ink52` hold back the idle's arm business, finger curls, drips, marks and pool bubbles while `o.act58` / `S.c52 === 'act'`), `scheme.js` (block `S58` after `S55`). Water's and Dry ice's blocks untouched. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid58-act-strip.jpg` (stage 3, twelve moments of one act in the full game view), `acid58-act-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.act58` (`?a58=0` or `false` gives the old act, which is Water's played on the sneak). Only the sneak uses it; the wrapper passes every other figure and clip on.
- **What the rules give it.** Acid's tap does not remove atoms: its yield counts the heavy atoms the page has already lost (`g.hom.count`, rules unchanged). Those atoms are the pieces that left this page (`A20.gone`, in the order they left): `S58` takes the last `count` heavy atoms, puts each back on the molecule's edge as a lit ball of the scene (`ballMat`), a bond's length out from the atom it left, away from the molecule's middle, on a bond to it. Each gulp lands on a beat; the last gulp is `j.hit`, where the page lets the yield through (the timing contract of `jumpPlan20`/`doHom` unchanged; `R21T.acid` is set per act from the plan, as `S26` does for Water).
- **The act** (`W58`, `ph58` is the one timeline both files read; `pose58` moves joints after the sneak's own rest pass; `face58` lays face, lean and pool over it through `W55`'s `z55` overlay):
  1. Leaving: a glance left and right, the claws rubbed before its chest; its pool draws in to a narrow lick under it (the pit `etch48` is held at home, `draw30` hook); it slides off leaning forward, claws back, flame tip streaming back; at the book's edge both claws reach over it and it hauls itself up (its height eased from bench to page); a fast slide on the paper, then the last 4.5 units on tiptoe in surges.
  2. At the molecule (it stands beside each atom on the atom's side, made smaller only if it would stand over the molecule's middle; between sides and to and from home it goes round the top of the molecule, behind it): rears up and looks it over; per atom: reach, fingers open, pinch, pull (the bond stretches, thins, greens at the atom's end and parts; the halves shrink back), held up beside its head and admired, a mouth far too wide (the face's O scaled up with a dark green inside), drop, snap shut, a fizz and a wisp of fume off the head, the atom seen dimly going down inside it (a pale ball drawn over the body, shrinking). Each further atom is quicker (first 1.5 s, second 1 s, the rest about one beat, quantised to beats) and greedier: the second hardly admired, the third and later the other claw shoves at the mouth too; the flame grows taller, the grin and teeth wider, the body a little fatter with each one.
  3. Home: licks its fingers, belches (a fume puff), turns its back, slides home seen from behind, flame tip bobbing, fatter; drops into its pit and settles.
  - Per stage: lengths times 0.6 (spark), 0.85, 1, 1.1, 1.25 (devourer, and its atoms take longer; stage 5's sleeve-pour dashes go on during the act); stage 5 strides with three surges instead of four.
- **The scene** (`S58`): an etched, bleached streak in the wood and a faint pale scorch on the paper are stamped along its way and fade (5.2 s, 3.2 s); pocks where the pluck fizzes; Water's wet trail and caustic are held off for it; its contact shadows keep their size though the pool is drawn in; no dust puffs.
- **Sound** (existing calls only, at the clip's moments; gulps are on beats): `drift`, `swish` and `pen` as it leaves; `tick` on the haul; `snap` (or `clink`) as an atom parts; `squish` then `feed` on each gulp; `squish` for the belch; `swish` and `pen` going home; `stamp` into the pit. The yield's `good` is the page's own at `j.hit`.
- **Stills** (bash, `.claude/r55`, ports 9891/9892; start with `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File startsrv.ps1 -Dir <r55 path>`, PIDs in `srv55.pids`): job `j_a58.js` (`at` = ms after the tap; `AA.rxKit.s58dbg()` gives the plan's times), `crop58.py` crops a grid. Strip: `NAME=s58 WSIZE=1920,1200 ./run.sh a58 "&stage=3&smi=OC1CC(O)CC(O)C1&at=800,1645,2185,4067,4867,5437,5677,6037,6412,7867,11200,12347" 380` then `python sheet55.py frames/s58 out.jpg 6 640`.
- **Clips for the helper** (one at a time, servers running; the triol page: dehydrate drives off three waters, so Acid chews 3 atoms):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/acid35/.claude/r55 && JOBN=a21 TMO=700 ./t.sh acid58_act "&hom=acid&part=act&stage=3&smi=OC1CC(O)CC(O)C1&lines=dehydrate&rack=dehydrate&hold=9000" 24 && python make4.py acid58-act.mp4 acid58_act`
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/acid35/.claude/r55 && JOBN=a21 TMO=800 ./t.sh acid58_act5 "&hom=acid&part=act&stage=5&smi=OC1CC(O)CC(O)C1&lines=dehydrate&rack=dehydrate&hold=11000" 24 && python make4.py acid58-act-5.mp4 acid58_act5`
- **Weak** (from the frames): at true game size it is small, so pinch, fraying bond, mouth and the swallowed atom are a few pixels; the eaten atoms reappear on the molecule as it leaves home (they had left on the fire); it faces the eye while it scurries round the top between far atoms; the held atom is a full-size ball; the paper scorch reads as faint dashes; the act is about 14 s at stage 3 with 3 atoms and longer at stage 5; stages 2 and 4 not looked at; sound not heard; nothing timed on a real graphics card.
## Fifty-seventh builder (15 November)

Branch `dryice-builder-49` (worktree `dryice49`; `steam` merged in first). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W57` directly after `W56`; one hook line in `W56`'s `man56`: while it acts the act sets its manner), `scheme.js` (block `S57` at the end of `start()`, just before `size(); newRun(); ...`). Nothing of Water's or Acid's blocks changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `dryice57-act-strip.jpg` (stage 3, twelve moments of one act in the full game view), `dryice57-act-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.act57` (`?a57=0` or `false` gives the old act: Water's clip on its body and the old `A20ACT.co2`). Only the shy one on the bench uses it (not the jar); Water's act is untouched (the wrappers pass every other figure, clip and creature straight on).
- **What the rules do and when it lands.** Dry ice's tap (live rules, `HOMS2`): on the last page, carbon dioxide made and three reagents never acted, the yield is multiplied by k once for each such reagent (three); NO atom changes. So the act touches one atom per shut reagent (stage 1: one darting touch). The touches fall on beats before the act's hit (the hit is put on a beat by `jumpPlan20`; gaps 2 then 1 beats, stage 5 2 then 1.5, halved when the beat is long); at each touch before the last the yield shown steps up by its share (`A20.hold.want`, geometric between the held yield and the final one), and the whole yield lands at the last touch (the act's own hit, as every act). Nothing in `path.js` is changed.
- **The clip** (`W57`, `play26` wrapped for `act`): Water's act is not run. It plays its own idle (`W56`: rolls, mist, wisps, frost go on moving) and lays the act over it: `C26.rest` is wrapped so the act's numbers (sink, turn, lean, bow, look, lids, squash) go in at each joint's own lag; `post57` then moves the block and hands (hug, and the reach to the atom: from the block to the atom's near side, at most `P57.rch` of its height), pours the rolls ahead and behind along the way, swells them at the molecule. Beats (`ph57`): gather (hugs the block, looks the way it will go, sinks till only head and eyes ride above the rolls); pour (`e` 0 to 1, rolls stretched along the way, peeping); rise out of the welling bank with a small overshoot; hold back, look at the atom, at you, a false start; the touches (block out to each atom, eyes squeezed at the touch, half out between touches; from stage 4 the hands cup the atom and the block glows in its chest); hug back; duck, turn its back, sink to the back of its drooping head, pour home, turn, settle. Lengths at pace 2 (`T57`, trip/work/home ms): 1 1100/2000/1500, 3 1900/4300/2400, 5 2700/5800/3400.
- **The page** (`S57`): `A20ACT.co2` is wrapped: atoms are the heavy one nearest home, then on along its bonds; it stands close by the first atom, behind it or to a side, on the paper and on as few atoms as it can (`?zb57=` how far, default 1.6 of its scale). In the scene (`s57Frame`, from `a20Spent`): the tide (soft fog planes lying on the bench or paper at their height, thick on the wood, banked at the book's edge, thin and grey-violet on the paper; laid as it moves, they spread and fade), rime marks on the wood and a faint cold damp on the paper (fading in 4 to 6 s; Water's wet marks are swallowed via `S33W.f`, Water's damp ring at home hidden while it acts), the bank welling round the atoms, the sigh falling from each touched atom, rime on the touched atom (white coat and frost needles that race over it, lighter on its neighbours, white on its bonds), melting after about 2 s to a little glossy dew and gone by 4.8 s (kept by the atom's place in the molecule, so a rebuilt molecule keeps it). In the page's ink (behind the atoms): frost ferns grow out on the paper from under each touched atom and fade; longer at stage 5. No dust puffs. Nothing on the bench moves.
- **Sound** (existing calls only): `drift` + `draw` riser as it pours out, `swish` at the book's edge, `draw` as the bank wells, at each touch `clink` (rising) + `pen` crackle, `good` at the hit (the act's own), `drift` as it turns, `draw` going home, `swish` as it settles.
- **Change of stage with the new figure**: not changed and not looked at here. It is still Water's `evo` played on the shy one's joints (builder 56's note: it morphs from WATER's old-stage skin). The act clears its own state when any other clip plays, so the evo runs as before.
- **Weak** (from the stills): see `dryice57-act-strip.txt`. Also: the stage choice of standing place is crude (on the test page the first atom is at the top of the page so it stands behind it with the O in its lap); fog on the bench is soft blurry planes (from the steep camera they read as cloud more than a creeping sheet); the sigh is hardly seen; stage 1 and 5 were looked at in stills only (they run, stage 5's bank floods the page top, stage 1 is a small roll that darts); the real tap was not played (only the test hook `jumpShow`, which has no yield change), so the stepped yield is not seen; sound not heard; no timing on a real graphics card (up to 90 fog planes and 40 marks, each a small transparent quad).
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r49` of this worktree, not in git): start the servers as builder 56 says (`python srv.py`, `python fsrv.py` there; ports 9791/9792), end them by PID. Stills: `NAME=a1 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh d57 "&part=act&stage=3&big=2" 400` (job `j_d57.js`; `at=ms,...` for other moments), then `python strip57.py a1 out.jpg 640` (optional crop `"x0,y0,x1,y1"` in 1280x720 units).
- **Clips for the helper** (one at a time, a few minutes each; `make4.py` is builder 55's, copied):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/dryice49/.claude/r49 && NAME=dryice57-act SHOW=300 WSIZE=2584,1532 timeout 900 ./run.sh d57 "&part=take&stage=3&secs=11" 880 && python make4.py dryice57-act.mp4 dryice57-act`
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/dryice49/.claude/r49 && NAME=dryice57-act-5 SHOW=300 WSIZE=2584,1532 timeout 1100 ./run.sh d57 "&part=take&stage=5&secs=14" 1080 && python make4.py dryice57-act-5.mp4 dryice57-act-5`

## Sixtieth builder (15 November)

Branch `acid-builder-35` (worktree `acid35`; `steam` merged in first). One file of code: `scheme.js`, block `S58` only (`kitart.js` untouched). Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `acid60-act-strip.jpg` (stage 3, twelve square crops of the act), `acid60-act-strip.txt`.

- **Size.** `s58Fig` measures its trunk, head and flame (not the pool's licks, not the arms) off its own joints as the camera sees them at home; `S58K` per stage is the height wanted as a share of the page (a box with some padding: 0.28 / 0.34 / 0.42 / 0.45 / 0.48). Seen: about a quarter of the page at stage 1, a third at stage 3 (about as tall as the triol), a little under a third at stage 5. It grows along the way out (`k` eased by distance, as before) and shrinks going home. `a20Fig`'s box was not its body (its `k` sat at the clamp 1.3), which is why it was small.
- **Where it stands.** Per atom, a grid search on that atom's page: its body box must keep clear of every atom (raised ones counted higher up the page), points on every bond, and the atoms it will eat and their bonds; nearest to the atom with its chest about level with it; head may rise past the page top over the bench; feet on the page. If any atom has no such place within reach (0.45 of its height), the scale steps down by 0.94 for all.
- **Trip.** Glance 450, bench slide 1000, haul 800, fast slide, creep 850 (ms, times the stage factor); home at least 1600. Stage 3 on the triol: leaves at about 1.2 s, at the first atom at 5.0 s; home 10.9 to 13.0 s. First atom 1100 ms (was 1500), second 900 (1000), lick 800 (950), pit 600 (700). Act about 13.6 s (was 13.4).
- **Eaten atoms.** The rules take them off with the fire and the act starts at the tap, so they cannot stay on without touching the rules: at the tap they come back as pencilled ghosts (paper-pale fill, a pencil ring from a back-face sphere, thin pale bond) and fill in solid as its claw reaches each one.
- **Record**: same as the fifty-eighth (job `j_a58.js`); strip: `NAME=s60e WSIZE=1920,1200 ./run.sh a58 "&stage=3&smi=OC1CC(O)CC(O)C1&at=200,1300,2100,2700,3300,3900,4500,5100,5500,5900,6200,6500,6900,7300,7700,8100,8600,9100,9500,9900,10500,11300,12100,12900" 480` then `python pick60.py frames/s60e out.jpg 2,3,4,6,9,10,11,14,17,19,21,23 6 400` (new `pick60.py` in `.claude/r55`, square crop holding home and the molecule).
- **Weak**: the ghost ring is thin and faint (reads as pale pink balls); on the bench it is still home size, so the first part of the trip is small; near the right page edge a claw pokes past the page; the wide mouth is hard to catch in a still; stage 5 is held a little smaller by the narrow strip left of the molecule and its broad sleeves may brush the atom it reaches for; stages 2 and 4, sound and a real graphics card not checked.

## Sixty-first builder (15 November)

Branch `slime-builder-61` (worktree `slime61`, from `steam`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W61`, the last block inside the creature closure, after `W57`). Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `slime61-stages.jpg` (the five Slime stages, Water's five below at the same scale), `slime61-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `slime61-silhouettes.jpg`, `slime61-stages.txt` (per-stage notes, animation ideas, what is wrong). Ruling BN: no approval round, one design carried through.

- **Switch.** `AA.rxKit.slime61 = 1` or `?s61=1`: OFF by default, the game shows the old Slime. The jar keeps the old Slime.
- **Built by Water's code** (`slime61Fig`), as builders 48/50 and 49/53 did: `SPEC61[0..4]` are numbers shaped like a stage of `S25`, put in Water's stage-3 slot (`S25[2]`, `W26.geo[3]`, `W26.ROLE[3]`, `W26.TARGET`) for the moment of building and put back; skin, joints, face, pen and wash are Water's; the figure carries `S.st` and `S.way61`. Skins are kept in `W61.geo`.
- **The five.** 1 the dollop (a squat blob, heavy lids, a thread off its top with a bead). 2 the slump (lopsided, a long slope, puzzled at its first stub of an arm). 3 the heap (the line-up's figure: low and wide, face in the mass, one arm lifted in an arch with strings back to the body). 4 the stretch (pulled up tall and leaning, both arms out with curtains of strings, a yawn). 5 the mire (a vast mound, two raised arms with strings, one fallen forward, blisters).
- **Slime's own parts.** Limb points are given where they ARE (`lim61`, `at61`). `arm61`: an arm, plus one limb for every string (`hang61`: from a point on the arm down into the body) or drip (`drip61`: a thread ending in a bead); each string runs as a hair INSIDE the arm (radius 0.07) and leaves it at its point, so it is one skin and the body is not swollen where chains lie together. Slopes are fat limbs running out low along the bench. `blis61`: blisters. `ring61`: pen bubbles on the skin (keep them off steep skin and away from the eyes: they smear, or read as extra eyes). Lopsided eyes (`o.eyes`). `tint61`: the four blues of `F30` changed for dusky violet-pink (`W61.C`), more bare paper on the lit side but none low down, and the margin (the pushed-out skin) takes the wash low down: without that the lit margin of the slick showed as a white band under the belly. In the `draw30` wrapper `G.pl` and the chest are hidden from the pen (no pool ripples, no flow lines), and the strokes are written by `ink48` with `S.a48` lent for that call only (`S.a61` holds them), so Acid's idle and act, which key on `S.a48`, never run on Slime.
- **Learned.** `ROLE.head` is the low belly joint (index 2) at stages 1, 2, 3, 5: there is no neck. A fold still shows across the heap where the skin creases in Water's rest pose; at stage 3 the face was moved up so it lies under the mouth. Tongues of slick (`ooze61`, kept, not used) drew as claws. A slick whose front edge is close to the foot gets a ragged spiky front rim: the slick is deeper in front (`pool(x, z, R, Rb, ...)` with Rb about 1.3 times the foot). More than about 9 thousand vertices made that rim worse. Sizes come from `homStand` (a height for each stage over `G.H`), so a wide low figure comes out very wide.
- **Weak** (from the sheets): silhouettes 4 and 5 are close (two raised arms with strings); stage 4 has its face on top of a tall lump and can read as a cartoon ghost; the fold line at stage 3 is a rough dashed line; the lids are flat pale ovals; faint ghost ovals where an arm passes in front of the body; stage 1's face is small at game size; stage 5 overlaps the top edge of the notebook; the slick is a plain oval; thin strings may break up on a phone; the rest motion is Water's (do not fire Slime with the switch on: the act would be Water's); about 8 to 9 thousand vertices and 14 to 120 joints a figure, no timing taken.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r61` of this worktree, untracked, copied from `r49`; ports 9991/9992; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st61.sh [stages] [rows]` (rows `s` and `w`) runs job `j_s61.js` with `&s61=1&hom=slime&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/sN` and `&hom=water&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/wN` (`f00003` the game view, `f00004` the silhouette), about 40 s a figure; `python sheet61.py` writes the three sheets; `./all.sh [stages]` renders and makes a quick contact sheet `c_all.jpg`. The block source is `.claude/r61/block61.js`; `python .claude/r61/apply.py` puts it into `kitart.js`.

## Fifty-ninth builder (15 November)

Branch `dryice-builder-49` (worktree `dryice49`; `steam` merged in first). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W59` directly after `W57`; two small hooks: in `W49`'s `tint49` the wash takes a uniform `v59` so it can sublime away from the edges in or be cut below a height, and in `W56`'s `man56` the manner is held while `S.x59` is live), `scheme.js` (block `S59` directly after `S57`, near the end of `start()`). Nothing of Water's (`W24`..`W36`, `S26`..`S36`) or Acid's (`W48`, `W52`, `W55`, `W58`, `S52`, `S55`, `S58`) blocks changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `dryice59-evolve-strip.jpg` (four rows, 1 to 2 ... 4 to 5; six moments each in the full game view: welled up hugging the block, fog over the page with frost ferns, the whiteout held, the dome drawn in, the new form at its tallest, home and settled), `dryice59-evolve-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.evo59` (`?v59=0` or `false` gives the old change of stage: Water's `evo` played on the shy one's joints; checked, it still runs). Only the shy one on the bench uses it (`S.way49`, not the jar); every other figure and clip passes straight on, so Water's and Acid's changes of stage are untouched.
- **What exists: "The whiteout".** `play26` is wrapped for Dry ice's `evo` clip; Water's `evo` is not run. The timeline is in beats of the music (`PAR59`, `ph59`): go, well, gather, whiteout (its last part held), reveal, home; 1 to 2 is 8.75 beats, 2 to 3 10.5, 3 to 4 11.75, 4 to 5 14.75 (4.4 to 7.4 s at pace 2). The hit (the start of the reveal) falls on a whole beat. The figure keeps its own idle (`W56`: rolls, breath, mist) underneath; `face59` lays each beat's face and body over it (a `C26.rest` wrapper), `post59` moves hands and block, `vis59` decides what of it is seen.
  1. **Go** (`S59`; `s59Place`/`s59Fit` as `S55`'s): the OLD stage's figure (built once by `dry49Fig`, beside the new one) sinks into its bank and pours from home over the open book to the place worked out once (the new form about half the screen tall, its middle a little above the screen's, beside the molecule by the bank's half-width; if the bank cannot lie between the screen's edge and the molecule the form is made smaller, at most to 0.78; while it stands there, a bank leaving a side of the screen slides it over rather than shrinking it, which can put the bank in front of the molecule's edge). It grows on the way, laying S57's tide of fog (and rime on the wood only) behind it, then wells up large, peeps at you (wide eyes) and shuts its eyes, hugging the block.
  2. **Gathering**: eyes shut, bowed and curled round the block; the block glows cold blue (its emissive, a blue light over it, a pool of cold light on the page); 3 to 4 the block slides from its hands into its chest. The bank pours out every way (`stage59`): 44 flat fog pieces lying on the paper (thin, grey-violet, the ruled lines show), 28 rolling billows on the paper at the front and in the middle (each with its shade on the paper and a pen curl), kept on the book and out of the molecule. Frost ferns (pen, lying in the paper, `ink59`) race out from under it: stems with barbs, 8 to 16 of them, 1.5 to 3 R long. The figure sublimes from its edges in (`v59.x` in the wash, the pen outline breaking in noise, its own strokes and wisps off) till only the glowing block and the shut eyes are left in the fog.
  3. **Whiteout**: the fog rises into a dome of 22 billows over the middle (drawn after the page, so it hides the centre; pen curls on its upper puffs); the room dims (builder 33's one-lamp pass driven from `S59` as `S55` drives it; its shadow of the form is weighted by how much of the form is there, so a sublimed body throws none) and is cooled by a multiply overlay; held: 36 motes fall slowly (every third a small pen star), the block's blue light swells once (1 to 2) or twice.
  4. **Reveal**: on the hit a flash, seven jagged pen cracks out of the block, a ring of frost light and a broken pen ring running out over the page. The old body is removed and the new one put in place on that frame, both wholly hidden (old fully sublimed, new fully cut, the dome over them). All the fog rushes back to the middle and the dome is drawn in; the new stage condenses top down (`v59.y`, a cut that sinks from above its head to below its bank; the dome's puffs go top first with it), the last fog pours off its shoulders (8 billows). It opens its eyes wide, looks down at its hands (raised a little), then shyly at you, and glances aside under half lids. Per step: 1 to 2 small and quick, the huddle sits up hugging its block; 2 to 3 the shy one holds the block out; 3 to 4 the block went into the chest in the gathering and the keeper stretches up taller as the block's glow rises; 4 to 5 the widest fog (over the whole notebook), the longest hush, a slower condensing, the old frost bowed (head lifting a little at the end), ferns across both pages that melt last.
  5. **Home**: the ferns melt from the tips in and leave beads of dew that fade (gone by 0.5 to 0.7 of the way home; the page is clean after, checked in the last frame of each row); it ducks, turns its back, sinks and pours home seen from behind, turns and settles; the new number is written beside it (`HEVO.num`, as Acid's).
  - **Sound** (existing calls only, on the beats): `drift` (the base) and `swish` as it leaves, `draw` as it wells, a `draw` riser and a soft `pen` crackle through the gathering (the ferns), `swish` into the hush, `flagSoft` at each pulse, `clink` (with its low tone) and `swish` at the crack; the base plays `stamp` as the form stands, `feed` breaths as it opens its eyes and looks, `good` and `stamp` as it settles; `drift` and `draw` going home.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r49` of this worktree, not in git; ports 9791/9792; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID; `my59.pids` holds the last ones). Stills: `./e59.sh <name> <stage it leaves> "<beats,...>" "<ms after home>"` (job `j_e59.js`, sheet `t59/<name>.jpg`; `AA.rxKit.s59dbg()` gives the beat `u`); the strip: `./all59.sh <prefix>`, which writes `t59/<prefix>-strip.jpg` (`strip59.py`).
- **Clips for the helper** (one at a time, servers running; each take a few minutes; `t.sh` with builder 21's job `j_a21.js`, `make4.py` builder 55's):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/dryice49/.claude/r49 && for s in 1 2 3 4; do JOBN=a21 TMO=900 ./t.sh ev59_$s "&hom=co2&part=evo&stage=$s&lead=1000&hold=10000" 24; done`
  - each step alone: `python make4.py dryice59-evolve-<s>.mp4 ev59_<s>`
  - all four back to back with number cards and sound: `python make4.py dryice59-evolve-all.mp4 ev59_1="1 to 2" ev59_2="2 to 3" ev59_3="3 to 4" ev59_4="4 to 5"`
- **Weak**: see `dryice59-evolve-strip.txt`. Also: the dome is a heap of round billows (it reads as cloud or cotton more than one soft dome); the fog pieces are flat cards on the paper and camera-facing cards (rolls, dome), not modelled volume; rime does not creep up the page's printed lines (only the ferns and the fog lie in the paper); with a wide bank (stages 4 and 5) the new form can stand with its bank in front of the molecule's left atoms; the old stage 1 figure, scaled for the new form, is a very large blob as it wells up; faint marks of the game's own (a grey bar, a pale ring) were seen on the page afterwards, with the switch off too; looked at in stills only, sound not heard, no timing on a real graphics card (up to about 150 small transparent cards, plus the lamp pass's two extra scene draws).

## Sixty-third builder (15 November)

Branch `salt-builder-63` (worktree `salt63`, from `steam`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W63`, the last block inside the creature closure, after `W61`). Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `salt63-stages.jpg` (the five Salt stages, Water's five below at the same scale), `salt63-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `salt63-silhouettes.jpg`, `salt63-stages.txt` (per-stage notes, animation ideas, what is wrong). Ruling BN: no approval round, one design carried through. The design is the line-up's B, "the hopper" (ruling BM).

- **Switch.** `AA.rxKit.salt63 = 1` or `?t63=1`: OFF by default, the game shows the old Salt. The jar keeps the old Salt.
- **Water's code, a body of its own.** Face, joints, pose, pen and wash are Water's (`fig26`, `play26`, `set30`, `draw30`), reached as builders 48 to 61 did, through Water's stage-3 slot. But the skin is NOT made by `geo26`: `geo63` builds the body out of BOXES (`SPEC63[0..4]` list them) and hands `fig26` a finished skin (`geo26` returns a skin already in `W26.geo[3]`), with every field Water's code asks of one (`J`, `bj`, `R`, `chains`, `reach`, `hairJ`, `pl`, `f`, `grad`, `skin`, `H`). Each box is carried whole by ONE joint (weight 1), so the figure bends only at joints. Boxes stand in one another; a step sits exactly on the one below so its foot edge is drawn. Skins are kept in `W63.geo`.
- **Helpers.** `mk63()` gives `box` (centre, half sizes, yaw, roll, pitch), `step` (a slab standing on a height), `bud` (a small crystal on a bigger one, in line with it, placed in the parent's half sizes), `arm` (jointed prisms and a cube fist; also makes the limb's joints), `grains`. `face63` puts the face on a box's front face; `salt63Fig` then rolls the face parts with that box (their `p0`/`q0` are rewritten). `glint63`: a pen star in the air. `sq63`/`on63`: pen on a face of a box (kept, not used now). A box is bound by `g` (the ground joint), `jb` (index in the spine), or `li` (a limb's joints, nearest to `at`).
- **Flat tones.** `tint63`: the body material is flat-shaded, so each face has one light; the four blues of `F30` become near-white and cold grey-blue (`W63.C`); the noise in where tones change is nearly removed; `W63.t` = the light at which a face is bare paper, at which the mid tone starts, at which shade starts. The lamp's warmth is taken off, the pencil made faint. The light comes from high: a face tipped a little down goes grey.
- **Pen.** `sil30` is wrapped for Salt's body (`pen30.s63`): the outline with one smoothing and a small wobble, then every box edge whose two faces are both seen as one straight stroke (edges are found once from the rest mesh, kept in `s.k63`). Hidden lines are cut by depth. In the `draw30` wrapper `G.pl` and the chest are hidden from the pen (no ripples, no flow lines); own strokes go through `ink48` with `S.a48` lent (`S.a63`). Water's three drips are switched off (`S.drips` emptied).
- **The five.** 1 the grain (one cube tipped on an edge, wide eyes, a bud). 2 the seed (a cube on a step, a cube head cocked, one stub arm, one brow up). 3 the hopper (stepped skirt, fists on hips, big tilted head, chin up). 4 the tower (four steps, square shoulders, one arm up with a finger of crystal, a frown, buds on the crown). 5 the cathedral (broad steps, a pinnacle each side, arms folded, a crown of cubes with a spire, eyes nearly shut).
- **Learned.** The bench is seen from high up: the tops of the steps are the biggest planes and the skirt reads as stacked slabs; thin steps looked like a tablet, so risers are tall (0.5 to 0.6). `homStand` sizes by `G.H`, the highest JOINT: the top joint is set below the real top (crown, spire, finger) so the figure stands as large as Water's. Edge's `--dump-dom` does not work for a page check here (it returns nothing); the harness page `vid.html` loads the same scripts in the same order as `scheme.html`.
- **Weak** (from the sheets): it still reads partly as a robot or block-game figure, most at stages 2 and 4; the skirt reads as slabs from above; the face lies on a grey face at 4 and 5; nothing says the crystal is clear; silhouettes 2 to 4 share a square base; outline and edge strokes double in places; boxes will slide through each other when joints move far (not tested in motion); the rest motion is Water's (do not fire Salt with the switch on: the act would be Water's). 96 to 1,624 vertices and 6 to 15 joints a figure, no timing taken.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r63` of this worktree, untracked, copied from `r61`; ports 10091/10092; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): the block source is `block63.js`, its stage list `spec63.js`; `./go.sh "1 2 3 4 5"` splices the list in (`splice.py`), puts the block into `kitart.js` (`apply.py`), renders (`st63.sh [stages] [rows]`, rows `s` and `w`: job `j_s63.js` with `&t63=1&hom=salt&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/sN`, `&hom=water&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/wN`; `f00003` the game view, `f00004` the silhouette; about 45 s a figure) and writes a quick sheet `look.jpg`; `python sheet63.py` writes the three sheets.

## Sixty-second builder (15 November)

Branch `slime-builder-61` (worktree `slime61`; `steam` merged in first, a fast-forward; the page started clean with and without `?s61=1`, no script error). File: `kitart.js` only (block `W62` directly after `W61`; `W61` untouched). No `scheme.js` change. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `slime62-idle-strip.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5: one game view, then six moments over 5 s as square crops round the home spot from the 1920-wide view, enlarged 2x), `slime62-jar.jpg` (the shelf, and the jar close at stages 1, 3, 5), `slime62-idle-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `W62` sets `AA.rxKit.slime61 = 1` unless the page has `?s61=0` (gives the old Slime, bench and jar). `AA.rxKit.idle62 = false` (or `?i62=0`) keeps the spirit but plays Water's rest on it. Bench and jar work at all five stages, nothing throws.
- **Its own idle, not Water's** (builder 56's method): `C26.rest` is wrapped (`rest62` fills only the face and the slick: lids, blinks, the yawn, looks, `px`/`pz`); `play26` is wrapped so `pose62` moves the joints after the whole play chain; time is the page clock (`clk62`, 0.55 in the jar, amplitudes 0.6). Keyed on `S.way61` only: Acid's idle (`S.a48`, which Slime only lends inside `W61`'s draw) and Dry ice's (`S.way49`) never start on it, and `W62` passes every other figure straight on.
  - All: the body joints sag down, spread and plump back up past their height (`sag62`), and every knock (settling, the jerk awake, stage 1's little bumps, stage 2's flop, a snap) sends a damped wobble through it (`wob62`), each joint reading the clock later the higher it is (the top lags the base, like jelly). Limbs ride the joint they grow from; slopes stay on the bench and spread as it sags. The slick creeps out and draws back. Lids droop, shut and jerk open (eyes wide, brows up, a wobble; the face's `lid` is lowered for the moment and put back when another clip plays); a slow yawn (the O mouth). Pen bubbles (`ink62`) rise up the front of the skin on lanes found once by marching the rest skin, grow, and pop in four arcs with a small dimple stroke. Water's drips are hidden.
  - Strings (`str62`): rebuilt every frame between their arm joint and the place they land, joints turned to lie along them; slack they belly outward and swing, pulled long they thin. Once an arm cycle the next string thins, snaps at the top of the haul and whips down into the body (its free end and every joint of its hair inside the arm go to nothing, so no sliver of skin is left), stays gone 0.6 s, then pulls up out of the body to the arm and thickens. (The brief had the strings thin on the droop; with strings that land below the arm a droop can only slacken them, so they thin on the haul.)
  - Drips: the bead gathers under the hand, stretches on its thread and drops; the skin bead goes to nothing the moment a scene bead (violet toon ball with an ink rim) takes its place and falls; it leaves a glossy violet spot on the figure's floor that shrinks and is drawn back into the slick (when it lands inside the slick the spot is hidden by it). At stages 2 and 3 the drop is timed to the arm being held up.
  - 1 jiggles at every little thing (a bump every 2.3 s), nods off (bows, lids down) and jerks awake; the thread on top swings after the body. 2 lifts its stub of an arm, eyes on it, turns the hand, lets it flop with a bounce that shakes the lump. 3 the arched arm droops (strings slack), hauls up past rest (strings thin, one snaps, a new one pulls out). 4 sways tall like taffy, sinks and pulls itself up, both arms and their string curtains swing out of step. 5 a deep slow swell, the two raised arms lift and fall out of step, blisters swell, five bubble lanes.
- **Bench fit.** At home the figure stands a little further back for the wide stages (`KIT.build` wrapper: root z -0.3/-0.6/-0.7/-0.9 for stages 2 to 5), so the slick keeps clear of the notebook's top edge (checked with `benchBoxes`: no notebook overlap at any stage; stage 5's lip nearly touches the edge).
- **Jar** (`jar62`, builder 56's way): the half-grown, ready and stage forms are the new Slime fitted to the glass and sat up on the jar's level, each playing its rest from its own `onBeforeRender` (`drive62`); no beads drop in the jar. The `hull27` width fix of builders 52/56 is applied (it threw in the jar). `JLIFE.slime` (scheme.js) still squashes the ready figure's group a little, which reads as part of its sag.
- **Cost** (headless software rendering, 1304 x 812, stage 3 at home, 30 frames each, very noisy on this machine): `pose62` 0.10 ms a frame. Frames 450 ms with the new idle (582 in a second pass), 285 with Water's rest on the spirit, 526 for the old Slime: the same within this run's noise; the pen bubbles add a few short strokes.
- **Act and change of stage with the new figure** (called directly with `rigPlay`, not fired in a run; nothing throws): the act is Water's `act` clip played on the spirit's skin (sinks flat, flows, rises, reaches with an arm, turns its back, flows home); strings and drips go with Water's moves unrebuilt (Water's pins hold their tips), so expect them to stretch oddly during the reach. During it the idle, beads and spots stop and the idle returns after. The change of stage is Water's `evo`: it morphs from WATER's skin of the old stage (`geo26(T, from)` builds Water's numbers), so the first part shows the spirit's joints on Water's old shape. Both need Slime's own clips next.
- **Weak** (from the frames): stage 1 moves least; slack strings fold into crumpled pen loops on the droop; the snap and the bead are quick and small at game size; the spot is mostly hidden in the slick; the faint ghost oval at the arm root is still there (not fixed); the static `ring61` pen bubbles stay put beside the moving ones; in the jar stage 1 floats a little above the level ring and all are small; stages 2 and 4 were looked at in a grid only; no real graphics card timing.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r61` of this worktree, untracked; ports 9991/9992; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID). Job `j_s62.js`, sheet `sheet62.py`, grid `grid62.py`. Strip: `NAME=s62c WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh s62 "&part=strip&stages=1,3,5&k=6&span=5000" 880` then `python sheet62.py frames/s62c out.jpg 1,3,5 6 "865,50,210;786,0,360;747,0,440" 2`; jar: `NAME=j62 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh s62 "&part=jar&stages=1,3,5" 480`; cost: `NAME=k62 ./run.sh s62 "&part=cost&stage=3" 480`; act/evo probe: `NAME=c62 ./run.sh s62 "&part=clips&stage=3" 480`; a snap or a bead drop close up: `NAME=e62 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh s62 "&part=ev&stage=3&what=snap&nf=16&dt=90" 480` (or `what=bead`). Block source `.claude/r61/block62.js` (already in `kitart.js`). Clips (script `t62.sh`, copied from builder 56's `t56.sh`; 30 frames a second, 2560 capture scaled to 1920, mp4 into the shots folder; the servers must be running):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/slime61/.claude/r61 && ./t62.sh slime62-idle 3 12` (12 s at rest, stage 3)
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/slime61/.claude/r61 && ./t62.sh slime62-idle-5 5 10` (10 s at rest, stage 5)

## Sixty-fourth builder (15 November)

Branch `seedling-builder-64` (worktree `seedling64`, from `steam`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W64`, the last block inside the creature closure, after `W61`: it needs Water's code and builder 48's `ink48`). Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `seedling64-stages.jpg` (the five Seedling stages, Water's five below at the same scale), `seedling64-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `seedling64-silhouettes.jpg`, `seedling64-stages.txt` (per-stage notes, animation ideas, what is wrong). Ruling BN: no approval round, one design carried through.

- **Switch.** `AA.rxKit.seed64 = 1` or `?g64=1`: OFF by default, the game shows the old Seedling (the pot). The jar keeps the old Seedling. The page starts with no script error with the switch on and off (job `j_ok64.js`).
- **Built by Water's code** (`seed64Fig`), as 48/50, 49/53 and 61 did: `SPEC64[0..4]` go in Water's stage-3 slot (`S25[2]`, `W26.geo[3]`, `W26.ROLE[3]`, `W26.TARGET`) for the moment of building and are put back; skin, joints, face, pen and wash are Water's; the figure carries `S.st` and `S.way64`. Skins are kept in `W64.geo`.
- **The five.** 1 the seed (tipped over, cracked round the middle, eyes in the crack, one root toe). 2 the hook (bent stem on two root feet, bud bowed and wearing the top of the seed coat as a cap, squinting). 3 the sprout (the line-up's figure: hooked stem, nodding bud head, one leaf arm up, one out). 4 the sapling (upright, bud opening, four leaves, one raised to its brow, a tendril). 5 the bloom (seven petals round the face, two great leaves like open arms, roots spread).
- **Seedling's own parts.** NO pool: `pad64` shrinks Water's pool to a pad under the tap root, and the wash sets `pl = 0.0` so nothing is treated as pool. The spine itself makes the hook: its keys go up, over and DOWN into the bud (keys need not rise). `feet64`, `back64`, `root64`: roots are limbs that end on the bench (pinned by Water's code, end below 0.55). `leaf64`: a limb with `flat: [0, 0, 1]` (flattened toward the eye) and a leaf's widths (`leafR64`); `vein64` its midrib and side veins in pen. `husk64`: two points of split coat at the seed's rim. `tend64`: a tendril (radius 0.078, an unbroken line). `zig64`: the broken edge of the coat, a zigzag pen line. `hair64`: root hairs, pen lines in the air. Petals are flat limbs round the head joint. Soil crumbs are Water's `drops`.
- **Three washes on one skin.** The fourth number of `w25a` (Water's foam, which the pen-and-wash shader only read for the pool) says what a place is made of: body by `o.foam(kp, th, y)`, a limb by `l.foam`: 0 seed, root, young stem (tan-brown), 0.5 leaf and bud (leaf green), 1 petal (pink). `tint64` swaps each of the four blues of `F30` for a mix of the three by that number (`g64`, `p64`); colours in `W64.C` (`b`, `g`, `p`). So the cap of stage 2 and the crack of stage 1 are only a colour by height on the same skin.
- **Learned.** The game sizes a figure by its height alone (`homStand`: a height for each stage over `G.H`), so a thin tall creature comes out small: shorten the stem and every part is bigger (done once; heads are 35 to 45 percent of the height). Leaving `TARGET` at Water's numbers gave 14 thousand vertices (flat limbs are counted as round): `W64.TARGET` is about 5 to 7 thousand for about 10 thousand real ones. A leaf that passes close to the head merges with it and hides the head's outline: keep a clear gap. Pen strokes laid by rays start at `c + d * 0.45 * G.H`: on a low figure give `c` a z in front of the body (`pen64` uses 1.2). The `s30` error in the job's log ("Cannot set properties of undefined (setting 'value')", line 1753) is older than this block: Slime's log has it too.
- **Weak** (from the sheets; more in `seedling64-stages.txt`): thin, so less presence than Water at stages 4 and 5; stage 4's sepals can read as horns and its raised leaf as a loop; stage 1 reads as an egg with a band and its crack does not open; stage 5 is crowded low down; lids a flat light green; root hairs and crumbs lost at game size; the rest pose is Water's (do not fire it with the switch on: the act would be Water's); about 10 thousand vertices and 15 to 124 joints a figure, no timing taken.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r64` of this worktree, untracked, scripts copied from `r61`; ports 10191/10192; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st64.sh [stages] [rows]` (rows `s` and `w`) runs job `j_s64.js` with `&g64=1&hom=seedling&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/sN` and `&hom=water&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/wN` (`f00003` the game view, `f00004` the silhouette), about 55 s a figure; `python sheet64.py` writes the three sheets. The start check: `NAME=ok_on ./run.sh ok64 "&g64=1" 150` and `NAME=ok_off ./run.sh ok64 "" 150` (`errs` must be empty). The block source is `.claude/r64/block64.js`; `python .claude/r64/apply.py` puts it into `kitart.js`.

## Sixty-fifth builder (15 November)

Branch `salt-builder-63` (worktree `salt63`), `steam` merged in first. File: `kitart.js`, block `W63` only. Stills only. Salt REDONE: builder 63's figure (a box head on a stepped box body, jointed box arms) was judged "a cardboard-box robot" and is gone. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `salt65-stages.jpg` (five stages, Water's five rendered again below at the same scale), `salt65-compare.jpg` (stage 3 before and after), `salt65-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `salt65-silhouettes.jpg`, `salt65-stages.txt` (what it shows, animation ideas, what is wrong).

- **Switch.** Unchanged: `AA.rxKit.salt63 = 1` or `?t63=1`, OFF by default; the jar keeps the old Salt.
- **The rule.** ONE main cube is the whole body AND the face (`m.main`: tipped, set down on its lowest corner by `rest63`; carried by spine joint 2, the "head"). It grows as salt grows: `m.twin` puts a smaller cube THROUGH it at its own turn, roll and tip (`m.bud` still grows one in line). No torso, shoulders, hips, fists, slabs: `step` and `arm` are removed. `fin63` makes the five spine joints from the main cube; its `H` (the top joint) is what `homStand` sizes by, set by hand per stage.
- **Shards.** `loc63` narrows a box along its own length when it has `tp` (0.93: to a point). `m.shard(par, side, root, tip, w)` is a hand: a limb of three joints, the whole prism carried by its root joint (it tilts there). `m.spike(b, from, to, w)` is a shard only grown on (stage 5's halo).
- **Crystal look.** Tones in `W63.C` (paper, pale cold grey-blue, grey-violet); a stage may carry its own tone limits `t` (stage 4 does: its front face went grey under the lamp). Inner edges are written with a finer pen (`W63.inner` = 0.5 of the outline's). `thru63` draws three faint far edges on the front face; `hop63` a stepped hopper hollow on a face (squares within a square); `glint63` is now an outlined four-point star. `m.bed` and `m.ring` lay small tipped cubes on the wood; `crust63` adds a pale flat patch under the figure (a Lambert material on purpose: a white basic material is taken for a "glint" by builder 29's style swap and painted solid).
- **The five.** 1 the grain (cube on its corner, big eyes, a bud). 2 the twin (a cube through its top corner like a hat askew, a first shard, one brow up). 3 the cluster (crest of cubes, props itself on two planted shards, heavy lids). 4 the crown (a leaning spire of cubes off one upper corner, one shard raised, a frown). 5 the druse (a great cube on a bed of small crystals, a crown of cubes, seven shards fanned behind like a halo, eyes nearly shut).
- **Learned.** The bench figure is seen almost from the FRONT (the holder is tipped back 0.8 rad): top faces are slivers, the ground is squeezed to about 0.4 in depth, things at z over about 2.7 / scale fall off the bench edge. So a crest must rise ABOVE the top edge to be seen, hopper hollows go on the side face, and anything hung under the cube reads as legs (the first pass, with long shards under a lifted cube, was a box on stilts). A figure taller than about 4.3 units at stage 4's scale leaves the top of the screen.
- **Weak** (from the sheets): faces still read as white card as much as rock salt (opaque, the seen-through lines too faint at game size); stage 3 on its two shards can read as a box on two legs; stage 4's spire is a clump; the crest of 3 sits mostly behind the cube; lids are paler than the face; outline and edge strokes double, bed cubes go to black dots at phone size; the crust is only a faint streak; Salt 1 and 2 stand smaller than Water 1 and 2; not tried in motion. Water's code logs an old error on Salt figures (`kitart.js` about line 1753, "Cannot set properties of undefined (setting 'value')", in `s30dbg().err`); it was there in builder 63's renders and does not stop the page. Page checked with the switch on and off through the kit's `vid.html`: no script error either way.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r63`, ports 10091/10092, `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): as builder 63 (`block63.js`, `spec63.js`, `./go.sh "1 2 3 4 5"`, `./st63.sh "1 2 3 4 5" w` for Water's row); `python sheet65.py` writes the four sheets. `block63.v1.bak` and `spec63.v1.bak` are builder 63's sources.
