## Seventy-seventh builder (15 November)

Branch `brimstone-builder-69` (worktree `brim69`; `steam` merged in first; the merged page started with no script error through the kit's harness, `part=ok`). Files: `kitart.js` (new block `W77` directly after `W73`'s end; `W69`/`W73` untouched: `W77` wraps `play26`, `C26.rest` and `brk69` from outside), `scheme.js` (new block `S77` directly after `S72`'s end). No other creature's block changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `brimstone77-act-strip.jpg` (stage 3, twelve square crops of one act holding home and the molecule), `brimstone77-act-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.act77` (`?a77=0` or `false` gives the old act: Water's clip on the smoke and the old `A20ACT.brimstone`). Only the bench Brimstone of `W69` (`S.way69`, not the jar) with its idle on (`idle73`) uses it; every other figure, clip and creature passes straight on.
- **What the rule counts and when it lands** (live `HOMS2`, `RULE.homCount`, `homCountN`): lit when sulfur went on the molecule this page (thiolene acted, or a cap); the tap multiplies the yield by the stage's number for the first sulfur ON THE MOLECULE and by 1.12 for each sulfur more (`g.hom.count` = every S atom; with the sigil Sulphur's Mask every OH too), sets `up.end` (the round ends now, the task counts as met) and `burnt`, so `roundEnd` writes `run.homSpent.brimstone` and `P.homSpent(g)` says spent for the rest of the run. No atom changes. `S77` touches exactly those atoms (`s77Atoms`: each S; each OH while the Mask is held), left to right. Touches fall on half beats: the first 2.5 beats after it stands, then gaps of 2, 1.5, 1, then half a beat. The held instant is one beat after the last touch, the hit two beats after that: the act's own `j.hit`, where the page lets the yield through (`jumpPlan20` puts it on a beat; `R21T.brimstone` is set per act from the plan, as `S72` does). The round then ends by the page's own flow (`fireNext` after `j.rest`). Nothing in `path.js` changed.
- **The act** (`W77` body, `S77` place and scene; `KIT.ph77` is the one timeline both read; `o.bites.s77` carries the plan). The group stays at home at home size (the LUMP and its flames stay there); every bone above the lump (`fig77`: the smoke) is moved by one transform about the foot of the smoke (base, size `k`, squash `sy`, lean along the way, a turn, a bow); the skin between the lump's top and the foot of the smoke stretches into the thread (its pen line hidden while away, `brk69` wrapped). Stage 3 on the test page (beat 500 ms): wake 1.5 s, pour 3.5 s, rise 1.5 s, three touches 1.25 / 2.25 / 3 s after it stands, hit 4.5 s after it stands, home about 5 s later, settled 18.7 s after the tap.
  1. Wake: the lump's flames sink to a fifth, the coal eyes open wide and blaze, brows down, it leans toward the molecule; the room dims (builder 33's one-lamp pass, `s77Lamp`, a copy of `s71Lamp` with its own state, at 0.55).
  2. Pour: sinks to a low bank (`sy` 0.3), pours off the lump along the bench by the book's top edge (`s58Line` through a point on the bench), over the edge onto the page, growing as it comes toward the eye; dark smoke rolls low off it (soft sprites, one in five with a pen edge) and leaves scorch in the wood and fainter on the paper (fading in 7.5 / 4.8 s); a broad soft shade under it.
  3. Rise: behind the molecule (`s77Plan`: its foot just behind the heavy atoms, its bowed head toward the molecule, its size fitted so its whole box stays inside the screen; asked 0.4 / 0.5 / 0.6 / 0.63 / 0.66 of the page), up to its height with a small overshoot.
  4. Count: lids half down, it looks at each atom; its longest arm (stage 2: its outermost billow) is laid on a curve from the shoulder to the atom (`arm77`; fingers turned along the reach; the arm thickens as it stretches) and touches; the atom catches a blue flame that stays (taller for each further atom: the crown) with a red ember in it, blue firelight on the paper (`s77PageMat`), a low note.
  5. Burn: drawn up to its full height, eyes blazing, both hands close round the burning atoms; on the hit the flames flare and a ring of blue fire runs out across the page in the paper's plane (a scene plane on the page, so creature and atoms stand over it), char behind it, the page left edged with a faint scorch that fades; then the fire gutters to embers and smoke.
  6. Home: turns its back (the back of the head, no face), drawn back along the way sinking and shrinking, re-forms on the lump small and low (`SP77`), flames guttered, cracks dull, coals dim.
  - Spent: while `P.homSpent(g)` (after its tap, and for the rest of the run) `S77` sets `SP.spent77` and `W77` shows the spent shape at rest (`spent77`: smoke sunk to 0.6 high, flames at 0.12, light 0.15, cracks and coals dim, lids half down, one puff).
  - Per stage: 1 the ember never leaves its lump: its wisp bones are laid on a curve to each atom with a curled tip, thickened; a small ring runs out; 3 is the tuned reference; 5 is slowest (x1.25), and when it draws itself up the fire climbs every heavy atom of the molecule.
- **Sound** (existing `sfx` calls only, on half beats): `handoff` + low `drone` as it wakes, `drone` + `swish` as it pours, `swish` at the book's edge, `draw` as it rises, at each touch `reflex('weld', n)` + a `drone` a step higher each time, `draw` through the held instant, at the hit `boom` + `strike(2)` + `swish` (the page's own `good` too), `swish` turning, a low `drone` going home, `snap` as it settles.
- **No yellow**: nothing the act draws is yellow (blue-violet fire, red-orange embers and eyes, smoke grey, brown-black scorch). The S atoms keep the game's own sulfur colour; the evolution's bursts still use `JARC.brimstone` (0xc9a227): not part of the act, left as found.
- **Change of stage with the new figure**: not touched and not looked at. Still Water's `evo` morphing from Water's old-stage skin (builder 73's note); listed, not fixed.
- **Stills** (bash, `.claude/r69`; ports 10291/10292; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID, `my77.pids`): sources `block77.js`, `s77.js`, put in by `python apply77.py`. Job `j_a77.js` (from `j_a72.js`): a real page `CSCC(CSC)CC=C` (two S), line thiolene, hydrate, dehydrate: thiolene puts an SH on, Brimstone lights with three sulfurs; with `zw=0`, `at` = ms after the wake starts; `AA.rxKit.s77dbg()` gives the plan. Strip: `NAME=st3 WSIZE=2560,1440 ./run.sh a77 "&stage=3&zw=0&smi=CSCC(CSC)CC=C&lines=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&rack=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&at=900,1900,2900,4300,6600,7850,8850,9750,10950,11500,14600,17300" 880` then `python strip72.py frames/st3 out.jpg 6 600 "450,0,500"`.
- **Clips for the helper** (one at a time, servers running; `t.sh`, `sheet.py`, `j_a21.js` copied from builder 72's kit, `make4.py` already here; not yet tried for this act):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/brim69/.claude/r69 && JOBN=a21 TMO=900 ./t.sh brimstone77_act "&hom=brimstone&part=act&stage=3&smi=CSCC(CSC)CC=C&lines=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&rack=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&hold=21000" 24 && python make4.py brimstone77-act.mp4 brimstone77_act`
  - stage 5: the same with `brimstone77_act5`, `&stage=5`, `hold=25000`, `TMO=1000`, `brimstone77-act-5.mp4`; stage 1: `brimstone77_act1`, `&stage=1`, `hold=16000`, `brimstone77-act-1.mp4`.
- **Weak** (from the frames): see `brimstone77-act-strip.txt`. Also: the thread is stretched skin in a straight line from the lump's top to the foot of the smoke (a grey tube, not a wisp along the way); the face is W69's (big round coals, a frown) and reads sulky more than terrible at this size; the reaching arm is a long grey limb that can read as a leg; at stage 1 the wisp is thin; the round-end screen comes while it is still going home (the page's own flow) and then the bench creature is hidden; stages 2 and 4 not looked at; sound not heard; no timing on a real graphics card (up to 40 flame sprites, 24 embers, 70 smoke sprites, 60 marks, one page plane).

## Seventy-ninth builder (15 November)

Branch `hydrogen-builder-75` (worktree `hyd75`; `steam` merged in first; the page started with no script error with the switch on and off, job `j_h79.js` `part=ok`; the only logged error is the old `s30` one at `kitart.js` ~1753, which also shows with the switch off). File: `kitart.js` only (block `W75` changed, new block `W79` directly after `W75`'s end and before `W74`). No `scheme.js` change. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `hydrogen79-stages.jpg` (the five stages on the bench, close, each at some moment of its idle), `hydrogen79-idle-strip.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5: the game view, then six moments over 5 s, square crops of the 1920-wide view enlarged 2x), `hydrogen79-jar.jpg` (the shelf, and Hydrogen's jar close at stages 1, 3, 5), `hydrogen79-idle-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `W79` sets `AA.rxKit.hyd75 = 1` unless the page has `?h75=0` (the old Hydrogen, bench and jar). `AA.rxKit.idle79 = false` (or `?i79=0`) plays Water's rest on the new figure. Bench and jar work at all five stages; nothing throws (`W79.err`, `HYD75.err` empty).
- **Part 1, readable on a phone (W75).** Bigger: the figure's share of its space is raised and the lift cut (`fit`/`lift` per stage: 1 5.7/0.15, 2 6.8/0.33, 3 7.5/0.42, 4 7.8/0.36, 5 8.1/0.34; was 4.2/0.4 to 8.2/0.7), so stage 1 is about 1.5 times as big and stage 3 about 1.2 times; the float height now comes from the idle. Stage 1 has two tiny wisp arms and a clearer pinch (a little two-lobed being, not a dot). Shadow darker and tighter (`o.shadow` strength 0.72 to 0.8, colour 0x0c0604), a round pool of pale light under every stage (`o.light` 0.3 to 0.46; it was a long beam, 6 x 7 of the shadow's size, now 3.4 x 3.6), glows stronger (halo and core about 1.5 times), pen firmer (`W75.pen` 1.05, was 0.82). Threads: `dens75` makes a thread's path dense (Catmull-Rom, three points a span), so its hull is a smooth line, not beads. Stage 4: the companion hangs out to the side below head height, tipped toward its thread, bigger (1.55) with eyes and a smile (its swing is kept low: tipped up, it went off the top of the screen and read as a balloon); the hip arm is a clean wisp held low (it was a loop back to the body). Stage 5: three companions (was four). `lay75` lays shadow and light under the lifted figure itself (it darts), and scales them by `S.h79` (wider and paler high, smaller and darker low).
- **Its own idle (W79), keyed on `S.way75` only.** `C26.rest` wrapped (`rest79`: the face only: looks, brows, blinks, smile, nod); `play26` wrapped so `pose79` runs after the whole play chain; clock `clk79` (0.75 in the jar). It cannot stay down: `drift79` is a cycle (5.4 to 7.6 s) of a slow drift up (each cycle a different height, side and length), it notices (eyes wide, looks down), paddles and tugs itself down past its place, and bobs back; heights are hashed per cycle, so it is never the same twice. The whole figure moves as ONE transform of the group `mid75` (offset, turn about the pinch, squash and stretch about the pinch from the motion's own acceleration), and limbs turn rigidly about their roots: nothing can come apart. The lick streams up and wavers like a pilot flame (stretched up when it tugs down, trailing sideways moves); the tail trails. Events (start jittered per period): a pop of pale light beside it (a sprite in the holder; it flinches, then is delighted, the glow flares), it peers down at its shadow and waves at it, a spin round once, a dart sideways and back with a faint streak (a plane in the holder), a loop. A soft ceiling (`W79.TOP`, 8.75 game units over its place) keeps it on the screen; a soft floor keeps it off the wood. Per stage: 1 hops, floats a hand's breadth, settles with a wobble, pops and peers; 2 walks along an invisible tightrope, sways, nearly tips every ~9 s and windmills back (arms counter its tilt), a short dart, a spin; 3 the full drift and tug, a loop (~13 s), a dart (~10 s), a spin now and then; 4 the companion swings round it on its thread (in the plane and round the vertical, the turn lagging along the thread), the body and head keep turning toward it, every 5.3 s it jerks its raised arm and the little one is pulled in and bobs; 5 the three companions wheel slowly about it like a mobile (each its own phase, lagging along the thread), its arms conduct, the whole rises and sinks.
- **No other idle on Hydrogen, Hydrogen's on no other.** `W79` passes every figure without `way75` straight on; the job's `figs` show `k73`, `k68`, `k67`, `k62`, `k52`, `k56`, `a48`, `way63`, `way69`, `way64` false on every Hydrogen figure. The old error at ~1753 is guarded for Hydrogen in `W79`'s `play26` wrapper (as builders 68 and 73 did).
- **Jar** (`jar79`, builder 73's way): the half-grown, ready and stage forms are the new Hydrogen fitted low in the glass. The body's own draw hook fired only once per figure, so the jar figure is posed from `draw30` (`tick79`, once a moment): it rises toward the lid, bumps and sinks back. No shadow, glow, pop or streak in the jar.
- **Cost** (headless software rendering, 1304 x 812, stage 3 at home, 30 frames, very noisy): `pose79` 0.03 ms a frame (it works out the motion three times for speed and acceleration). Frames 434 and 540 ms with the new idle, 575 with Water's rest on it, 585 for the old Hydrogen: within noise. It adds two sprites/planes (pop, streak).
- **Act and change of stage with the new figure** (called directly with `rigPlay`; nothing throws, the idle returns after): the act is Water's `act` on Hydrogen's joints (sinks, slides, reaches with an arm, turns, comes home), played with `mid75` put back at its place (hanging at its rest height, no drift); the change of stage is Water's `evo`, morphing from WATER's skin of the old stage. Both need Hydrogen's own clips.
- **Weak** (from the frames): see `hydrogen79-idle-strip.txt`. Still small in the full game view (about a tenth of the screen's height; the space between shelf and notebook allows little more); reads as a little ghost more than a mote; stage 5 busy, and a companion hanging below can read as a toy on a string; threads have some dotting left; in the jar it is small and does not quite reach the lid; the glow does not tint the shelf or notebook, and it has no lamp-side light of its own; stages 2 and 4 seen only in stills; no timing on a real graphics card.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r75` of this worktree, untracked; ports 10391/10392; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID, `my79.pids`). Block source `block79.js` (`python apply79.py` puts it after `W75`'s end). Job `j_h79.js` (from builder 73's `j_b73.js`): start check `NAME=ok_on ./run.sh h79 "&part=ok" 180` (and `&h75=0`); stills `NAME=st79 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh h79 "&part=still&big=2&stages=1,2,3,4,5&wait=3400" 540` then `python stsheet79.py frames/st79 out.jpg`; strip `NAME=s79 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh h79 "&part=strip&stages=1,3,5&k=6&span=5000&off=900" 580` then `python sheet62.py frames/s79 out.jpg 1,3,5 6 "815,20,270;785,0,330;755,0,380" 2`; jar `NAME=j79 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh h79 "&part=jar&stages=1,3,5" 440` then `python jarsheet79.py frames/j79 out.jpg`; cost `NAME=k79 ./run.sh h79 "&part=cost&stage=3" 380`; act/evo probe `NAME=c79 ./run.sh h79 "&part=clips&stage=3" 380`. Clips (script `t79.sh`, copied from builder 73's `t73.sh`; 30 frames a second, scaled to 1920, mp4 into the shots folder; the servers must be running; one at a time):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/hyd75/.claude/r75 && ./t79.sh hydrogen79-idle 3 12` (12 s at rest, stage 3)
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/hyd75/.claude/r75 && ./t79.sh hydrogen79-idle-5 5 10` (10 s at rest, stage 5)

## Eightieth builder (15 November)

Branch `woodspirit-builder-80` (worktree `wood80`, from `steam`). File: `kitart.js` (block `W80`, the last block inside the creature closure, after `W78`: it needs Water's code, builder 48's `ink48` and `W48.benchY`, builder 49's `flat49`/`tex49` and builder 64's `pad64`/`lim64`). Stills only. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `woodspirit80-stages.jpg` (the five Wood spirit stages, Water's five below at the same scale), `woodspirit80-game.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5 in the full game view), `woodspirit80-silhouettes.jpg`, `woodspirit80-stages.txt` (per-stage notes, animation ideas, what is wrong). Ruling BN: no approval round, one design carried through.

- **Switch.** `AA.rxKit.wood80 = 1` or `?w80=1`: OFF by default, the game shows the old Wood spirit. The jar keeps the old Wood spirit. The page starts with no script error with the switch on and off (job `j_ok80.js`).
- **Built by Water's code** (`wood80Fig`), as 48/50, 49/53, 61, 64, 69, 75 and 78 did: `SPEC80[0..4]` go in Water's stage-3 slot for the moment of building and are put back; the figure carries `S.st` and `S.way80`. Skins are kept in `W80.geo`, roles in `W80.ROLE`.
- **The toast** (line-up candidate C; the only seated body of the ten). `mk80`: a pad hidden under the seat, then balls up the body (belly widest, chest, neck). `head80`: the head and a short lick lifting off its crown. `lim80`: legs and arms given where they are (legs lie on the wood, feet turned up; one arm props, one is raised). `face80`: heavy lids, high brows, a broad smile. `shim80`/`flick80`: pen lines of heat-shimmer and boiling-off flicks. `bub80`: Water's bubbles in the belly (they hardly show).
- **The five.** 1 the drop (a bead with two toes out, a spark over its crown). 2 the tot (sat like a small child, legs splayed, staring at its first flame). 3 the toast (the line-up's figure: sat back on one arm, legs out to the side, flame arm raised). 4 the host (lying back on one elbow, one knee up, flame held high, small flames on arm and knee). 5 the distiller (upright and facing us, legs wide, arms open, a flame on each palm, flames at shoulders and knees).
- **Flame, vapour, ring: drawn, not skin.** `flame80`: a sprite tongue (bare paper inside, blue-lilac low and at the edge, a broken fine dark line) in a soft lilac light (`tex49('glow')`); `o.flames` is `[x, y, z, height, turn, light]`. `vap80`: a pale wavering ribbon with broken pen lines (`o.vap`). Both hang on the BONE nearest to them, so they stay on the hand and the head when the rest pose moves them (set at the spec's place on the root, the flame floated off the hand). `ring80`: the alpha map of a dried spill's ring; ring and damp patch lie in a group `S.lay80` that `lay80` lays flat on the bench every draw. None of these is in the silhouette sheet.
- **Size.** A low figure is sized small by its tallest joint, so the root hangs in a group of its own (`S.mid80`, scale `o.sz`: 1.5, 1.45, 1.12, 1.08, 1.3). Stages 3 to 5 reach the top of the screen: they cannot be larger at this home place.
- **Wash and pen.** `tint80` swaps Water's four blues for `W80.C` (faint rose, wood-smoke grey-rose shade), no pooling, little hatching. Pen 0.95 of `W30.lw`. No pool: `G.pl` hidden from the pen; Water's wet mark and drips hidden.
- **Learned.** A body plume of radius 0.1 to 0.2 up from the head was all pen line: a rabbit's ear or an onion top; it is now a short lick with the vapour drawn above it. A peach tint (0.985, 0.87, 0.84) on a figure with a belly is bare skin; cooler and paler is better but still pink. Lids of a darker rose read as blushing cheeks; they are nearly the body's colour. Pen arcs for the spill's edge on the wood drew as straight scratches: out. A small flame near the chin is a dribble.
- **Weak** (from the sheets; more in `woodspirit80-stages.txt`): reads as a pink doll or baby more than a clear liquid (flat wash, little bare paper); stage 5 hardly grander than 4, legs over the notebook edge, a pale bib on its belly; stage 1 near Water 1; the crown lick makes a drop head; the flame is plain to see, not "nearly invisible"; flames, vapour and ring do not move; the rest pose is Water's (do not fire the act or the change of stage with the switch on: they would be Water's); 8 to 14 thousand vertices, 16 to 39 joints, no timing taken. The `s30` error in the job's log (line 1753) is the old one.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r80` of this worktree, untracked, scripts copied from `r78`; ports 10591/10592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py`, end them by PID): `./st80.sh [stages] [rows]` (rows `s` and `w`) runs job `j_s80.js` with `&w80=1&hom=wood&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/sN` and `&hom=water&big=2&stage=N` into `frames/wN` (`f00003` the game view, `f00004` the silhouette), about 90 s a figure; `python look80.py` makes two quick close sheets, `python crop.py sN` one close crop; `python sheet80.py` writes the three sheets. The start check: `NAME=ok_on ./run.sh ok80 "&w80=1" 150` and `NAME=ok_off ./run.sh ok80 "" 150` (`errs` must be empty). The stages are in `.claude/r80/spec80.js`; `python putspec.py` puts them into `block80.js` and `python apply.py` puts that into `kitart.js`.

## Eighty-first builder (15 November)

Branch `ammonia-builder-78` (worktree `amm78`; `steam` merged in first; the page started with no script error with the switch on and off, checked through the kit's harness, job `j_ok78.js`). File: `kitart.js` only (block `W78` changed, new block `W81` directly after `W78`'s end). No `scheme.js` change. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `ammonia81-stages.jpg` (the five stages on the bench, zoomed, each at some moment of its idle), `ammonia81-idle-strip.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5: the game view, then six moments over 4 s, square crops round the home spot of the 1920-wide view enlarged 2x), `ammonia81-jar.jpg` (the shelf, and Ammonia's jar close at stages 1, 3, 5), `ammonia81-idle-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `W81` sets `AA.rxKit.amm78 = 1` unless the page has `?m78=0` (the old Ammonia, bench and jar). `AA.rxKit.idle81 = false` (or `?i81=0`) plays Water's rest on the new figure and builds its streaks into the skin again (only for comparing; set it before the figure is first built: skins are cached per stage and per that flag). Bench and jar work at all five stages; nothing throws (`W81.err`, `AMM78.err` empty; the `?m78=0` page is clean too).
- **Part 1, gas not scribble (W78).** Wash: `W78.C` deeper teal and lilac, bare paper only on the lit side (`hl` 0.80 to 0.90, was 0.66 to 0.80). Body balls 1.2 times thicker (the thread 1.18), arms 1.32 (needle tips 1.1). Brows one flat line (`ang` about 0.04), lids lower (0.5 to 0.52); the idle tips the head back (`tilt` about 0.16) with the eyes looking down (`looky` -0.55). Stage 1 redrawn: a sharp lick leaning on its slant (head ball 0.5 drawn to a long point at 0.66 rad), one streak, foot and frost; `sz` 1.75 (was 1.5); stage 2 `sz` 1.5. Stage 5: four long wide streaks rising and spreading back like a banner (the crown blades and the low streaks gone), arms thick enough to take wash, frost 1.9 by 1.2. Frost and damp a third larger at every stage. `blade78` keeps its path (`ab`, `wd`, `cu`); `amm78Fig` takes the blades out of the skin when `W81` draws them (`o.rib`) and calls `W78.hook` (W81's `mk81`) once the figure is built.
- **Streaks are ribbons of vapour (W81).** Each streak is a strip of 24 points (`line81`: its path smoothed, then a short curl at the end) drawn with a canvas wash (lilac below, teal, pale above, faint streaks, fading toward the tip) on a Lambert material with the canvas as emissive (as `flat49`: the pen-and-wash swap leaves it alone; a plain MeshBasic one came out as paper, and the strip needs a normal attribute or Lambert draws it black). Two pen strokes along its upper edge (a broken thin line, written by `ink48` in the root bone's frame, kept off the curl). Anchored at the spine joint it leaves, so it never comes away.
- **The frost (W81).** In `S.lay78` (on the wood every draw): a canvas of sharp six-armed crystals and spikes (white with a dark pen edge) round a damp ring, and a dark contact shade under the foot; W78's pale bloom made wider and fainter (the cold tint on the wood).
- **Its own idle (W81), keyed on `S.way78` only.** `C26.rest` wrapped (`rest81` gives Water's own bones their numbers: `lean` bends the body from the pinned foot, the neck's `tilt` and `hturn`, `armR`, `hair` the point, the face's looks, brows, blinks, `smile`, `sq`), so the body stays one; `play26` wrapped so `pose81` runs after the whole play chain (ribbons, tear, wisp, frost). Page clock `clk81` (0.6 in the jar); the clip's lag is used at 0.35 so the tips follow a hair late. Every move is a key (`kf81`): fast arrival with a small click, then held dead; long keys (stage 5) are smooth. Quiver: bursts (`win81`) of a 13.7 and 21.3 Hz shiver on the lean and tilt, hard on and off. Ribbons: two travelling waves (each stage's wind size and speed), a pennant snap (a fast ripple running root to tip every 2.6 to 4.4 s), the shiver at their roots. The tear beads at the point over 1.4 s and is flung sideways at each head snap (`ev`), arcs, falls and fades in 0.8 s. A cold wisp (Brimstone's `tex73` puff, pale teal) boils off the wet patch and drifts back. The frost creeps out and draws back (7.3 s). Per stage: 1 sniffs in ten jerks over 6.2 s (point up, round to each side, eyes darting), shivers in five bursts, strains out once and is tugged back; 2 the lunge over and over (3.4 s: winds back, lunges with the needle arm, flicks the tip, recovers, tosses its head, smirking), shivers in the hold; 3 strains out along its slant in two jerks, flicks its arm out and back, is tugged back, tosses its head, snaps round to look with eyes narrowed and brows down, holds, turns away with a toss, flicks again (7.2 s); 4 stands 5% taller, points its raised arm at four things in turn with head and eyes following each (8.4 s), mane faster and wider; 5 stands still (shiver 0.004), banner fastest and widest, then one slow turn of the head and a narrowed look, held, and back (11 s).
- **No other idle on Ammonia, Ammonia's on no other.** `W81` passes every figure without `way78` straight on; the job's `figs` show `k73`, `way69`, `k68`, `k67`, `k62`, `k52`, `k56`, `a48`, `way63`, `way49`, `way64` false on every Ammonia figure. The old error at `kitart.js` ~1753 is fixed for Ammonia in `W81`'s `play26` wrapper (the margin material is given a width, as builders 68 and 73 did); `s30dbg().err` can still show it from other figures.
- **Jar** (`jar81`, builder 73's way): the half-grown, ready and stage forms are the new Ammonia fitted to the glass (ribbons included in the fit), each playing its rest from its own `onBeforeRender` (`drive81`). No frost or wisp in the jar.
- **Cost** (headless software rendering, 1304 x 812, stage 3 at home, 30 frames each; another recorder was running, very noisy): `pose81` 0.03 ms a frame. Frames 750 ms with the new idle, 1053 with Water's rest on it, 1092 for the old Ammonia, 1376 with the new idle in a second pass: not to be told apart from noise; it adds 1 to 4 ribbon strips (48 vertices each, rewritten every frame), up to 8 pen strokes, 4 sprites and 2 flat planes.
- **Act and change of stage with the new figure** (called directly with `rigPlay`; nothing throws, the idle returns after): the act is Water's `act` on Ammonia's joints (sinks to its foot, slides, rises, reaches with a joint, turns its back, comes home); the ribbons stay on, riding their joints with the wind going; the tear, wisp and quiver stop (`pose81(S, false)`); the frost follows the figure. The change of stage is Water's `evo`: it morphs from WATER's skin of the old stage. Both need Ammonia's own clips. `scheme.js`'s old Ammonia jump (squash and spin, `JUMPS.ammonia`) still drives the holder group; the evolution's bursts use `JARC.ammonia` (0xbcd6dc, a pale teal: fits).
- **Weak** (from the frames; see `ammonia81-idle-strip.txt`): at full 1920 width it is 150 to 200 px tall, so quiver and arm flick are small without zoom; stage 5 is barely larger than stage 4 (banner and frost wider only) and its top streak touches the screen's top edge; curled ribbon tips at 4 and 5 can still read as hair or tentacles; the tear is tiny at game size; in the jar it is small and the ribbons faint; stage 4's lower arm still reads partly as a second leg; stages 2 and 4 seen only in stills; no clip watched; no timing on a real graphics card.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r78` of this worktree, untracked; ports 10491/10492; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID). Block source `block81.js` (`python apply81.py` puts it in after `W78`). Job `j_m81.js` (from builder 73's `j_b73.js`): start check `NAME=ok_on81 ./run.sh m81 "&part=ok&m78=1" 200` (and `&m78=0`); stills `NAME=m81 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh m81 "&part=still&big=2&m78=1&stages=1,2,3,4,5" 400` then `python sheet68.py frames/m81 2 5 out.jpg "1,2,3,4,5"`; strip `NAME=s81 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh m81 "&part=strip&m78=1&stages=1,3,5&k=6&span=4000" 560` then `python sheet62.py frames/s81 out.jpg 1,3,5 6 "860,20,240;830,0,300;810,0,330" 2`; jar `NAME=j81 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh m81 "&part=jar&m78=1&stages=1,3,5" 400`; cost `NAME=k81 ./run.sh m81 "&part=cost&stage=3" 300`; act/evo probe `NAME=c81 ./run.sh m81 "&part=clips&stage=3" 300`; `all81.sh` runs them all. Clips (script `t81.sh`, copied from builder 73's `t73.sh`, with `make4.py`; 30 frames a second, 2560 capture scaled to 1920, mp4 into the shots folder; the servers must be running; one at a time):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/amm78/.claude/r78 && ./t81.sh ammonia81-idle 3 12` (12 s at rest, stage 3)
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/amm78/.claude/r78 && ./t81.sh ammonia81-idle-5 5 10` (10 s at rest, stage 5)

## Eighty-third builder (15 November)

Branch `seedling-builder-64` (worktree `seedling64`; `steam` merged in first). Only blocks `W74` (`kitart.js`) and `S74` (`scheme.js`) changed. The owner, on a phone: "The seedling egg just pops out of nowhere?" Now the plant visibly winds down into its OWN husk.

- **The winding down** (3.75 beats, about 1.9 s at pace 2, at 2 to 3, 3 to 4 and 4 to 5; was 1.5, 1.75, 2.25): the leaves roll one by one, top first (0 to 0.5 of the span); the stem coils down (`e.f`, 0.12 to 0.8); the page's coat is taken up from the husk at the figure's foot (`o.hb74`, the husk's width per stage `HB74`, given by W74 every frame; faded up over that husk in the first 0.1, `e.hf`), then swells from that size to the seed's while its two halves rise from flared open to upright (`e.gr`, 0.2 to 0.65); the lips close over the top from the zigzag to a point (`e.cl` 0.61 to 1, from 0.64); the eyes stay open looking up till 0.86 and are the last thing seen in the gap; the click at 0.95 (was 0.85).
- **Beats** (go, look, wind, swell, ...): 1 to 2 `[2, 0.5, 1.25, 1.25, ...]` hit on 5 (same); 2 to 3 `[2, 0.75, 3.75, 1.5, ...]` hit on 8 (was 7); 3 to 4 `[2.25, 0.75, 3.75, 2.25, ...]` hit on 9 (was 8); 4 to 5 `[2.5, 1, 3.75, 3.75, ...]` hit on 11 (same). The rest unchanged.
- **1 to 2**: the coat takes up the lying seed tilted with it (`K.bd.rotation.z`) and rights itself as it swells; its eyes shut early as before.
- **The coat's look**: the figure husk's tan-brown (`#dcbfa8`), a canvas texture of wash, grain and short pen strokes (`s74Grain`, uv written by `s74Fill`). The inside of the shell is drawn before the figure (renderOrder 12, BackSide), its outside after it (`m2`, 24, FrontSide), so the plant never shows through; the seam, tip and halo moved above it (24.5, 24.7, 25).
- **The burst**: the new plant starts at 0.3 of its folded size and grows to it over 0.4 beats (no full-size first frame).
- **Weak**: see `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots\seedling83-fold-strip.txt`. Placement still left of centre; the face looks forward more than up; inside the gap the bud is a green blob crossed by pen strokes; 1 to 2's righting is quick; 2 to 3 and 4 to 5 not rendered after the change; stills only.
- **To render**: same as the seventy-fourth (`./e74.sh`), plus `fold83.py` in `.claude/r64` (the square-crop strip: `python fold83.py <out.jpg> f83_3 0.33,0.5,0.72 f83_1 0.25,0.5,0.6 "<labels>" "<labels>"` after `COLS=5 ./e74.sh f83_3 3 "2.7,3.13,3.57,4.0,4.43,4.87,5.3,5.73,6.17,6.6" ""` and `COLS=5 ./e74.sh f83_1 1 "2.55,2.9,3.25,3.6,4.4" ""`).

## Eighty-fifth builder (15 November)

Branch `woodspirit-builder-80` (worktree `wood80`; `steam` merged in first; the merged page started with no script error with the switch on and off, checked through the kit's harness, job `j_ok80.js`). File: `kitart.js` only (block `W80` changed, new block `W85` directly after `W80`'s end). No `scheme.js` change. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `woodspirit85-stages.jpg` (the five stages on the bench, close, each at the same moment of its idle), `woodspirit85-compare.jpg` (stage 3 before and after), `woodspirit85-idle-strip.jpg` (stages 1, 3, 5: the game view, then six moments over 6 s, square crops round the home spot of the 1920-wide view enlarged 2x), `woodspirit85-jar.jpg` (the shelf, and Wood spirit's jar close at stages 1, 3, 5), `woodspirit85-idle-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `W85` sets `AA.rxKit.wood80 = 1` unless the page has `?w80=0` (the old Wood spirit, bench and jar). `AA.rxKit.idle85 = false` (or `?i85=0`) plays Water's rest on the new figure (only for comparing). Bench and jar work at all five stages (bench looked at for all five, jar at 1, 3, 5); nothing throws (`W85.err`, `WOOD80.err` empty; the `?w80=0` page and the default page start clean). `AA.rxKit.W85.fix = <seconds>` freezes its idle clock at one moment (for stills; the job's `&fix=`).
- **Part 1, a clear spirit, not a pink baby (W80).** Colour: `W80.C` is a very pale neutral grey (mostly paper), a rose-wine edge (0.70, 0.42, 0.54) only at the rim and on the shadow side, a pale grey-mauve shade; no pink flesh. `tint80` adds (body only, not the margin): where the skin faces us the bench is seen through it as a grey film with its grain (contrast-stretched luminance of the scene behind, desaturated, `C.see` 0.6), a rose-wine rim on the shadow side, a bright crescent highlight on each round part (a band round a fixed view-space light). Figure: `head85` (a jaw ball under the head, a domed crown, a small evaporating point; no lick), heads smaller and sunk lower on thicker necks, eyes smaller (`er` 0.8), heavier lids, longer flatter thicker brows, broad mouths; bodies longer with a paunch; limbs long and loose, running to a point (no toes). 1 the drop (a pure drop, no feet, a sleepy face, the spark over its point; `sz` 2.0); 2 the tot sat cross-legged, both hands held to a flame floating in front of its belly (`sz` 1.75; a flame's 7th number 1 hangs it on the body, not a hand); 3 sat back on one arm, one leg out long, one knee up, flame arm raised; 4 lying back on its elbow, knee up, flame high, two small flames along the arm; 5 broad and upright, knees wide and up, arms open, a flame on each palm, four small flames along the shoulders, legs kept inside the bench (feet z 0.5 to 0.58). Vapour ribbon wider (sprite 0.72 of its height, wider bands) and shorter, leaning away from the flame (`vap` 6th number). The pen's flicks and shimmer are now W85's and move. Flames are built in the jar too.
- **Its own idle (W85), keyed on `S.way80` only.** `C26.rest` wrapped (`rest85`: the face, Water's neck tilt/nod/turn, Water's `swell`/`sq` of the spine for the breath, `lean` and `turn` from the seat, so the body stays one and the planted legs and propping hand stay pinned); `play26` wrapped so `pose85` runs after the whole play chain; clock `clk85` (0.6 in the jar). `pose85` turns the flame arms about their shoulders, the knee leg about the hip, the host's resting forearm about the elbow (rigid turns of joints: nothing parts); the flames are W80's sprites on the hand bones, scaled each frame (waver, and `fk` per event), their glow flashing when one pops back; smoke (`tex73` puff) curls up where one went out; a pale additive light on the bench under each main flame and a faint lilac light on the face follow the flame; contact shade under the seat and under every planted hand and foot; a bead (`bead85`) runs down its shadow side every 7.5 s and soaks in (the damp patch darkens); the dried ring and a copy widen and fade out of step (16 s); pen flicks on the crown, shoulders and the raised arm's back lift off, curl and form again; shimmer lines climb off head and shoulders (tall at stage 5's blessing); the vapour wavers and leans. Cycles: 1 (10 s) dozes, the spark winks off and pops back twice, it opens one eye at it (`S.F.eyes[1]` held shut) and dozes again; 2 (12 s) hands creep to the flame, it flares, hands snatched back, it leans back wide-eyed, giggles (a fast shake of lean and tilt, squint, wide smile), hands creep back, it blows the flame bright; 3 (18 s) raises the flame in greeting and nods, lowers it, watches it, the flame dwindles and a slow breath across the fingertips brings it back, it shifts its seat and draws up its knee, the flame goes out with a curl of smoke, it looks at its fingers (eyes wide, brows up), snaps (a jerk of the arm) and the flame pops back, a slow contented blink and a wider smile; 4 (16 s) lifts its resting forearm, tosses the flame across to it (an arc over its head), holds it there looking at it, tosses it back, then 3's dwindle, seat shift, out and snap, blink; small flames run along the arm; 5 (16 s) raises both flames slowly together like a blessing (flames grow, shimmer and vapour rise tall, eyes half shut, head up), lowers them, dwindle and breath on one hand, the other goes out and is snapped back, a small turn of the seat, blink; a flame runs along the shoulders.
- **No other idle on Wood spirit, Wood spirit's on no other.** `W85` passes every figure without `way80` straight on; the job's `figs` show `k73`, `way69`, `k68`, `k67`, `k62`, `k52`, `k56`, `a48`, `way63`, `way49`, `way64` false on every Wood spirit figure. The old error at `kitart.js` ~1753 is guarded for Wood spirit in `W85`'s `play26` wrapper (as builders 68, 73 and 81 did); `s30dbg().err` can still show it from other figures.
- **Jar** (`jar85`, builder 81's way): the half-grown, ready and stage forms are the new Wood spirit fitted to the glass, flames included, each playing its rest from its own `onBeforeRender` (`drive85`). No ring, damp, bead, light or smoke in the jar.
- **Cost** (headless software rendering, 1304 x 812, stage 3 at home, 30 frames each, very noisy): `pose85` 0.06 ms a frame. Frames 1096 and 680 ms with the new idle, 1020 with Water's rest on it, 940 for the old Wood spirit: not to be told apart from noise. It adds up to 8 small sprites (puffs, held and air flames, face light, bead), 1 to 2 light planes, 3 to 6 shade planes, a second ring, and about 14 to 20 pen strokes rewritten each frame.
- **Act and change of stage with the new figure** (called directly with `rigPlay`; nothing throws, the idle returns after): the act is Water's `act` on Wood spirit's joints (sinks into its seat, slides, rises, reaches with an arm joint, turns its back, comes home); the flames ride their hand bones through it (no waver), flicks, smoke, bead and light stop (`pose85(S, false)`). The change of stage is Water's `evo`: it morphs from WATER's skin of the old stage. Both need Wood spirit's own clips. The evolution's bursts in `scheme.js` use `JARC.wood` (0x9a6b3a, a brown that leans to whisky): to change with Wood spirit's own evolution. `scheme.js`'s old Wood spirit act (`bdrop` flies) is untouched.
- **Weak** (from the frames; see `woodspirit85-idle-strip.txt`): the pink doll/baby read is gone, but it reads as a white rubbery or marshmallow figure more than a clear liquid (the see-through is a faint grey film); flicks over the crown can read as stray hairs, shimmer as cartoon fume lines; stage 5's mouth is a heavy black grin; stage 1 is an egg with a face; the flame is small at stage 1 and in the jar (no mist on the glass); stage 5's head and raised flames touch the top of the screen; the seat shift, knee, toss and out-and-snap were not caught in the strip's frames (stages 2 and 4 seen only in stills); no clip watched; no timing on a real graphics card.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r80` of this worktree, untracked; ports 10591/10592; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID, `my85.pids`). Sources `spec85.js` (the five stages) and `block85.js` (W85); `python put85.py` puts both in. Job `j_w85.js` (from builder 81's `j_m81.js`): start check `NAME=ok_on ./run.sh ok80 "&w80=1" 170` (and `&w80=0`, and none); stills `NAME=w5u WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh w85 "&part=still&big=2&stages=1,2,3,4,5&fix=3.2" 540` then `python sheet85.py stages frames/w5u out.jpg 2`; strip `NAME=s85 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh w85 "&part=strip&stages=1,3,5&k=6&span=6000" 580` then `python sheet85.py strip frames/s85 out.jpg 1,3,5 6`; compare `python sheet85.py compare <shots>/woodspirit80-stages.jpg frames/w5u f00004 out.jpg`; jar `NAME=j85 WSIZE=2584,1532 ./run.sh w85 "&part=jar&stages=1,3,5" 480`; cost `NAME=k85 ./run.sh w85 "&part=cost&stage=3" 380`; act/evo probe `NAME=c85 ./run.sh w85 "&part=clips&stage=3" 380`. Clips (script `t85.sh`, copied from builder 81's `t81.sh`, with `make4.py`; 30 frames a second, 2560 capture scaled to 1920, mp4 into the shots folder; the servers must be running; one at a time):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/wood80/.claude/r80 && ./t85.sh woodspirit85-idle 3 12` (12 s at rest, stage 3)
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/wood80/.claude/r80 && ./t85.sh woodspirit85-idle-5 5 10` (10 s at rest, stage 5)

## Seventy-sixth builder (15 November)

Branch `salt-builder-63` (worktree `salt63`; `steam` merged in first; the merged page started with no script error through the kit's harness, `part=ok`). Files: `kitart.js` (new block `W76` directly after `W72`'s end; `W63`/`W68`/`W72` untouched: `W76` wraps `play26`, `ev68` and `C26.rest` from outside and calls `post72`, `walk72`, `turn68`, `salt63Fig`), `scheme.js` (new block `S76` directly after `S72`'s end; it calls `s59Place`, `s72Steps`, `s72Mark`, `s72Kick`, `s58Line` and builder 33's lamp pieces without changing them). No other creature's block changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `salt76-evolve-strip.jpg` (four rows, 1 to 2 ... 4 to 5; six moments each in the full game view: set down at the centre, half crusted with the crust on the paper, the block glowing in the dimmed room, the cracks, slabs falling with the new form, home and settled), `salt76-evolve-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.evo76` (`?v76=0` or `false` gives the old change of stage: Water's `evo` on the crystal's joints; checked: it still runs to the end, no error). Only the bench Salt (`S.way63`, not the jar, idle `idle68` on) uses it; every other figure and clip passes straight on.
- **What exists: "The geode".** `play26` is wrapped for Salt's `evo` clip; Water's `evo` is not run. The timeline is in beats of the music (`PAR76`, `KIT.ph76`, written into `R21.evo.e76` so the page reads the same numbers): go, crust, still, crack (the hit, on a whole beat), reveal, home. 1 to 2 is 14.5 beats, 2 to 3 17.5, 3 to 4 19, 4 to 5 22.5 (7.3 to 11.3 s at pace 2).
  1. **Go**: the OLD stage's figure (built once by `salt63Fig`, beside the new one) snaps to attention and marches from home to the place by the act's own step (`walk72`/`stp72`, the body turned whole about its corner by `post72`; stage 1 tumbles like a dice; the step over the book's edge from `s72Steps`), growing on the way; a tick, grains and a mark at each landing (S72's). The place is S59's `s59Place`; then `s76Plan` fits the whole block (both forms inside it) on the screen and clear of the molecule, with a per-step cap on its height (small at 1 to 2, a boulder at 4 to 5). The block's frame is the figures' own: the holder's tip (`rotation.x -0.8`, set in `homStand`) times the old form's root as posed this frame.
  2. **Crust**: it hops, chin up, and shuts its eyes slowly. In rushes on the half beats small cubes (opaque, ink rim) bud on its own crystals, placed where each box's bone has it (`KIT.bw76`): shards, buds and hat first, then the other crystals, its face last. In the last rush the block grows round it: slabs (back, sides, top, then the front) stack bottom up in four steps, each with a crust of small cubes that pop out of its faces (chunkier at 4 to 5). The block is cut into slabs along the lines it will crack along. On the paper (`s76Paper`, a canvas on a flat mesh) a white stepped stain runs out from under it in right-angled fingers with small cubes at their tips, kept on the page (across both pages at 4 to 5).
  3. **Still**: the old figure is hidden once the block is whole (no swap shows); the room dims (builder 33's one-lamp pass, `s76Lamp`, a copy of S71's with its own state); a cold blue light (an additive glow on the block's front and top, a glow on the page) wakes in slow pulses (1, 2, 2, 3), the dark shape of the larger new form shows through (its boxes drawn faintly over the block); a glint twinkles on the block's lamp-side corner; one loud tick a beat before the hit.
  4. **Crack**: on the hit straight cracks shoot out from a point both ways along the cut lines (ink strokes with a white flash along each, on the front, top and sides), plus short ones (6 at 4 to 5). A beat later the slabs fall: each tips about its outer bottom edge and drops (those on the molecule's side fall forward instead), and breaks into grains on the paper; the back slabs fall back. 1 to 2: one crack across, the top lifts off and tips; 2 to 3: two halves fall apart left and right; 3 to 4: the block is taller, cut in four, the top quarter where the spire is goes first, the rest half a beat later; 4 to 5: a grid of nine, the middle and bottom first, then the sides, then the top row left to right like a fan.
  5. **Reveal**: the NEW stage is put in place while hidden and shown as the slabs open; its bed grows back out of the rubble in four steps; glints run along the main cube's edges (lamp side first), flash at its corners and on the shards and buds, and die down. Eyes shut, then open; it looks down its nose at the rubble (chin up, lids half down) and gives one short nod with a click. 1 to 2: the twin lifts its shard to its hat, straightens it, it slips back askew with a click, a frown; 2 to 3: its shards snap out of a fold and the crest buds pop; 3 to 4: the pointing shard comes up; 4 to 5: the halo turns open in three clicks. The crust it grew outside the block breaks to grains with the slabs.
  6. **Home**: the grains are drawn in toward its foot and go; the paper crust cracks into squares and fades (gone by 0.4 of the way home; page clean, checked in the last frame of each row); two quarter turns, the march home seen from behind (W72's hopper square on its back), two quarter turns, a click; the new number is written beside it (`HEVO.num`).
  - **Sound** (existing `sfx` calls only, on the beats): `click` at attention, `tick` at each landing (`clink(2, 'salt')` at the book's edge), three quick `tick`s per crust rush, two as the slabs grow, `click` as the block closes, a `draw` riser through the stillness with `flagSoft` at each pulse, `click` + `tick(12)` (the loud tick) before the hit, `snap(5, 1)` per crack, `snap(6, 1)` then `clink(to + 4, 'salt')` at the split (`boom` at 4 to 5), a `tick` per slab breaking, the base's `stamp`, `feed`s and `good`, `click` at the nod, `swish` at the turns.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r63` of this worktree, not in git; ports 10091/10092; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID; `my76.pids`). Sources `w76/block76.js`, `w76/s76.js`; `python apply76.py` puts them in. Stills: `./e76.sh <name> <stage it leaves> "<beats,...>" "<ms after home>"` (job `j_e76.js`, from builder 71's; sheet `t76/<name>.jpg`; `AA.rxKit.s76dbg()` gives the beat `u`); the strip: `./all76.sh <prefix>`, which writes `t76/<prefix>-strip.jpg` (`strip76.py`). A take of 4 to 5 needs up to 10 minutes on this machine (`e76.sh` allows 1000 s).
- **Clips for the helper** (one at a time, servers running; each take a few minutes; `t.sh` with builder 21's job `j_a21.js`, `make4.py` builder 55's, both already in `.claude/r63`):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/salt63/.claude/r63 && for s in 1 2 3 4; do JOBN=a21 TMO=1200 ./t.sh ev76_$s "&hom=salt&part=evo&stage=$s&lead=1000&hold=13000" 24; done`
  - each step alone: `python make4.py salt76-evolve-<s>.mp4 ev76_<s>`
  - all four back to back with number cards and sound: `python make4.py salt76-evolve-all.mp4 ev76_1="1 to 2" ev76_2="2 to 3" ev76_3="3 to 4" ev76_4="4 to 5"`
- **Weak**: see `salt76-evolve-strip.txt`. Also: the block reads as a crusted white crate more than a rough lump; the glow is a faint blue and the cracks are hard to tell from the glow at strip size; the new form is about a third of the screen tall (the block round it is capped at about two thirds); at the split slabs, grains and glints are busy; a slab vanishes into its grains in one frame; if the way figures are held changes, the block's frame (the fixed -0.8 tip) must change with it; looked at in stills only, sound not heard, no timing on a real graphics card (up to 9 cells of up to 5 slabs, about 150 crust cubes, up to 90 grown cubes, about 200 grains, a 512 canvas redrawn at each crust step).

## Eighty-fourth builder (15 November)

Branch `slime-builder-61` (worktree `slime61`; `steam` merged in first). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W71` only), `scheme.js` (block `S71` only). No other creature's block changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `slime84-evolve-strip.jpg` (four rows, 1 to 2 .. 4 to 5; six square crops each on the event: the old form, melted, gathered, the pull at its tallest, just landed, settled as the new form at the place), `slime84-pulls.jpg` (the four pulls at their tallest, side by side), `slime84-evolve-strip.txt`.

- **The fault** (owner, watching the four changes on a phone): every change showed the same plain glossy stalk with a ball head and a stock face standing up out of a flat puddle; the pool with stuck-on eyes and the lumpy dome were stand-ins of the same kind. Rule from now on for every evolution: what is on screen is the creature itself, its own pen-and-wash body and face, changing shape; never a prop that replaces it; the four changes must not show the same piece.
- **What changed: the melt, the gathering and the pull are Slime's own skinned figure** (the timing and beats of "the pull" are unchanged, `PAR71`, `ph71`). The old figure is never hidden (`e.gone` is always false). The page's pool grid, ink hull, rim, wet highlight, bubbles and dimples, the tube column(s), the peeling string tubes and the floating canvas face are all switched off (the code is still in `S71`, unused: `s71Grid`, `s71Col`, `s71Face`).
  1. **Melt** (`post71`): the old stage's own body joints sag flat (`fy` to 0.32) and spread (`fs` to 1.22), arms sink last (drawn toward their roots and down), its own slick spreads (the root's `px`/`pz` to 1.25, through the `C26.rest` wrapper as `z.px`). The head joint keeps its height scale (at least 0.85) so its own face rides on top; lids droop heavy, a half blink.
  2. **Gathering**: it draws back in and humps up (`fy` back up by 0.4, slick to 1.1), trembles (a small fast sway of the body joints by height), its own pen bubbles keep rising (`ink62`); its own eyes open wide and dart.
  3. **Pull**: every joint (body, arms, strings, thread) is lifted by how high it stands under the face (`g`: 0 at the base, 1 at the face and above), so the head and face rise whole and the base stays a heap; the body thins toward the face (bone scales and positions drawn in), leans away from the molecule and sways as it teeters. The face's eyes and mouth are widened back on the thinned head. Its own face is alarmed, looking down (`al71`). The top it is pulled to is just inside the screen's top along the figure's own up (`S71` now measures `Hm71` along the root's up, `KIT.top84` = 1.05 of it); the figure is made smaller as it is pulled up (`scheme.js`: 0.2 / 0.18 / 0.3 / 0.34 of its size by `e.lift` for 1 to 2 .. 4 to 5), as taffy thins. **The swap** is at the middle of the pull (`e.swap`, half way through its beat, the thinnest moment): the old figure goes, the new one is already stretched the same way, so what teeters and falls is the new stage. The page's size goes from the old to the new through the stretch.
  4. **Slump** as before (`sq0`, the wobble, the ripple rings), plus a damp mark (`s66Mark`) where it lands. Drips are flung off its own tops (its face, its raised hands: `o.tops71`).
  5. **Each its own pull**: 1 to 2 the dollop pulled up whole by its top (thread and all) into a short teardrop; its new stub arm is drawn into the body while it is pulled and grows out as it plops (`e.sink`). 2 to 3 its ONE arm is hauled straight up first and alone (`e.up` in 0.18 of the pull), the body is lifted after it (`lift` lags 0.16) and hangs under it; the new heap's arm stays straight up after the body drops and arches over (`e.arch`). 3 to 4 the arched arm and the top together, both up at once; it stays risen (`sq0` 1.3) and flings both arms out. 4 to 5 both arms hauled up and splayed (0.42 rad) plus the crown (lifted to 0.78 of the top): three tops, one face, sagging down together. Strings are rebuilt every frame between the arms where they are now and where they land (`str62` called again), every other one snaps back into the body as it is pulled, the rest thin.
  6. Nothing switches on in a frame: the only swap is old to new figure at the thinnest of the stretch.
- **Switch**: `AA.rxKit.evo71` as before (on). `?v71=0` gives Water's evo on Slime's joints.
- **To render**: as the seventy-first (servers `srv.py`, `fsrv.py` in `.claude/r61`, ports 9991/9992), but with the new job `j_e84.js` (j_e71 with a longer debug line) and `e84.sh` (longer timeout); `./all84.sh <prefix> "1 2 3 4"` renders the six moments of each step at 1600 x 1000 and writes the square-crop strip `t71/<prefix>-strip.jpg` (`strip84.py`, crops from `s71dbg().scr`: the foot and the top on screen); `python strip84.py --pulls out.jpg <take>_1:3 <take>_2:3 <take>_3:3 <take>_4:3` the pulls sheet. Clips: same commands as the seventy-first.
- **Weak** (from the frames, see `slime84-evolve-strip.txt`): no stand-in is left, but each pull wears a skirt of heavy black pen streaks (the stretched strings and the skin's own strokes) and reads as a tall ghost drape more than a thin taffy neck: the skin has too few joints between the base and the face to pinch into a neck, so it stretches as a bell; the four pulls differ at the top (whole dollop, one arm, arm and top, two arms and crown) but share that bell; 2 to 3's arm stands up after landing as a thin stalk with its bead; the melt's face is small at 4 to 5; the drips are glossy balls (scene pieces) with an ink rim; the old `S71` pieces are kept in the code, unused; stills only, no clip, sound not heard.

## Eighty-sixth builder (15 November)

Branch `hydrogen-builder-75` (worktree `hyd75`; `steam` merged in first; the merged page started with no new script error through the kit's harness, job `j_h79.js` `part=ok`: `errs` empty, only the old `s30` error at `kitart.js` ~1753). Files: `kitart.js` (new block `W86` directly after `W79`'s end; one hook in `W75`'s `lay75`: while `S.x86f` is set the shadow and pool of light lie under the acting figure's pinch, raised onto the page over the page), `scheme.js` (new block `S86` directly after `S77`'s end). No other creature's block changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `hydrogen86-act-strip.jpg` (stage 3, twelve square crops of one act holding home and the molecule, two rows of six), `hydrogen86-act-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.act86` (`?a86=0` or `false` gives the old act: Water's clip on Hydrogen's body and the old `A20ACT.h2` threads). Only the bench Hydrogen of `W75`/`W79` (`S.way75`, not the jar, idle `idle79` on) uses it; every other figure, clip and creature passes straight on.
- **What the rule does and when it lands** (live code, not the older "chain floats over" rule in HOMUNCULI's early tables): `RULE.homSimple.h2 = 'a'`: lit once the page has driven off `HOMSIMPLE.h2.need` = 2 hydrogens (`g.tally.off_hydrogen - g.hom.h0`; oxidize drives off one per OH it turns to C=O); the tap multiplies the yield by the stage's number (`hom2Use`: `g.E *= HOMSIMPLE.h2.k`), once a round. No atom changes, nothing is carried over the page turn, no bet: so there is no spark and no fizzle. `S86` touches the places the counted hydrogens left from (`A20.gone`, kind `h2`, this page, in the order they left, each mapped to the nearest heavy atom), one beat apart (stage 1 one and a half). The hit is the act's own `j.hit` (`hitU = hit / D`), two and a half beats after the last touch, on a beat (`jumpPlan20`), where the page lets the yield through; `R21T.h2` is set per act from the plan. Nothing in `path.js` changed.
- **The act** (`W86` body, `S86` places and scene; `KIT.ph86` is the one timeline both read; `o.bites.s86` carries the plan with places in the world). The holder stays at home (its own soft shadow hidden while away); the whole figure is moved by one transform of `mid75` about its pinch (place, size `kz`, squash, turns), its idle (arms, lick, tail, companions) running under it; blended in from the idle in the first 0.3 beat and back to it at the end. Stage 3, beat 500 ms: squash 0.5 s, flight 1.75 s, brake 0.5 s, hover 0.5 s, touches 1.0 and 1.5 s after it arrives, hit 2.75 s after it arrives (about 6 s after the tap), settled at home about 9.5 s after the tap.
  1. Squash: down and wide like a spring, quivering, eyes wide, grinning, then up.
  2. Flight: an arc from home to past its place with a loop-the-loop over the bench (it turns once round with the loop) and a corkscrew after it, growing toward the eye (`kh`, stage 3 about 2.2 times home size), a streak behind it (a ribbon turned to the eye, white core, lilac edges, half a second), its shadow racing along bench then page (it steps up at the book's edge by the page's own floor).
  3. Brake: overshoots, arms out, leaning back, bobs back to its place beside the molecule on the side toward home (it may hang over the gutter: it floats), head about one and a half to two atoms across.
  4. Hover: one beat, rubbing its hands, lids half down, cheeky.
  5. Weave: shrunk to about an atom's height, it darts on a Catmull-Rom line in front of each counted atom (patting it: a little pale pop, `clink` and `tick` rising) and behind the bond between, out behind the last; its glow lights every atom near it (additive sprites); patted atoms stay lit a while.
  6. Up and hit: it rises, flings its arms up, pulls its lobes apart; on the hit it claps (lobes squashed together, arms in): a burst of light with a lilac halo, a ring of light runs out across the page in the paper's plane (a shader plane on the page), a pale flash under it gone in a fifth of a second, all atoms flash; it tumbles backward once round, away from the molecule, delighted; companions pop half a beat apart after it.
  7. Home: turns its back, flies home on a rising arc seen from behind, wobbling, shrinking to home size; turns round over its place, ducks down and bobs to rest.
  - Per stage: 1 hops three low bounds to its place and back, squeezes thin (stretched, effort face) through the gaps, a small ring, and the pop knocks it over (on its side a beat, then it rights itself); 3 the tuned reference; 5 slower (x1.15), a bigger loop and corkscrew, the companions trail on their threads (each swung to trail its motion, a little late) and pop one after another.
- **Sound** (existing `sfx` only, on half beats): `tick` as it winds up, `drift` and `swish` at take-off, `bond` at the loop, `tick` per hop (stage 1), `swish` and `tick` braking, `clink`/`tick` rising at each pat, `draw` through the rise, at the hit `snap`, a high `clink` and `drift` (the page's own `good` too), `tick`/`clink` per companion pop, `swish` turning, `drift` going home, `bond` as it settles. The old trip's `drift` and `stamp` are muted for this act (`j.f.up`, `j.f.land`).
- **Change of stage with the new figure**: not touched and not looked at; still Water's `evo` morphing from Water's skin of the old stage (builder 79's note). Listed, not fixed.
- **Stills** (bash, `.claude/r75`; ports 10391/10392; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID, `my86.pids`). Job `j_a86.js` (from `j_a77.js`): a real page `CC(O)CCCC(O)C`, line oxidize (both OH turn to C=O, two hydrogens off, Hydrogen lights; a weld and a ring follow); `AA.rxKit.s86dbg()` gives the plan, the phase, the ring. Strip: `NAME=st3 WSIZE=1920,1080 ./run.sh a86 "&stage=3&zw=0&smi=CC(O)CCCC(O)C&lines=oxidize&rack=pcc,hydrate,dehydrate&at=300,1010,1550,2400,3000,3750,4050,5600,6100,7100,8100,9700" 880` then `python strip72.py frames/st3 out.jpg 6 600 "300,20,640"`. Stages 1 and 5 were looked at with `at=1200,2900,4200,6100,6500,8800`.
- **Clip for the helper** (one at a time, servers running; `t.sh`, `sheet.py`, `j_a21.js`, `make4.py` copied from builder 77's kit; not yet tried for this act):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/hyd75/.claude/r75 && JOBN=a21 TMO=900 ./t.sh hydrogen86_act "&hom=h2&part=act&stage=3&smi=CC(O)CCCC(O)C&lines=oxidize&rack=pcc,hydrate,dehydrate&hold=14000" 24 && python make4.py hydrogen86-act.mp4 hydrogen86_act`
  - stage 5: the same with `hydrogen86_act5`, `&stage=5`, `hold=15000`, `hydrogen86-act-5.mp4`; stage 1: `hydrogen86_act1`, `&stage=1`, `hold=16000`, `hydrogen86-act-1.mp4`.
- **Weak** (from the frames; see `hydrogen86-act-strip.txt`): in the weave it is only about an atom high, so the pats and their pops are small on a phone; the streak is faint on white paper; with this molecule it hovers over the gutter; the back view going home is small; stage 5's long arms read as dark sticks while braking; its shadow on the paper is faint; the plan's own estimate of the figure's screen height (`hS`) is about a third of what is drawn, so the size is set from the atoms' radius instead; stages 2 and 4 not looked at; sound not heard; no timing on a real graphics card (up to 40 glow sprites, 22 pop sprites, one 64-point ribbon, one page plane).

## Eighty-second builder (15 November)

Branch `brimstone-builder-69` (worktree `brim69`; `steam` merged in first; the page started with no new script error through the kit's harness, `part=ok`, with `v82` on and off). Files: `kitart.js` (W77: the thread fix; new block `W82` directly after `W77`'s end), `scheme.js` (new block `S82` directly after `S77`'s end; it calls S59's `s59Place`, S74's `s74Wide`/`s74Box`/`s74Tex` and S77's `s77Tex`/`s77PageMat` without changing them). No other creature's block changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `brimstone82-thread.jpg` (the act's thread, stage 3, before and after, square crops: over the molecule, going out, coming back), `brimstone82-evolve-strip.jpg` (four rows, 1 to 2 ... 4 to 5; six moments each in the full game view), `brimstone82-evolve-strip.txt`.

- **The thread (owner's fix, commit `b9ce9c6`, "W77: the thread is a wisp of smoke, not a rod").** While the smoke is away from its lump, the skin's triangles that join the lump to the smoke (stage 1: the whole wisp) are not drawn: `a77` is a per-vertex smoke weight on the geometry, the wash and margin drop any triangle whose weight is mixed (uniform `u82`), and the pen line leaves out the corners of those triangles (`brk69` wrapped). The thread is drawn instead by `lay82`: up to 96 soft puffs of Brimstone's own smoke (`tex73`, broken pen edge) laid along the way it went (S77's `way0`/`wayB`, on the bench and over the book's edge) or along the wisp's bones at stage 1; it wavers across the way, drifts up, thins to nothing in stretches that travel along it, and small puffs lift off it. `KIT.thread82 = false` gives the old tube. Checked at stages 1, 3, 5 going out, over the molecule and coming back. The reaching arm (the long hand onto each atom) is unchanged: still a thin limb with a pen line.
- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.evo82` (`?v82=0` or `false` gives the old change of stage: Water's `evo` on the smoke's joints). Only the bench Brimstone (`S.way69`, not the jar, idle `idle73` on) uses it; every other figure and clip passes straight on.
- **What exists: "The eruption".** `play26` is wrapped for Brimstone's `evo` clip; Water's `evo` is not run. Timeline in beats of the music (`PAR82`, `KIT.ph82`, written into `R21.evo.e82`): go, down, swell (its last part held), erupt, form, home. 1 to 2 is 10.5 beats, 2 to 3 12, 3 to 4 13.75, 4 to 5 17.25 (5.3 to 8.6 s at pace 2). The hit falls on a whole beat (5, 6, 7, 9).
  1. **Go**: the OLD stage's figure (built once by `brim69Fig`, beside the new one, playing its own idle) lifts its lump a little in a cradle of its own smoke puffs (S82), drifts from home over the open book to the place (S59's `s59Place`, then `s74Wide`/`s74Box` for the new form: between the screen's left edge and the molecule, inside the screen), growing on the way (old form 0.82 of its size there, the ember 0.6), and sets the lump down (`stamp`); the paper under it scorches in a ring.
  2. **The drawing-down** (`post82o`): it bows lower and lower; every smoke bone is drawn down to the foot of the smoke in a slow spiral (lower smoke thinning first, the head last; the coal eyes go out last, the face hidden at the end); the puffs, wisps, mouth puff, haze and spit embers go with it; the flames gutter out. The room goes darker than in any other change: builder 33's one-lamp pass (`s82Lamp`, its own copy, lamp drawn in tighter, full strength) and a dark veil over bench, book and molecule (`K.dk`, a screen plane drawn before the creature and its fire, clearer round the lump). The scorch creeps out through the paper (`s82ScTex`: a ragged stain, charred rim, browned paper round it; kept short of the molecule except in 4 to 5, where it runs across both pages).
  3. **The swelling**: the lump swells (`PAR82.sw`, 0.14 to 0.28) and shudders in pulses on the beat, the cracks widening (`u69.y`); a red glow over the lump with a pale red-white heart (additive, no yellow), its red light on the page; molten beads ooze from the cracks, run down and cool on the page leaving flecks; thin blue flame tongues start along the cracks; embers spit up on each pulse. Held: still, blazing, silent.
  4. **The eruption**: on the hit (`boom`, `strike(2)`, `swish`) the old figure is removed and the new one put in its place with its smoke shut inside its lump and its lump the size of the swollen old one (`lw82`), under the flash and the fire. A pillar of blue fire made of Brimstone's own flame tongues (`fire73`: three behind the lump, six stacked up the column, three licks at the base; two blue glows) roars up out of the lump to 0.5 / 0.68 / 0.8 / 0.93 of the screen's height; dark puffs of its own smoke boil round its foot. The new stage's own smoke (its skin, pen line, wash, coal eyes) boils up out of the lump through the fire, swirling, lower parts first, head last (`post82n`); its eyes open. Embers rain from the fire (14 / 22 / 32 / 52) and wink out on the page, each leaving a scorch fleck that fades; a ring of blue fire runs out across the paper (S77's page shader, smaller).
  5. **The forming**, each step its own: 1 to 2 the smoke pops up as one puff with an overshoot and settles on the lump like a hood, then scowls aside at its first flame; 2 to 3 a straight tall column that then bends over and lets its head hang; 3 to 4 shoulders spread broad with a springy overshoot, the arms unfold, the raised hand last; 4 to 5 the longest swelling (4 beats), the scorch across both pages, the tallest pillar, the mantle spreading slow and wide, embers everywhere. The pillar sinks into the new stage's own flames round the lump.
  6. **Home**: the scorch fades (gone by about half the way home; page clean in the last frame of each row), it lifts its lump again in its smoke, turns its back, drifts home seen from behind, sets the lump down (`snap`), turns to face front; the new number is written beside it (`HEVO.num`, as S74).
- **No yellow**: nothing W82/S82 draws is yellow (blue-violet fire, red-orange to pale red-white heat and embers, umber scorch, grey smoke). The old jar colour 0xc9a227 (`JARC.brimstone`) is not used by this evolution; it was used only by the game's old `burst3` puffs in the two non-rigged evolution paths. `S82` wraps `burst3` so that for Brimstone a burst asked in #c9a227 is drawn half blue-violet (#6a5cff), half red-orange (#e8401a). `JARC` itself (and the jar floor) is unchanged. The S atoms keep the game's own sulfur colour.
- **Sound** (existing `sfx` calls only, on the beats): `swish` + low `drone` as it goes, `stamp` as it sets the lump down, `drone` + `swish` as it is drawn down, a higher `drone` through the swelling and a short `drone` on each pulse, nothing in the held instant, `boom` + `strike(2, 1.4)` + `swish` on the hit, `draw` as the new smoke rises (the base plays `stamp`, `feed`, `good`), `swish` turning, `drone` going home, `snap` as it settles.
- **To render** (bash, `.claude/r69` of this worktree, untracked; ports 10291/10292; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID, `my82.pids`). Stills: `./e82.sh <name> <stage it leaves> "<beats,...>" "<ms after home>" "&seed=N"` (job `j_e82.js`, from builder 74's `j_e74.js`; sheet `t82/<name>.jpg`; `AA.rxKit.s82dbg()` gives the beat `u`, the place, the fit, the lump, the pillar's height); the strip: `./all82.sh <prefix>`, which writes `t82/<prefix>-strip.jpg` (`strip82.py`). Thread stills: `NAME=th3a WSIZE=2560,1440 ./run.sh a77 "&stage=3&zw=0&smi=CSCC(CSC)CC=C&lines=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&rack=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&at=3800,7850,14200" 880` (stage 1: `&stage=1`, `at=3000,6500,9500`; stage 5: `&stage=5`, `at=4300,9800,17000`), then `python thread82.py` (reads `keep/th3b` and `keep/th3a2`).
- **Clips for the helper** (one at a time, servers running; each take a few minutes; `t.sh` with builder 21's job `j_a21.js`, `make4.py` builder 55's, both in `.claude/r69`):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/brim69/.claude/r69 && for s in 1 2 3; do JOBN=a21 TMO=900 ./t.sh ev82_$s "&hom=brimstone&part=evo&stage=$s&lead=1000&hold=11000" 24; done && JOBN=a21 TMO=1000 ./t.sh ev82_4 "&hom=brimstone&part=evo&stage=4&lead=1000&hold=13500" 24`
  - each step alone: `python make4.py brimstone82-evolve-<s>.mp4 ev82_<s>`
  - all four back to back with number cards and sound: `python make4.py brimstone82-evolve-all.mp4 ev82_1="1 to 2" ev82_2="2 to 3" ev82_3="3 to 4" ev82_4="4 to 5"`
  - the act with the new thread (stage 3): `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/brim69/.claude/r69 && JOBN=a21 TMO=900 ./t.sh brimstone82_act "&hom=brimstone&part=act&stage=3&smi=CSCC(CSC)CC=C&lines=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&rack=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&hold=21000" 24 && python make4.py brimstone82-act.mp4 brimstone82_act`
- **Weak**: see `brimstone82-evolve-strip.txt`. Also: the new smoke reads pale, nearly white, under the lamp pass (the smoke's wash takes the lit scene behind it); the pillar's stacked tongues are additive and read white-hot more than blue where they overlap; the scorch is one drawn stain scaled up (the same shape every time); the veil darkens the molecule and the cards as much as the bench; the lump is very large at the place in 1 to 2; the arms' unfolding (3 to 4) is a straight pull from the shoulder; looked at in stills only, sound not heard, the switch off checked only for a clean start and no error, no timing on a real graphics card (up to 12 fire sprites, 64 embers, 48 puffs, 64 flecks, 2 page planes and one veil plane, plus the old figure while it lives).

## Eighty-seventh builder (15 November)

Branch `slime-builder-61` (worktree `slime61`; `steam` merged in first). Files: `kitart.js` (block `W71` only), `scheme.js` (block `S71` only). Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `slime87-pulls.jpg` (the four pulls at their tallest, now, over the same moment before from `slime84-pulls.jpg`), `slime87-pulls.txt`.

- **The fault**: at the pull the stretched Slime was covered in heavy black vertical pen streaks (its own pen marks, its strings and the outline's inner creases, all stretched with the skin) and read as a scribbled-over draped ghost.
- **What changed**:
  1. **One clean outer line** while `S.cl71` (from the pull until `e.sl` 0.62 after landing; set in `post71`, cleared in `end71`). A `draw30` wrapper in `W71` lends `S.a61 = null` (no own pen marks) and empty `K.lanes` (no bubble rings) for the call; a `stroke30` wrapper drops every pen run that lies on the body's own shape; then `line71` traces the outer edge of the body's skin laid flat on the screen (`mask71`: a coarse cover from all its triangles; `edge71`: the largest piece followed round, smoothed) and writes it as three long overlapping strokes in the figure's own pen weight. Beads off the body are still drawn.
  2. **Strings drawn in**: for the pull every string and drip of the arms is shrunk to nothing where it hangs (scale to 0, hardly moved, so the body's skin is not dragged into a sail); they re-form between `e.sl` 0.3 and 0.6 after landing. Drip beads and spots hidden meanwhile.
  3. **Neck**: the thinning along the pull is narrowest just under the face (`nk` peaks at g about 0.65 instead of 0.5).
  4. **Arm**: a hauled arm tapers from root to a slightly smaller rounded tip (scale 1 to about 0.4 along it, tip included) instead of a stick with a full-size bead.
  5. **Drips** (`S71`): flat violet wash (`K.dw`, `#c9abe8`, 0.88) with a thin dark violet rim (`K.dr`, 1.07), and smaller (0.018 to 0.036 R).
  6. Nothing else: timing, melt, gathering, slump, sounds, switch as the eighty-fourth.
- **To render**: as the eighty-fourth; new `p87.sh <prefix>` renders only the pull at its tallest and just landed for each step (`frames/<prefix>_1..4`), then `python strip84.py --pulls out.jpg <p>_1:0 <p>_2:0 <p>_3:0 <p>_4:0`; `python make87.py <after> <before> <out>` the now-over-before sheet.
- **Weak** (from `slime87-pulls.jpg`): no streaks or fringe left, but the traced outline follows the true outer shape, so at 2 to 3 and 3 to 4 it runs along thin stretched webs of skin from the raised arm to the base (two or three long lines), and it loops round the slick's bare-paper rim below the visible wash; the body still stretches as a bell rather than a pinched taffy neck (too few joints); the wash is not paler where thinnest; stills only, no clip, sound not heard.
