## Eighty-eighth builder (15 November)

Branch `ammonia-builder-78` (worktree `amm78`; `steam` merged in first, a fast-forward; the merged page started with no script error through the kit's harness, job `j_ok78.js`: `errs` empty). Files: `kitart.js` (new block `W88` directly after `W81`'s end; `W78`/`W81` untouched), `scheme.js` (new block `S88` directly after `S86`'s end). No other creature's block changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `ammonia88-act-strip.jpg` (stage 3, twelve square crops of one act holding home, the line-up and the molecule, two rows of six), `ammonia88-act-strip.txt`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.act88` (`?a88=0` or `false` gives the old act: Water's clip on Ammonia's body, the old squash-and-spin on the holder, the old ink). Only the bench Ammonia of `W78`/`W81` (`S.way78`, not the jar, idle `idle81` on) uses it; every other figure, clip and creature passes straight on. The act ran with no error at stages 1, 3 and 5 (`W88.err`, `S88.err` empty); stages 2 and 4 not run.
- **What the rule does and when it lands** (live code: `HOM2LIT.ammonia`, `hom2Use`; HOMUNCULI's "the page's three reagents fire again" is right in substance): lit when aminate (`ritter`) acted on this page or a knit is in its chain; the tap puts the page's line (the reagents that acted, in order: `pageLine`) back in the queue with a use given back each (`give`, `more`: they show at once as further circles of the line-up), multiplies the yield by the stage's number (`homGrow`), once a round. The yield is let through at the act's own `j.hit` (`hitU = hit / D`, on a beat by `jumpPlan20`); the page then fires the queue by its own flow (`fireNext` after `j.rest`), each reagent from its circle. So `S88` pricks those circles (`s88Marks`: `STEP.x + (pageSteps + i) * STEPD`, in the queue's order), one lunge each on half beats (stage 3: gaps of 1.5 beats, then 1), and the flourish is the hit. `R21T.ammonia` is set per act from the plan. Nothing in `path.js` changed.
- **The act** (`W88` body, `S88` places and scene; `KIT.ph88` is the one timeline both read; `o.bites.s88` carries the plan). The holder stays at home (its own soft shadow hidden while away; `homJump` wrapped so the old squash-and-spin never touches it); the whole figure is moved by one transform of `mid78` about its foot (place, size, a turn about the upright, a mirror so it can face either way, changed only while it is edge on in a pivot, a tip along its way and a stretch-and-thin while it darts), the idle (ribbons, tear, wisp) running under it; the act sets lean, neck and face over `rest81`, and the needle arm (the arm ending furthest forward) is laid out straight by `arm88`. Stage 3 on the test page (beat 500 ms): attention 0.5 s, dart 0.25 s, edge 0.75 s, dart 0.25 s; salute 0.75 s; touches 1.0 / 1.75 / 2.25 s after it plants; hit 3.0 s after it plants (about 5.3 s after the tap); home 1.75 s after the hit's beat ends; settled about 7.5 s after the tap.
  1. Attention: drawn up, chin up, needle down at its side; the foot lets go with a crack of frost (a frost patch stays at home while it is away).
  2. Dart: one dead-straight dart over the bench to just short of the book's edge, a little to one side (zig), thinned to a streak, frost blooms laid behind its foot in the wood; stops dead with a click.
  3. Edge: upright, it looks the page over down its nose (head one way, then the other), pivots to face its marks.
  4. Dart onto the page (zag), growing toward the eye (stage 3 about twice home size, about as tall on the screen as the molecule); plants with a burst of frost.
  5. Salute: needle straight up, then en garde on the first mark.
  6. Lunges: for each mark, in order, the body tips forward about its foot until the arm, stretched to 1.6 of its length, puts the needle point exactly on the circle (a white frost spark, a flash ring of cold round the circle, a frost mark on it for a moment, `clink` rising); it recovers as fast with a toss of the head; between marks it advances one straight step with a frost bloom; each lunge 15% crisper.
  7. Flourish: on the hit the needle sweeps up and is held high, chin up, glints at the point.
  8. Home: one sharp pivot to its back, the same two straight darts seen from behind, plants in its frost with a snap, turns to the front and blends back into its idle.
  - Per stage: 1 (no arms) goes in three little stabbing hops each way and pricks with its pointed head (the whole lick tips down to the mark); 3 the tuned reference; 5 one long dart each way, stands once level with the row and reaches all three marks with a long level thrust (barely a slide).
- **Sound** (existing `sfx` only, on half beats): `tick` at attention, `snap` + `tick` + `swish` leaving, `tick`/`snap` at the edge, `swish` per dart, `snap` + `tick` planting, at each touch `clink(8 + 3i)` + `tick`, `tick` per advance, `draw` through the flourish, at the hit `clink(16)` + `snap` (the page's own `good` too), `tick`/`swish` turning, `snap` + `tick` at home. The old trip's `drift` and `stamp` are muted (`j.f.up`, `j.f.land`).
- **Change of stage with the new figure**: not touched and not looked at; still Water's `evo` morphing from Water's skin of the old stage (builder 81's note). Listed, not fixed.
- **Stills** (bash, `.claude/r78`; ports 10491/10492; start `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` there, end them by PID, `my88.pids`). Sources `block88.js`, `s88.js`, put in by `python apply88.py`. Job `j_a88.js` (from `j_a86.js`); `AA.rxKit.s88dbg()` gives the plan. Strip: `NAME=fin3 WSIZE=1920,1080 ./run.sh a88 "&stage=3&zw=0&smi=C=CCCC(O)CCC(O)C&lines=ritter,dehydrate,hydrate&rack=ritter,dehydrate,hydrate&at=250,600,1100,1610,2150,2780,3150,3530,4800,5350,5620,6900" 980` then `python strip72.py frames/fin3 out.jpg 6 600 "190,0,720"`; quick sheets `python look88.py frames/NAME out.jpg 180,0,720 6 330`.
- **Clip for the helper** (one at a time, servers running; `t.sh`, `sheet.py`, `j_a21.js` copied from builder 86's kit, `make4.py` already here; not yet tried for this act):
  - `cd /c/Work/atlas-atoms/.claude/worktrees/amm78/.claude/r78 && JOBN=a21 TMO=900 ./t.sh ammonia88_act "&hom=ammonia&part=act&stage=3&smi=C=CCCC(O)CCC(O)C&lines=ritter,dehydrate,hydrate&rack=ritter,dehydrate,hydrate&hold=12000" 24 && python make4.py ammonia88-act.mp4 ammonia88_act`
  - stage 5: the same with `ammonia88_act5`, `&stage=5`, `ammonia88-act-5.mp4`; stage 1: `ammonia88_act1`, `&stage=1`, `hold=14000`, `ammonia88-act-1.mp4`.
- **Weak** (from the frames; see `ammonia88-act-strip.txt`): from this steep camera its round head hides the body, so at stage 3 the salute and lunges read as a hunched reach down onto the circles more than a long fencer's thrust (stage 5, standing level with the row, reads far better); it stands over circles 1 to 3 and just under the round counter, which is drawn over it (the plan keeps its body off a box guessed from the first circle's place); the pivot frame looks as if it lies down; frost blooms and sparks are small on a phone; the reagents fire again only after it has gone (the page's flow), so the prick and the refire are apart; the ribbons keep their idle wind (they do not stream with the darts); stage 1 is a big round ball; stages 2 and 4 not run; sound not heard; no timing on a real graphics card (48 marks, 16 sprites, one shade).

## Ninety-first builder (15 November, halted by the owner; no code changed)

- **The lingering dark band** is not the thread (`lay82` in `W77` hides once `e.hb` is 0 in `settle`/`done`). It is S77's bank in `s77Frame` (`scheme.js`): while `moving` (phase `pour` and `back`) it lays dark puffs (`0x463e3b`, opacity 0.5, life 1.9 s) every 70 ms and scorch marks (`s77Mark`, every 0.9 units, life 4.8 s on paper / 7.5 s on the bench, alpha 0.55 / 0.9) along the way, so the way back leaves a dark line from home to the molecule for seconds after `tHm`. Fix to make: no marks on `back`, pale short-lived broken puffs that end before `tHm`, and clamp the way-out marks to be gone by then.

## Ninety-second builder (15 November)

Branch `brimstone-builder-69` (worktree `brim69`; `steam` merged in first). The owner threw out the acts and changes of stage made so far ("messy drawing, stiff or ugly motion, too much going on, not like Water's") and asked for ONE creature done right. Files: `kitart.js` (new block `W92` directly after `W82`'s end; one line of `W69`'s wash: `u69.z` makes the smoke opaque), `scheme.js` (inside `S77`: `s92Plan`, `s92Frame` and three short branches marked `v92`; new block `S92` directly after `S82`'s end). No other creature's block changed. Shots in `C:\Work\atlas-atoms\.claude\shots`: `brimstone92-act.mp4`, `small\brimstone92-act-phone.mp4`, `brimstone92-act-sheet.jpg`, `brimstone92-evolve-all.mp4`, `small\brimstone92-evolve-phone.mp4`, `brimstone92-evolve-sheet.jpg`.

- **Switch ON by default.** `AA.rxKit.v92` (`?v92=0` or `false` gives builder 77's act and builder 82's eruption, untouched). Only the bench Brimstone (`S.way69`, not the jar, idle `idle73` on).
- **Why Water's change of stage works** (from a dense sheet of `small\evolve36-all.mp4`; for the next creature's builder):
  1. One idea, four shapes: figure, ball, the ball opened, the new figure. About five seconds from leaving home to standing.
  2. The middle is one big simple shape the rig draws perfectly (a sphere): one closed outline, so no stretched skin and no torn line; the swap hides inside it.
  3. Huge: the ball is about half the screen's height, the new figure a little more, in the middle of the open book.
  4. Weight: it speeds up into the roll, the swollen ball is held about a second with a small wobble, the burst is two frames, the figure overshoots and settles; the way home is quick.
  5. The page stays light: a soft shade, pale wet marks that dry, the creature's own colour. Nothing dark, nothing left behind.
- **The rule this builder held to: the creature is never taken apart.** The whole figure (lump, flames, smoke, face) is carried by the page's own holder (`homJump`'s place, height, size, squash and stretch, lean, turn; in the change of stage `HEVO.pos`), so every frame is the rest pose's figure with its one outline. The kit only adds overlap (the smoke column sheared by height: it trails in the first half of an arc and swings past on the landing), the face, and the size of its fire. `hop92` is one hop: push off, arc, landing squash that is the next hop's crouch.
- **The act** (`KIT.ph92` is the one timeline; `o.bites.s77` with `v92` carries the plan; the rule's contract is builder 77's: the atoms `s77Atoms` counts, `R21T.brimstone` from the plan, the yield at the act's own `j.hit`, on a beat). Stage 3, beat 500 ms: about 9.5 s from the tap to settled.
  1. Ready (1 beat): down and wide, coals open.
  2. Out: two or three hops (a beat each; 1.5 at stage 5), growing; landings are moved beside the straight way if an atom lies on it.
  3. It stands ON THE MOLECULE'S OWN PAGE, whole figure inside the page and above the score, clear of every atom (`s92Plan` searches the page for the largest free place up to `S92H` of the page's height, nearest the molecule).
  4. The spit, one sulfur a beat: leans back and swells, snaps forward, a tongue of its own blue fire flies a clear arc from its mouth to the atom (0.6 beat) and stands there with a small pop; a recoil.
  5. The hit: crouches, draws itself up tall on the beat; the fires on the atoms flare once and are out 1.5 s later.
  6. Home: a dip, its back to the eye (the swap is in the squash: no edge-on frames), the same hops from behind, round to the front in the last landing's squash, a spring, settled. Spent: the same whole figure a little sunk, low fire, dim coals, lids half down (`SP92`).
- **The change of stage, "the one flame"** (`KIT.ph92e`, `PAR92`, written into `R21.evo.e92`; place by `s59Place`/`s74Wide`/`s74Box` as before). Beats of go, wrap, hold, reveal, show, home: 9.5, 11, 12, 14 for the four steps; the hit on a whole beat (4, 5, 6, 7).
  1. Go: the OLD stage hops to the place in two hops, growing.
  2. Wrap: its own blue fire rises round it as ONE great tongue of flame (`S92`: a sprite drawn once at 512 x 1024: violet edge, blue body, pale heart, two low side tongues, one ink outline); the old figure crouches and draws in under it and is hidden behind it.
  3. Hold: the flame alone, breathing and swaying a little, nothing else in the picture; at the end it gathers itself (down and wide).
  4. Reveal, on the beat: the flame is drawn up thin and gone; the NEW stage springs up out of a squash where it burned, past its height and back.
  5. Show: held to be read (1.5 to 2.5 beats).
  6. Home: dip, back, two hops, front, settled; the new number is written beside it.
  - The four share the idea and grow: the flame reaches 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.72 of the screen above its foot and is held longer each time.
- **Cut** (all still there with `?v92=0`): the thread and the smoke leaving its lump, the reaching arm, the bank of smoke, scorch marks, the ring of fire, embers on the atoms, the dimmed room in both, the dark veil, the scorch stain, the red glow and molten beads, the pillar of stacked tongues, the rain of embers and flecks, the cradle of puffs, the smoke spiral.
- **Other fixes.** The smoke's wash is opaque while it acts or changes (`u69.z`: the book's edge no longer shows through its head). Its own pen marks are left out while its back is to the eye (`draw30` wrapped). Its scorch and light planes lie on the paper while it is on the book (`lay69` wrapped, `S.ly92`). The figure no longer jumps back to the molecule for a few frames at the act's end.
- **Takes** (bash, `.claude/r69`, ports 10291/10292; `python srv.py` and `python fsrv.py` first, end them by PID, `my92.pids`; sources `w92.js`, `s92a.js`, `s92.js`, put in by `python apply92.py`; `./all92.sh` runs all five):
  - act: `WSIZE=1920,1080 JOBN=a21 TMO=1500 ./t.sh brimstone92_act "&hom=brimstone&part=act&stage=3&smi=CSCC(CSC)CC=C&lines=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&rack=thiolene,hydrate,dehydrate&hold=12500" 24 && python make4.py brimstone92-act.mp4 brimstone92_act`
  - changes: `for s in 1 2 3; do WSIZE=1920,1080 JOBN=a21 TMO=1200 ./t.sh ev92_$s "&hom=brimstone&part=evo&stage=$s&lead=1000&hold=7800" 24; done; WSIZE=1920,1080 JOBN=a21 TMO=1200 ./t.sh ev92_4 "&hom=brimstone&part=evo&stage=4&lead=1000&hold=9300" 24`, then `python make4.py brimstone92-evolve-all.mp4 ev92_1="1 to 2" ev92_2="2 to 3" ev92_3="3 to 4" ev92_4="4 to 5"`
  - `WSIZE` does not change what is kept: this kit's recorder writes 1280 x 720 frames whatever the window (builder 73's `t73.sh` captured larger); the phone clips are crops of 1280-wide takes scaled up, so they are soft. A take is about three minutes.
  - phone clips: act `-ss 3.4 -t 11.8`, `crop=640:640:500:0,scale=900:900`; changes `crop=720:720:120:0,scale=900:900`.
- **Still weak** (judged from dense frame sheets of the takes, not from stills): see the hand-back; in short: on its own page the figure at the molecule is small (about a quarter of the page's height on the test molecule); the great flame can read as a blue drop; a pale square of its own light shows faintly round the large new form; the front-to-back swap is one frame inside a squash; the lump's own flames are spiky when the figure is large; stages 1 and 5 of the act and steps 1 to 2 and 4 to 5 of the change were recorded but only step 2 to 3 and 3 to 4 and the stage 3 act were studied frame by frame; sound not heard; no timing on a real graphics card.

## Ninety-fourth builder (15 November)

Branch `brimstone-flame-94` (worktree `brimflame94`, fresh from `steam`). ONE narrow job: the great flame of Brimstone's change of stage, drawn alive. Only ADDED: block `W94` in `kitart.js` (directly after `W92 END`) and this section. No existing line changed; nothing calls it yet.

- **What it is.** `AA.rxKit.flame94 = { make(T), update(m, o), light(o) }`. `make(THREE)` gives one flat `Mesh` (one `ShaderMaterial`, no texture, no canvas) whose origin is the flame's FOOT; add it to the scene (not to a turned parent). It sets `renderOrder 32`, `depthTest/depthWrite false`, `userData.plain/nobox`, as the page's sprite has.
- **Inputs of `update(m, o)`.** `t` seconds (any running clock, e.g. `now / 1000`); `rise` 0..1 (grown up from the lump: height and some width); `h`, `w` world units (height to the tip at rise 1; the widest width; about `w = 0.62 h`); `breath` a phase in radians (slow swell of height and width); `draw` 0..1 (the foot pinches, the whole is stretched up to a thread, lifts and is gone; at 1 nothing is drawn); `face` the camera (the sheet copies its turn); optional `sway` (lean), `a` (opacity, 1), `smoke` (0..1, 1), `seed`. `light(o)` takes the same `o` (`t`, `rise`, `draw`, `breath`) and gives 0..1: the brightness for the light on the paper; the same three-sine flicker moves the flame's height, so light and flame agree.
- **The swap (two lines, both in `scheme.js` block `S92`, where the page's flat tongue lives; `W92` only makes the timeline).** (1) in `s92Make`, in place of the `K.fl = new T.Sprite(...)` line: `K.fl = AA.rxKit.flame94.make(T); scene.add(K.fl);` (2) in `s92Evo`, keep `m.position.set(...)` and put in place of `m.scale.set(...); m.material.rotation = ...; m.material.opacity = ...;`: `const o94 = { t: now / 1000, rise: Math.min(1, e.fl), h: E.fH92, w: E.fH92 * 0.62, breath: 2 * Math.PI * Math.max(0, u - c[2]), draw: u >= c[3] ? Math.min(1, (u - c[3]) / (0.55 * P.b[3])) : 0, sway: e.sway, face: cam }; AA.rxKit.flame94.update(m, o94);` and, for the light, multiply `K.gl.material.opacity` by `AA.rxKit.flame94.light(o94)` instead of the `Math.sin(now / 47)` term. (`e.fw` and `e.fa` are then not needed: `draw` does the thinning and the going.)
- **How it is drawn (all in the fragment shader, from continuous time: nothing steps).** One FIELD: a broad-footed great tongue (its middle line sways in waves that travel up) joined smoothly with four side tongues that each grow and sink on their own slow noise and lean out, less a noise that streams upward and is pushed sideways as it climbs; above the tip the field only just fails, so islands break off, rise, shrink and go. WASH: the field cut at three depths (deep blue-violet with a darker pooled rim; lighter blue; a pale heart low and left; red-orange at the root; no yellow), an edge whose softness varies along it, paper grain through the top third; the lower two thirds are 0.97 opaque. PEN: the same edge in dark blue-violet from the field's screen slope, a hair off the wash, heavy low right and light high left, broken by a second stream so the strokes travel with the tongues; a few strokes on the lighter blue's edge. SMOKE: three thin grey wisps off the tip.
- **Test.** `.claude/r94` (not in git; ports 10691/10692): `flame94.html` (the flame alone, three seconds a sheet) and job `j_f94.js` (the flame alone at the middle of the open book on the real page, half the screen high). Shots: `brimstone94-flame.jpg` (12 consecutive frames at 30 a second, then rise 0.2 0.5 0.8 1 and draw 0.3 0.7), `small\brimstone94-flame-phone.mp4` (6 s: rises, stands and breathes, drawn up).
- **Weak.** Software rendering only (no timing on a real graphics card; about 14 noise look-ups a pixel on one sheet). The pen is thin and partly lost on the upper left; shed flamelets are few and small; at some moments the standing shape is a plain dome with little tongue; the drawn-up thread is a plain ribbon that sheds almost nothing; never tried inside the real change of stage (the figure behind it, the page's own blue light), so the swap lines above are untested; the heart is a pale smear, not a drawn form.
